### [1] [What Makes a Bacterial Model a Good Reservoir Computer? Predicting Performance from Separability and Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19850)
*Laura Alonso Bartolomé,Jean-Loup Faulon,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该研究探索细菌代谢模型作为物理储层计算基质的潜力，通过动态通量平衡分析模拟微生物生长动力学，评估其在非线性分类任务中的计算性能，并分析动力学特性与计算性能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统因其通过复杂内部动力学处理环境信息的能力而成为有前景的计算基质。研究旨在探索细菌代谢模型能否作为物理储层，以及其计算性能是否可以通过与可分离性和相似性相关的动力学特性进行预测。

Method: 使用动态通量平衡分析（dFBA）模拟五种细菌、一种酵母和29个大肠杆菌单基因缺失突变体的生长动力学，以葡萄糖和木糖浓度为输入，生长曲线为储层状态。通过线性读出评估随机非线性分类任务的计算性能，并通过生长曲线状态矩阵计算核秩和泛化秩来表征与可分离性和相似性相关的储层特性。

Result: 多个微生物模型实现了高分类准确率，表明细菌代谢动力学可以支持非线性计算。不同物种间存在明显差异：一些模型收敛更快，另一些达到更高的最大准确率，揭示了收敛速度与峰值性能之间的权衡。所有大肠杆菌突变体均被野生型模型主导，表明基因缺失减少了有效计算所需的动力学丰富度。核秩与泛化秩的差异通常与准确率提高相关，但在低秩值下的模型间偏差和敏感性限制了其实际预测能力。

Conclusion: 细菌代谢模型构成了有前景的储层计算基质，为识别具有有利计算特性的微生物菌株提供了初步步骤，有助于未来的实验实施。

Abstract: Biological systems are promising substrates for computation because they naturally process environmental information through complex internal dynamics. In this study, we investigate whether bacterial metabolic models can act as physical reservoirs and whether their computational performance can be predicted from dynamical properties linked to separability and similarity. We simulated the growth dynamics of five bacterial species, one yeast species, and 29 Escherichia coli single-gene deletion mutants using dynamic flux balance analysis (dFBA), with glucose and xylose concentrations as inputs and growth curves as reservoir states. Computational performance was assessed on random nonlinear classification tasks using a linear readout, while reservoir properties linked to separability and similarity were characterised through kernel and generalisation ranks computed from growth-curve state matrices. Several microbial models achieved high classification accuracy, showing that bacterial metabolic dynamics can support nonlinear computation. Clear differences were observed between species, with some models converging more rapidly and others reaching higher maximum accuracy, revealing a trade-off between convergence speed and peak performance. In contrast, all E. coli mutants were dominated by the wild-type model, suggesting that gene deletions reduce the dynamical richness required for efficient computation. The difference between kernel and generalisation ranks was generally associated with improved accuracy, but deviations across models and sensitivity at low rank values limited its predictive power in practice. Overall, these results show that bacterial metabolic models constitute promising substrates for reservoir computing and provide a first step towards identifying microbial strains with favourable computational properties for future experimental implementations.

</details>

### [2] [SL(C)AMma: Simultaneous Localisation, (Calibration) and Mapping With a Magnetometer Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19946)
*Thomas Edridge,Manon Kok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种基于磁力计阵列的SLAM滤波算法：SLAMma用于定位与建图，SLCAMma同时估计校准参数，显著减少室内定位漂移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内定位面临GNSS信号衰减和本体感受传感器积分漂移问题，现有磁力计SLAM在未探索区域表现不佳，磁力计阵列虽能直接估计里程计但存在测量不一致性

Method: 提出两种滤波算法：SLAMma用于磁力计阵列的SLAM，SLCAMma扩展为同时估计磁力计校准参数，通过蒙特卡洛模拟和实验验证

Result: 在充分姿态激励下能准确估计校准参数，实现传感器间测量一致性；实验显示在单磁力计SLAM失败时，新方法能提供良好轨迹估计，漂移减少超80%

Conclusion: 磁力计阵列结合提出的滤波算法能有效解决室内定位漂移问题，显著提升定位精度和鲁棒性，特别是在未探索区域

Abstract: Indoor localisation techniques suffer from attenuated Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and from the accumulation of unbounded drift by integration of proprioceptive sensors. Magnetic field-based Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) reduces drift through loop closures by revisiting previously seen locations, but extended exploration of unseen areas remains challenging. Recently, magnetometer arrays have demonstrated significant benefits over single magnetometers, as they can directly estimate the odometry. However, inconsistencies between magnetometer measurements negatively affect odometry estimates and complicate loop closure detection. We propose two filtering algorithms: The first focuses on magnetic field-based SLAM using a magnetometer array (SLAMma). The second extends this to jointly estimate the magnetometer calibration parameters (SLCAMma). We demonstrate, using Monte Carlo simulations, that the calibration parameters can be accurately estimated when there is sufficient orientation excitation, and that magnetometers achieve inter-sensor measurement consistency regardless of the type of motion. Experimental validation on ten datasets confirms these results, and we demonstrate that in cases where single magnetometer SLAM fails, SLAMma and SLCAMma provide good trajectory estimates with, more than 80% drift reduction compared to integration of proprioceptive sensors.

</details>


### [3] [Radar Odometry Subject to High Tilt Dynamics of Subarctic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19962)
*Matěj Boxan,William Larivée-Hardy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对极端地形条件下的旋转FMCW雷达里程计问题，提出了一种新型雷达-惯性里程计方法，在动态地形中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转FMCW雷达里程计方法通常假设平坦地面条件，这在城市环境或平坦矿区足够，但在亚北极等极端动态地形中面临挑战，需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的雷达-惯性里程计方法，采用倾斜-邻近子图搜索技术，并对扫描点与旋转估计轴之间的垂直位移设置硬阈值，以应对极端地形变化。

Result: 在2公里长的动态轨迹上，该方法比第二好的比较方法提升了0.3%的性能；在城市基准测试中达到最先进水平；在具有高横向滑动和陡峭沟渠穿越的复杂雷达序列中表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的雷达-惯性里程计方法在极端动态地形条件下显著优于现有方法，为解决亚北极等恶劣环境中的雷达里程计问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Rotating FMCW radar odometry methods often assume flat ground conditions. While this assumption is sufficient in many scenarios, including urban environments or flat mining setups, the highly dynamic terrain of subarctic environments poses a challenge to standard feature extraction and state estimation techniques. This paper benchmarks three existing radar odometry methods under demanding conditions, exhibiting up to 13° in pitch and 4° in roll difference between consecutive scans, with absolute pitch and roll reaching 30° and 8°, respectively. Furthermore, we propose a novel radar-inertial odometry method utilizing tilt-proximity submap search and a hard threshold for vertical displacement between scan points and the estimated axis of rotation. Experimental results demonstrate a state-of-the-art performance of our method on an urban baseline and a 0.3% improvement over the second-best comparative method on a 2-kilometer-long dynamic trajectory. Finally, we analyze the performance of the four evaluated methods on a complex radar sequence characterized by high lateral slip and a steep ditch traversal.

</details>


### [4] [Efficient Reinforcement Learning using Linear Koopman Dynamics for Nonlinear Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19980)
*Wenjian Hao,Yuxuan Fang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的强化学习框架，通过Koopman算子学习线性提升动力学，结合演员-评论家架构优化参数化闭环控制器，使用单步预测而非多步传播估计策略梯度，提高样本效率并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非线性机器人系统的最优闭环控制问题，旨在提高强化学习在机器人控制中的样本效率，同时避免传统基于模型方法对精确系统动力学的依赖。

Method: 基于Koopman算子理论学习线性提升动力学模型，将其集成到演员-评论家架构中进行策略优化。采用单步预测而非多步传播来估计策略梯度，减少计算成本和模型传播误差，形成在线小批量策略梯度框架。

Result: 在多个模拟非线性控制基准和两个真实硬件平台（Kinova Gen3机械臂和Unitree Go1四足机器人）上验证。相比无模型强化学习基线，样本效率更高；相比基于模型的强化学习基线，控制性能更优；与依赖精确系统动力学的经典基于模型方法相比，控制性能相当。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功实现了非线性机器人系统的高效最优闭环控制，在样本效率、控制性能和计算成本之间取得了良好平衡，为实际机器人应用提供了有效的基于模型强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimal closed-loop control of nonlinear robotic systems. The proposed approach learns linear lifted dynamics through Koopman operator theory and integrates the resulting model into an actor-critic architecture for policy optimization, where the policy represents a parameterized closed-loop controller. To reduce computational cost and mitigate model rollout errors, policy gradients are estimated using one-step predictions of the learned dynamics rather than multi-step propagation. This leads to an online mini-batch policy gradient framework that enables policy improvement from streamed interaction data. The proposed framework is evaluated on several simulated nonlinear control benchmarks and two real-world hardware platforms, including a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm and a Unitree Go1 quadruped. Experimental results demonstrate improved sample efficiency over model-free RL baselines, superior control performance relative to model-based RL baselines, and control performance comparable to classical model-based methods that rely on exact system dynamics.

</details>


### [5] [Strain in Sound: Soft Corrugated Tube for Local Strain Sensing with Acoustic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20017)
*Michael Chun,Ananya Nukala,Tae Myung Huh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 软波纹管传感器通过气流激发驻波共振，利用频率变化估计局部应变，机器学习算法实现高精度测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够精确估计软体非均匀变形和局部应变的传感器，用于机器人、可穿戴设备等应用

Method: 设计软波纹管传感器，气流通过时激发驻波共振产生声调；拉伸改变共振频率和频率-流速关系；通过扫频收集数据，使用梯度提升回归器进行机器学习分析

Result: 双周期管（3.1mm和4.18mm）平均绝对误差0.8mm，单周期管（3.1mm）平均绝对误差1mm；在人体模型手指上成功区分多关节配置

Conclusion: 软波纹管传感器结合声学共振和机器学习，能够有效估计软体非均匀变形和局部应变，在柔性机器人、可穿戴传感等领域具有应用潜力

Abstract: We present a soft corrugated tube sensor designed to estimate strain in each half segment. When air flows through the tube, the internal corrugated cavities induce pressure oscillations that excite the tube's standing wave resonance mode, generating an acoustic tone. Stretching the tube affects both the resonance mode frequency, due to changes in overall length, and the frequency-flow speed relationship, due to variations in cavity width, which is particularly useful for local strain estimation. By sweeping flow rates in a controlled manner, we collected resonance frequency data across flow speeds under various local stretch conditions, enabling a machine learning algorithm (gradient boosting regressor) to estimate segmental strain with high accuracy. The dual-period tube design (3.1 mm and 4.18 mm corrugation periods) achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.8 mm, while the single-period tube (3.1 mm) provided a satisfactory MAE of 1 mm. Testing on a mannequin finger demonstrated the sensor's capability to differentiate multi-joint configurations, showing its potential for estimating non-uniform deformations in soft bodies.

</details>


### [6] [Toward Safe Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Interventions using World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20151)
*Harry Robertshaw,Nikola Fischer,Han-Ru Wu,Andrea Walker Perez,Weiyuan Deng,Benjamin Jackson,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于TD-MPC2世界模型的自主机械取栓导航框架在模拟和体外实验中表现优于传统SAC算法，首次实现了在保留患者血管数据和荧光引导体外实验中的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机械取栓面临血管几何结构高度可变和实时精确控制的挑战。现有强化学习方法在面对多样化患者解剖结构或长导航距离时鲁棒性有限，需要更通用的AI辅助血管内介入方案。

Method: 采用基于世界模型的TD-MPC2框架，这是一种整合规划和学习动力学的模型基强化学习方法。在多个导航任务上训练TD-MPC2智能体，并在保留的患者特定血管结构上进行评估，与最先进的SAC算法进行对比。进一步在荧光引导下使用患者特定血管模型进行体外验证。

Result: 模拟实验中，TD-MPC2平均成功率显著高于SAC（58% vs. 36%，p<0.001），平均尖端接触力为0.15N，远低于1.5N的血管破裂阈值。体外实验中，TD-MPC2成功率（68%）与SAC（60%）相当，但TD-MPC2实现了更优的路径比（p=0.017），代价是更长的手术时间（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 这是首次在保留的硅基数据和荧光引导体外实验中验证的自主机械取栓导航演示，凸显了世界模型在安全、通用AI辅助血管内介入中的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous mechanical thrombectomy (MT) presents substantial challenges due to highly variable vascular geometries and the requirements for accurate, real-time control. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for the automation of endovascular navigation, existing approaches often show limited robustness when faced with diverse patient anatomies or extended navigation horizons. In this work, we investigate a world-model-based framework for autonomous endovascular navigation built on TD-MPC2, a model-based RL method that integrates planning and learned dynamics. We evaluate a TD-MPC2 agent trained on multiple navigation tasks across hold out patient-specific vasculatures and benchmark its performance against the state-of-the-art Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm agent. Both approaches are further validated in vitro using patient-specific vascular phantoms under fluoroscopic guidance. In simulation, TD-MPC2 demonstrates a significantly higher mean success rate than SAC (58% vs. 36%, p < 0.001), and mean tip contact forces of 0.15 N, well below the proposed 1.5 N vessel rupture threshold. Mean success rates for TD-MPC2 (68%) were comparable to SAC (60%) in vitro, but TD-MPC2 achieved superior path ratios (p = 0.017) at the cost of longer procedure times (p < 0.001). Together, these results provide the first demonstration of autonomous MT navigation validated across both hold out in silico data and fluoroscopy-guided in vitro experiments, highlighting the promise of world models for safe and generalizable AI-assisted endovascular interventions.

</details>


### [7] [Bimanual Robot Manipulation via Multi-Agent In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20348)
*Alessio Palma,Indro Spinelli,Vignesh Prasad,Luca Scofano,Yufeng Jin,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCICLe框架使标准LLM无需微调即可执行少样本双手机器人操作，通过领导者-跟随者分解动作空间，结合辩论和裁判机制提升协调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作的高维关节动作空间和紧密的臂间协调约束使标准上下文窗口迅速饱和，限制了现有ICL方法在双手机器人控制中的应用。

Method: 提出BiCICLe框架：1) 将双手机器人控制建模为多智能体领导者-跟随者问题，将动作空间解耦为顺序的、条件化的单臂预测；2) 引入"臂间辩论"迭代优化过程；3) 使用第三个LLM作为裁判评估和选择最合理的协调轨迹。

Result: 在TWIN基准测试的13个任务中，BiCICLe达到最高71.1%的平均成功率，比最佳免训练基线高6.7个百分点，超越大多数监督方法，并在新任务上展示出强大的少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: BiCICLe首次使标准LLM能够执行少样本双手机器人操作而无需微调，通过动作空间解耦和协调机制有效解决了双手机器人操作中的高维协调挑战。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful reasoning engines for embodied control. In particular, In-Context Learning (ICL) enables off-the-shelf, text-only LLMs to predict robot actions without any task-specific training while preserving their generalization capabilities. Applying ICL to bimanual manipulation remains challenging, as the high-dimensional joint action space and tight inter-arm coordination constraints rapidly overwhelm standard context windows. To address this, we introduce BiCICLe (Bimanual Coordinated In-Context Learning), the first framework that enables standard LLMs to perform few-shot bimanual manipulation without fine-tuning. BiCICLe frames bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem, decoupling the action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. This naturally extends to Arms' Debate, an iterative refinement process, and to the introduction of a third LLM-as-Judge to evaluate and select the most plausible coordinated trajectories. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves up to 71.1% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.7 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. We further demonstrate strong few-shot generalization on novel tasks.

</details>


### [8] [Stochastic Barrier Certificates in the Presence of Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20208)
*Rayan Mazouz,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于随机障碍函数的动态障碍物环境下随机动态系统安全分析方法，引入时不变和时变障碍证书，通过凸优化提供有限时域安全概率下界保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动态障碍物环境下随机动态系统的安全性问题，现有方法在处理动态障碍物时存在保守性，需要更精确的安全概率保证方法

Method: 引入离散时间连续空间系统的时不变和时变随机障碍函数证书，利用Bellman最优性原理减少保守性，将多项式障碍函数合成转化为凸的平方和规划问题

Result: 时变障碍证书相比现有方法提供更紧的安全概率下界，在非线性系统与动态障碍物场景中验证了方法的准确性和可扩展性

Conclusion: 基于随机障碍函数的时变证书方法能有效处理动态障碍物环境，通过凸优化实现可计算的紧致安全保证，提升了随机系统安全分析的准确性和实用性

Abstract: Safety of stochastic dynamic systems in environments with dynamic obstacles is studied in this paper through the lens of stochastic barrier functions. We introduce both time-invariant and time-varying barrier certificates for discrete-time, continuous-space systems subject to uncertainty, which provide certified lower bounds on the probability of remaining within a safe set over a finite horizon. These certificates explicitly account for time-varying unsafe regions induced by obstacle dynamics. By leveraging Bellman's optimality perspective, the time-varying formulation directly captures temporal structure and yields less conservative bounds than state-of-the-art approaches. By restricting certificates to polynomial functions, we show that time-varying barrier synthesis can be formulated as a convex sum-of-squares program, enabling tractable optimization. Empirical evaluations on nonlinear systems with dynamic obstacles show that time-varying certificates consistently achieve tight guarantees, demonstrating improved accuracy and scalability over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [9] [Toward Cooperative Driving in Mixed Traffic: An Adaptive Potential Game-Based Approach with Field Test Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20231)
*Shiyu Fang,Xiaocong Zhao,Xuekai Liu,Peng Hang,Jianqiang Wang,Yunpeng Wang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应势博弈（APG）协同驾驶框架，用于解决CAV与HDV混合交通环境中个体需求与系统优化的协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法往往忽视参与者的个体需求和异质性，难以在CAV与HDV共存的混合交通环境中有效应用。需要一种能够同时优化个体目标和系统目标，并能适应HDV行为不确定性的协同框架。

Method: 1. 基于个体效用函数的一般形式及其单调关系建立系统效用函数，实现个体与系统目标的同步优化；2. 引入Shapley值计算每辆车在系统中的边际效用，量化其动态影响；3. 通过持续比较观测到的HDV行为与APG估计动作，动态优化HDV偏好估计。

Result: 消融研究表明，自适应更新Shapley值和HDV偏好估计显著提高了混合交通中的协同成功率。对比实验显示APG在安全性和效率方面优于其他协同方法。现场测试验证了该方法在真实场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: APG框架通过整合个体效用与系统优化、动态量化车辆影响、以及自适应估计HDV偏好，有效解决了CAV与HDV混合交通中的协同驾驶问题，提高了系统的安全性和效率。

Abstract: Connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs), which represent a significant advancement in autonomous driving technology, have the potential to greatly increase traffic safety and efficiency through cooperative decision-making. However, existing methods often overlook the individual needs and heterogeneity of cooperative participants, making it difficult to transfer them to environments where they coexist with human-driven vehicles (HDVs).To address this challenge, this paper proposes an adaptive potential game (APG) cooperative driving framework. First, the system utility function is established on the basis of a general form of individual utility and its monotonic relationship, allowing for the simultaneous optimization of both individual and system objectives. Second, the Shapley value is introduced to compute each vehicle's marginal utility within the system, allowing its varying impact to be quantified. Finally, the HDV preference estimation is dynamically refined by continuously comparing the observed HDV behavior with the APG's estimated actions, leading to improvements in overall system safety and efficiency. Ablation studies demonstrate that adaptively updating Shapley values and HDV preference estimation significantly improve cooperation success rates in mixed traffic. Comparative experiments further highlight the APG's advantages in terms of safety and efficiency over other cooperative methods. Moreover, the applicability of the approach to real-world scenarios was validated through field tests.

</details>


### [10] [Cortex 2.0: Grounding World Models in Real-World Industrial Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20246)
*Adriana Aida,Walida Amer,Katarina Bankovic,Dhruv Behl,Fabian Busch,Annie Bhalla,Minh Duong,Florian Gienger,Rohan Godse,Denis Grachev,Ralf Gulde,Elisa Hagensieker,Junpeng Hu,Shivam Joshi,Tobias Knoblauch,Likith Kumar,Damien LaRocque,Keerthana Lokesh,Omar Moured,Khiem Nguyen,Christian Preyss,Ranjith Sriganesan,Vikram Singh,Carsten Sponner,Anh Tong,Dominik Tuscher,Marc Tuscher,Pavan Upputuri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cortex 2.0提出从反应式控制转向规划-执行范式，通过在视觉潜在空间中生成候选未来轨迹并进行评分，显著提升了工业机器人长时程操作的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人操作需要跨平台、跨任务、跨物体分布的可靠长时程执行。现有的视觉-语言-动作模型虽然表现出良好的泛化能力，但本质上是反应式的，仅基于当前观察优化下一个动作，无法评估潜在未来，因此在长时程任务中容易因错误累积而失效。

Method: Cortex 2.0采用规划-执行范式：1) 在视觉潜在空间中生成候选未来轨迹；2) 对这些轨迹进行评分，评估预期成功率和效率；3) 仅执行评分最高的候选轨迹。该方法基于世界模型进行规划，而非单纯的反应式控制。

Result: 在单臂和双臂操作平台上，对四个复杂度递增的任务进行评估：拾放、物品和垃圾分拣、螺丝分拣、鞋盒拆包。Cortex 2.0在所有任务中均优于最先进的视觉-语言-动作基线模型。在具有严重杂乱、频繁遮挡和接触密集操作的非结构化环境中，系统保持可靠，而反应式策略会失效。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的规划能够在复杂的工业环境中可靠运行。Cortex 2.0通过从反应式控制转向规划-执行范式，显著提升了长时程机器人操作的鲁棒性和成功率，为工业机器人操作提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial robotic manipulation demands reliable long-horizon execution across embodiments, tasks, and changing object distributions. While Vision-Language-Action models have demonstrated strong generalization, they remain fundamentally reactive. By optimizing the next action given the current observation without evaluating potential futures, they are brittle to the compounding failure modes of long-horizon tasks. Cortex 2.0 shifts from reactive control to plan-and-act by generating candidate future trajectories in visual latent space, scoring them for expected success and efficiency, then committing only to the highest-scoring candidate. We evaluate Cortex 2.0 on a single-arm and dual-arm manipulation platform across four tasks of increasing complexity: pick and place, item and trash sorting, screw sorting, and shoebox unpacking. Cortex 2.0 consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action baselines, achieving the best results across all tasks. The system remains reliable in unstructured environments characterized by heavy clutter, frequent occlusions, and contact-rich manipulation, where reactive policies fail. These results demonstrate that world-model-based planning can operate reliably in complex industrial environments.

</details>


### [11] [Onboard Wind Estimation for Small UAVs Equipped with Low-Cost Sensors: An Aerodynamic Model-Integrated Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20290)
*Bingchen Cheng,Tielin Ma,Jingcheng Fu,Lulu Tao,Tianhui Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用小型无人机基本机载传感器（无需额外风测量设备）来估计飞行状态和风的方法，通过EKF与气动模型集成及自适应移动平均估计技术，提高风估计的准确性和平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为小型无人机实现自主风估计以支持节能飞行，但现有方法通常依赖额外风测量设备或流角测量，增加了成本和复杂性。需要开发仅使用基本自主飞行所需传感器的风估计方法。

Method: 核心方法包括：1) 扩展卡尔曼滤波器与气动模型集成；2) 自适应移动平均估计技术，用于提高风估计的准确性和平滑性。该方法无需流角测量，仅使用低成本基本机载传感器。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法能有效估计稳态和时变3D风矢量。分析了气动模型精度对风估计误差的影响以评估实际适用性。飞行测试验证了方法的有效性及实时机载计算的可行性，并系统检查了测试中的不确定性和误差源。

Conclusion: 该方法为小型无人机提供了仅使用基本机载传感器的有效风估计方案，支持节能飞行。研究为后续改进奠定了基础，包括对不确定性和误差源的系统分析。

Abstract: To enable autonomous wind estimation for energy-efficient flight in small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), this study proposes a method that estimates flight states and wind using only the low-cost essential onboard sensors required for autonomous flight, without relying on additional wind measurement devices. The core of the method includes an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with the aerodynamic model and an Adaptive Moving Average Estimation (AMAE) technique, which improves the accuracy and smoothness of the wind estimation. Simulation results show that the approach efficiently estimates both steady and time-varying 3D wind vectors without requiring flow angle measurements. The impact of aerodynamic model accuracy on wind estimation errors is also analyzed to assess practical applicability. Flight tests validate the effectiveness of the method and its feasibility for real-time onboard computation. Additionally, uncertainties and error sources encountered during testing are systematically examined, providing a foundation for further refinement.

</details>


### [12] [VTouch++: A Multimodal Dataset with Vision-Based Tactile Enhancement for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20444)
*Qianxi Hua,Xinyue Li,Zheng Yan,Yang Li,Chi Zhang,Yongyao Li,Yufei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VTOUCH数据集：用于双手机器人接触丰富操作的视觉触觉数据集，通过矩阵式任务设计和自动化数据收集解决现有数据集缺乏物理交互信号、系统任务组织和规模不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作，特别是接触丰富的任务仍然具有挑战性，主要原因是缺乏具有丰富物理交互信号、系统任务组织和足够规模的数据集。

Method: 1. 利用基于视觉的触觉传感提供高保真物理交互信号；2. 采用矩阵式任务设计实现系统化学习；3. 部署自动化数据收集管道覆盖真实世界需求驱动场景以确保可扩展性。

Result: 1. 在跨模态检索和真实机器人评估中进行了广泛的定量实验验证数据集有效性；2. 通过跨多个机器人、策略和任务的泛化推理展示了真实世界性能。

Conclusion: VTOUCH数据集通过提供丰富的物理交互信号、系统任务组织和可扩展的数据收集，有效解决了双手机器人接触丰富操作的数据集瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence has advanced rapidly in recent years; however, bimanual manipulation-especially in contact-rich tasks remains challenging. This is largely due to the lack of datasets with rich physical interaction signals, systematic task organization, and sufficient scale. To address these limitations, we introduce the VTOUCH dataset. It leverages vision based tactile sensing to provide high-fidelity physical interaction signals, adopts a matrix-style task design to enable systematic learning, and employs automated data collection pipelines covering real-world, demand-driven scenarios to ensure scalability. To further validate the effectiveness of the dataset, we conduct extensive quantitative experiments on cross-modal retrieval as well as real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate real-world performance through generalizable inference across multiple robots, policies, and tasks.

</details>


### [13] [ETac: A Lightweight and Efficient Tactile Simulation Framework for Learning Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20295)
*Zhe Xu,Feiyu Zhao,Xiyan Huang,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ETac是一个高保真、高效率的触觉仿真框架，通过轻量级数据驱动变形传播模型实现软体接触动力学仿真，支持大规模策略训练，在单GPU上实现4096个并行环境下的869FPS吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器在灵巧机械手中应用日益广泛，但依赖触觉感知的学习策略面临挑战，主要因为软体仿真在保真度和计算成本之间存在权衡。需要既能高保真模拟弹性软体交互，又能高效支持大规模策略训练的仿真框架。

Method: ETac采用轻量级数据驱动变形传播模型来捕捉软体接触动力学，实现高质量仿真并提升效率。该框架作为仿真后端，能够产生与有限元法相当的表面变形估计，并适用于真实触觉传感器建模。

Result: 在单个RTX 4090 GPU上，ETac支持4096个并行环境的强化学习，总吞吐量达到869FPS。训练出的盲抓取策略在四种物体类型上平均成功率84.45%，展示了ETac在触觉技能学习中的高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: ETac通过平衡仿真保真度和计算效率，使基于触觉的技能学习既高效又可扩展，为触觉感知的机器人操作策略训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are increasingly integrated into dexterous robotic manipulators to enhance contact perception. However, learning manipulation policies that rely on tactile sensing remains challenging, primarily due to the trade-off between fidelity and computational cost of soft-body simulations. To address this, we present ETac, a tactile simulation framework that models elastomeric soft-body interactions with both high fidelity and efficiency. ETac employs a lightweight data-driven deformation propagation model to capture soft-body contact dynamics, achieving high simulation quality and boosting efficiency that enables large-scale policy training. When serving as the simulation backend, ETac produces surface deformation estimates comparable to FEM and demonstrates applicability for modeling real tactile sensors. Then, we showcase its capability in training a blind grasping policy that leverages large-area tactile feedback to manipulate diverse objects. Running on a single RTX 4090 GPU, ETac supports reinforcement learning across 4,096 parallel environments, achieving a total throughput of 869 FPS. The resulting policy reaches an average success rate of 84.45% across four object types, underscoring ETac's potential to make tactile-based skill learning both efficient and scalable.

</details>


### [14] [AdaTracker: Learning Adaptive In-Context Policy for Cross-Embodiment Active Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20305)
*Kui Wu,Hao Chen,Jinzhu Han,Haijun Liu,Churan Wang,Yizhou Wang,Zhoujun Li,Si Liu,Fangwei Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaTracker：一种自适应上下文策略学习框架，通过显式建模不同机器人的物理约束和运动动态，实现跨形态的零样本视觉目标跟踪


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常为每个机器人平台单独训练模型，导致可扩展性差且泛化能力有限。不同机器人的物理约束和运动动态差异巨大，实现跨平台统一视觉跟踪具有挑战性。

Method: 提出AdaTracker框架，包含：1）Embodiment Context Encoder从历史数据推断特定形态的约束；2）Context-Aware Policy利用上下文表示动态调制，以零样本方式为未见过的形态推断最优控制动作；3）引入两个辅助目标确保准确上下文识别和时间一致性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，AdaTracker在跨形态泛化、样本效率和零样本适应方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: AdaTracker通过显式建模形态特定约束和自适应上下文学习，实现了跨多样机器人平台的鲁棒视觉跟踪，为统一模型在异构机器人系统中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Realizing active visual tracking with a single unified model across diverse robots is challenging, as the physical constraints and motion dynamics vary drastically from one platform to another. Existing approaches typically train separate models for each embodiment, leading to poor scalability and limited generalization. To address this, we propose AdaTracker, an adaptive in-context policy learning framework that robustly tracks targets on diverse robot morphologies. Our key insight is to explicitly model embodiment-specific constraints through an Embodiment Context Encoder, which infers embodiment-specific constraints from history. This contextual representation dynamically modulates a Context-Aware Policy, enabling it to infer optimal control actions for unseen embodiments in a zero-shot manner. To enhance robustness, we introduce two auxiliary objectives to ensure accurate context identification and temporal consistency. Experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that AdaTracker significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in cross-embodiment generalization, sample efficiency, and zero-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [15] [A Vision-Language-Action Model for Adaptive Ultrasound-Guided Needle Insertion and Needle Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20347)
*Yuelin Zhang,Qingpeng Ding,Longxiang Tang,Chengyu Fang,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的超声引导下自适应自动化针头插入与跟踪方法，通过统一的框架实现实时动态调整，显著提升跟踪精度和插入成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导下的针头插入是一项关键但具有挑战性的操作，现有方法多依赖手工设计的模块化控制流程，在复杂情况下性能下降。需要一种能够自适应调整、统一处理跟踪与控制的方法。

Method: 提出VLA模型框架，包含：1）跨深度融合（CDF）跟踪头，整合浅层位置和深层语义特征；2）跟踪条件（TraCon）寄存器，用于参数高效的特征调节；3）不确定性感知控制策略和异步VLA管道，实现自适应针头插入控制。

Result: 在针头跟踪和插入实验中，该方法持续优于最先进的跟踪器和手动操作，实现了更高的跟踪精度、更高的插入成功率以及更短的操作时间。

Conclusion: 该VLA框架为机器人超声系统提供了一种统一的自适应针头跟踪与插入控制方法，展示了在智能介入手术中的良好应用前景。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US)-guided needle insertion is a critical yet challenging procedure due to dynamic imaging conditions and difficulties in needle visualization. Many methods have been proposed for automated needle insertion, but they often rely on hand-crafted pipelines with modular controllers, whose performance degrades in challenging cases. In this paper, a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model is proposed for adaptive and automated US-guided needle insertion and tracking on a robotic ultrasound (RUS) system. This framework provides a unified approach to needle tracking and needle insertion control, enabling real-time, dynamically adaptive adjustment of insertion based on the obtained needle position and environment awareness. To achieve real-time and end-to-end tracking, a Cross-Depth Fusion (CDF) tracking head is proposed, integrating shallow positional and deep semantic features from the large-scale vision backbone. To adapt the pretrained vision backbone for tracking tasks, a Tracking-Conditioning (TraCon) register is introduced for parameter-efficient feature conditioning. After needle tracking, an uncertainty-aware control policy and an asynchronous VLA pipeline are presented for adaptive needle insertion control, ensuring timely decision-making for improved safety and outcomes. Extensive experiments on both needle tracking and insertion show that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art trackers and manual operation, achieving higher tracking accuracy, improved insertion success rates, and reduced procedure time, highlighting promising directions for RUS-based intelligent intervention.

</details>


### [16] [OVPD: A Virtual-Physical Fusion Testing Dataset of OnSite Auton-omous Driving Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20423)
*Yuhang Zhang,Jiarui Zhang,Bowen Jian,Xin Zhou,Zhichao Lv,Peng Hang,Rongjie Yu,Ye Tian,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVPD是一个虚拟物理融合的自动驾驶测试数据集，基于真实车辆在环测试，集成了虚拟背景交通和车路协同感知，构建可控的闭环测试环境，包含20个测试片段、15个原子场景、近3小时多模态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集缺乏真实车辆动力学反馈以及与周围交通和道路基础设施的闭环交互，难以反映自动驾驶系统的实际部署准备度。为填补这一空白，需要高保真、可回放、可诊断的测试数据。

Method: 采用真实车辆在环测试方法，在试验场构建虚拟物理融合的测试环境。集成虚拟背景交通与车路协同感知，形成可控的交互式闭环测试系统。数据集包含来自20个团队的测试片段，覆盖15个原子场景链。

Result: 创建了包含近3小时多模态数据的OVPD数据集，包括车辆轨迹和状态、控制指令、数字孪生渲染的环视观测数据。支持长尾规划决策验证、开环或平台支持的闭环评估，以及安全、效率、舒适度、规则遵守和交通影响等多维度综合评估。

Conclusion: OVPD数据集为自动驾驶系统提供了可操作的故障诊断和改进证据，填补了现有公开数据集在真实车辆动力学反馈和闭环交互方面的空白，支持更全面的系统验证和迭代改进。

Abstract: The rapid iteration of autonomous driving algorithms has created a growing demand for high-fidelity, replayable, and diagnosable testing data. However, many public datasets lack real vehicle dynamics feedback and closed-loop interaction with surrounding traffic and road infrastructure, limiting their ability to reflect deployment readiness. To address this gap, we present OVPD (OnSite Virtual-Physical Dataset), a virtual-physical fusion testing dataset released from the 2025 OnSite Autonomous Driving Challenge. Centered on real-vehicle-in-the-loop testing, OVPD integrates virtual background traffic with vehicle-infrastructure perception to build controllable and interactive closed-loop test environments on a proving ground. The dataset contains 20 testing clips from 20 teams over a scenario chain of 15 atomic scenarios, totaling nearly 3 hours of multi-modal data, including vehicle trajectories and states, control commands, and digital-twin-rendered surround-view observations. OVPD supports long-tail planning and decision-making validation, open-loop or platform-enabled closed-loop evaluation, and comprehensive assessment across safety, efficiency, comfort, rule compliance, and traffic impact, providing actionable evidence for failure diagnosis and iterative improvement. The dataset is available via: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Yuhang253820/Onsite_OPVD

</details>


### [17] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将多目标词典优化问题通过非均匀量化和位移转换为单目标标量优化问题，扩展MPPI求解器处理无二次输入成本问题，提出新的谓词鲁棒性度量，实现可解释且可扩展的词典STL最小违规运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划需要满足多个可能冲突的规范，当无法同时满足所有规范时，需要根据优先级最小化规范违规。STL提供了形式化语言来定义这些规范，但词典优化问题通常计算成本高昂

Method: 1. 使用非均匀量化和位移技术将多目标词典优化问题转换为单目标标量优化问题；2. 扩展确定性模型预测路径积分(MPPI)求解器，使其能高效处理无二次输入成本的优化问题；3. 提出结合空间和时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量

Result: 所提方法为词典STL最小违规运动规划提供了可解释且可扩展的解决方案，能够在单目标求解器框架内有效处理多规范冲突情况

Conclusion: 通过将词典优化转换为单目标优化并扩展MPPI求解器，实现了高效、可扩展的STL最小违规运动规划，为自动驾驶系统在规范冲突情况下提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [18] [MOMO: A framework for seamless physical, verbal, and graphical robot skill learning and adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20468)
*Markus Knauer,Edoardo Fiorini,Maximilian Mühlbauer,Stefan Schneyer,Promwat Angsuratanawech,Florian Samuel Lay,Timo Bachmann,Samuel Bustamante,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério,Thomas Eiband*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态机器人技能自适应框架，支持触觉、自然语言和图形界面三种交互方式，通过工具型LLM架构实现安全自然语言控制，并在工业机器人上验证实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人应用需要非专家用户能够轻松适应不同任务和环境的灵活系统，但不同适应需求需要不同的交互模态。

Method: 集成五种组件：基于能量的人类意图检测、工具型LLM架构（选择并参数化预定义函数而非生成代码）、核化运动基元（KMPs）进行运动编码、概率虚拟夹具引导演示记录、遍历控制用于表面处理。提供三种交互模态：触觉空间校正、自然语言语义修改、图形界面可视化编辑。

Result: 工具型LLM架构能够将技能适应从KMPs推广到遍历控制，实现语音指令表面处理。在7自由度扭矩控制机器人上的验证表明该方法在工业环境中的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态交互框架通过集成多种技术组件，为工业机器人技能适应提供了灵活、安全且用户友好的解决方案，特别适合非专家用户在工业环境中使用。

Abstract: Industrial robot applications require increasingly flexible systems that non-expert users can easily adapt for varying tasks and environments. However, different adaptations benefit from different interaction modalities. We present an interactive framework that enables robot skill adaptation through three complementary modalities: kinesthetic touch for precise spatial corrections, natural language for high-level semantic modifications, and a graphical web interface for visualizing geometric relations and trajectories, inspecting and adjusting parameters, and editing via-points by drag-and-drop. The framework integrates five components: energy-based human-intention detection, a tool-based LLM architecture (where the LLM selects and parameterizes predefined functions rather than generating code) for safe natural language adaptation, Kernelized Movement Primitives (KMPs) for motion encoding, probabilistic Virtual Fixtures for guided demonstration recording, and ergodic control for surface finishing. We demonstrate that this tool-based LLM architecture generalizes skill adaptation from KMPs to ergodic control, enabling voice-commanded surface finishing. Validation on a 7-DoF torque-controlled robot at the Automatica 2025 trade fair demonstrates the practical applicability of our approach in industrial settings.

</details>


### [19] [Temporal Difference Calibration in Sequential Tasks: Application to Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20472)
*Shelly Francis-Meretzki,Mirco Mutti,Yaniv Romano,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于机器人视觉-语言-动作模型的序列校准方法，通过时序差分价值估计来改进不确定性量化，在部分轨迹观测下提升任务成功置信度的校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人序列任务中缺乏可靠的不确定性量化方法，特别是在仅观察到部分轨迹的情况下。现有研究对序列校准的评估和改进探索不足，需要建立连接不确定性校准和强化学习的理论框架。

Method: 提出了序列校准的公式化方法，引入了Brier分数的序列扩展，证明了对于二元结果其风险最小化器与VLA策略的价值函数一致。利用时序差分价值估计作为时间上的原则性校准机制，将不确定性校准与强化学习联系起来。

Result: 实验表明，时序差分校准在模拟和真实机器人数据上相对于最先进方法提升了性能。有趣的是，当使用TD校准时，VLA的单步动作概率能够产生有竞争力的不确定性估计，这与之前使用不同校准技术的研究发现形成对比。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了序列不确定性校准与强化学习价值函数之间的理论联系，证明了时序差分估计可以作为有效的校准机制。这一框架为机器人VLA模型提供了改进的不确定性量化方法，特别是在部分轨迹观测的场景中。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models for robotics have highlighted the importance of reliable uncertainty quantification in sequential tasks. However, assessing and improving calibration in such settings remains mostly unexplored, especially when only partial trajectories are observed. In this work, we formulate sequential calibration for episodic tasks, where task-success confidence is produced along an episode, while success is determined at the end of it. We introduce a sequential extension of the Brier score and show that, for binary outcomes, its risk minimizer coincides with the VLA policy's value function. This connection bridges uncertainty calibration and reinforcement learning, enabling the use of temporal-difference (TD) value estimation as a principled calibration mechanism over time. We empirically show that TD calibration improves performance relative to the state-of-the-art on simulated and real-robot data. Interestingly, we show that when calibrated using TD, the VLA's single-step action probabilities can yield competitive uncertainty estimates, in contrast to recent findings that employed different calibration techniques.

</details>


### [20] [Passive Variable Impedance For Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20557)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Nepomuk Werner,Ribin Balachandran,Thomas Hulin,João Silvério,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的框架来稳定变刚度阻抗控制和多控制器仲裁，通过被动化方法解决闭环系统的非被动问题，实现灵活共享控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享控制方法常使用阻抗控制来跟踪机器人末端的目标位姿，其引导行为由刚度增益决定。当同时跟踪多个重要性变化的目标位姿时，需要随时间变化的权重来仲裁不同控制器的输出力矩。现有方法在变刚度和控制器仲裁方面存在闭环系统非被动问题，需要统一的稳定框架

Method: 将变刚度阻抗控制和控制器仲裁（通过缩放相加输出力矩）重新表述为统一框架，识别闭环系统中的被动性违反，提供被动化方法。不限制刚度矩阵或仲裁因子的设计，两者都可以是包含非对角元素的矩阵值，并随时间任意变化

Result: 提出的方法能够稳定标准阻抗控制器，允许开发新颖灵活的共享控制方法。在仿真和不同系统的真实机器人实验中验证了有效性，展示了可根据共享控制需求利用的不同行为

Conclusion: 建立了一个统一的被动化框架来处理变刚度阻抗控制和多控制器仲裁问题，为开发灵活共享控制方法提供了理论基础和实用工具，不限制刚度矩阵和仲裁因子的设计自由度

Abstract: Shared Control methods often use impedance control to track target poses in a robotic manipulator. The guidance behavior of such controllers is shaped by the used stiffness gains, which can be varying over time to achieve an adaptive guiding. When multiple target poses are tracked at the same time with varying importance, the corresponding output wrenches have to be arbitrated with weightings changing over time. In this work, we study the stabilization of both variable stiffness in impedance control as well as the arbitration of different controllers through a scaled addition of their output wrenches, reformulating both into a holistic framework. We identify passivity violations in the closed loop system and provide methods to passivate the system. The resulting approach can be used to stabilize standard impedance controllers, allowing for the development of novel and flexible shared control methods. We do not constrain the design of stiffness matrices or arbitration factors; both can be matrix-valued including off-diagonal elements and change arbitrarily over time. The proposed methods are furthermore validated in simulation as well as in real robot experiments on different systems, proving their effectiveness and showcasing different behaviors which can be utilized depending on the requirements of the shared control approach.

</details>


### [21] [Kinematic Optimization of Phalanx Length Ratios in Robotic Hands Using Potential Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20686)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Jeongdo Ahn,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于潜在灵巧度的五指机器人手节段长度比优化框架，使用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度作为评估指标，通过加权目标函数在约束下寻找最优设计配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人手设计阶段，如果不定义特定物体或操作任务，很难定量评估节段长度比对灵巧度的影响。因此需要一种系统化的框架来优化五指机器人手的节段长度比。

Method: 采用基于体素的工作空间离散化表示，关节运动均匀离散化评估。使用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度作为评估指标，通过加权目标函数在约束下优化拇指和其他手指的设计集，排除不产生重叠工作空间的组合。

Result: 结果显示各节段对整体灵巧度的贡献不均等，识别了影响每个节段的因素。权重系数的选择不一定直接最大化单个性能指标，因为评估指标在设计空间中分布不均匀。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析可达性、灵巧性和可控性之间的权衡提供了系统化方法，可作为多指机器人手运动学设计的实用指南。

Abstract: In the design stage of robotic hands, it is not straightforward to quantitatively evaluate the effect of phalanx length ratios on dexterity without defining specific objects or manipulation tasks. Therefore, this study presents a framework for optimizing the phalanx length ratios of a five-finger robotic hand based on potential dexterity within a kinematic structure. The proposed method employs global manipulability, workspace volume, overlap workspace volume, and fingertip sensitivity as evaluation metrics, and identifies optimal design configurations using a weighted objective function under given constraints. The reachable workspace is discretized using a voxel-based representation, and joint motions are discretized at uniform intervals for evaluation. The optimization is performed over design sets for both the thumb and the other fingers, and design combinations that do not generate overlap workspace are excluded. The results show that each phalanx does not contribute equally to the overall dexterity, and the factors influencing each phalanx are identified. In addition, it is observed that the selection of weighting coefficients does not necessarily lead to the direct maximization of individual performance metrics, due to the non-uniform distribution of evaluation measures within the design space. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to analyze the trade-offs among reachability, dexterity, and controllability, and can serve as a practical guideline for the kinematic design of multi-fingered robotic hands.

</details>


### [22] [A Kinematic Framework for Evaluating Pinch Configurations in Robotic Hand Design without Object or Contact Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20692)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Hyunmok Jung,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于指尖工作空间交互的机器人手捏取能力运动学评估方法，无需物体几何或接触力模型信息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取评估方法大多依赖物体几何或接触力模型，限制了其在机器人手设计早期阶段的应用，需要一种仅基于运动学结构的评估方法

Method: 计算每个指尖的可达工作空间，通过评估指尖对之间的关系检测可行的捏取配置，分析四种不同运动学结构对手捏取配置的影响

Result: 该方法能够仅基于机器人手的运动学结构评估其捏取能力，为不同机器人手设计比较和设计阶段捏取能力分析提供了有用工具

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架可作为比较不同机器人手设计和在设计阶段分析捏取能力的有用工具，为早期设计阶段提供有效的运动学评估方法

Abstract: Evaluating the pinch capability of a robotic hand is important for understanding its functional dexterity. However, many existing grasp evaluation methods rely on object geometry or contact force models, which limits their applicability during the early stages of robotic hand design. This study proposes a kinematic evaluation method for analyzing pinch configurations of robotic hands based on interactions between fingertip workspaces. First, the reachable workspace of each fingertip is computed from the joint configurations of the fingers. Then, feasible pinch configurations are detected by evaluating the relationships between fingertip pairs. Since the proposed method does not require information about object geometry or contact force models, the pinch capability of a robotic hand can be evaluated solely based on its kinematic structure. In addition, analyses are performed on four different kinematic structures of the hand to investigate their impact on the pinch configurations. The proposed evaluation framework can serve as a useful tool for comparing different robotic hand designs and analyzing pinch capability during the design stage.

</details>


### [23] [Visual-Tactile Peg-in-Hole Assembly Learning from Peg-out-of-Hole Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20712)
*Yongqiang Zhao,Xuyang Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Matteo Leonetti,Emmanouil Spyrakos-Papastavridis,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用拆卸任务（PooH）辅助学习装配任务（PiH）的视觉-触觉技能学习框架，通过反转和随机化拆卸轨迹为装配提供专家数据，显著提升学习效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在Peg-in-hole装配任务中需要大量探索，效率低下。拆卸任务（PooH）比装配任务（PiH）更容易执行，因为只需克服现有摩擦而无需精确对准，数据收集更高效。利用PooH任务辅助PiH学习可以显著减少探索成本。

Method: 1. 将PooH和PiH统一建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDPs），共享视觉-触觉观测空间；2. 首先训练视觉-触觉PooH策略；3. 将PooH轨迹（包含运动学、视觉和触觉信息）时间反转并进行动作随机化，为PiH提供专家数据；4. 在策略学习中，视觉感知辅助钉孔接近，触觉测量补偿钉孔错位。

Result: 1. 视觉-触觉策略比单模态策略降低6.4%的接触力；2. 在已知物体上平均成功率达到87.5%，在未知物体上达到77.1%；3. 相比从零开始训练的RL方法，成功率提升18.1%。

Conclusion: 通过利用更容易的拆卸任务（PooH）为困难的装配任务（PiH）提供专家数据，提出的视觉-触觉技能学习框架显著提高了学习效率和成功率，为机器人操作任务提供了一种有效的逆任务辅助学习方法。

Abstract: Peg-in-hole (PiH) assembly is a fundamental yet challenging robotic manipulation task. While reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in tackling such tasks, it requires extensive exploration. In this paper, we propose a novel visual-tactile skill learning framework for the PiH task that leverages its inverse task, i.e., peg-out-of-hole (PooH) disassembly, to facilitate PiH learning. Compared to PiH, PooH is inherently easier as it only needs to overcome existing friction without precise alignment, making data collection more efficient. To this end, we formulate both PooH and PiH as Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) in a unified environment with shared visual-tactile observation space. A visual-tactile PooH policy is first trained; its trajectories, containing kinematic, visual and tactile information, are temporally reversed and action-randomized to provide expert data for PiH. In the policy learning, visual sensing facilitates the peg-hole approach, while tactile measurements compensate for peg-hole misalignment. Experiments across diverse peg-hole geometries show that the visual-tactile policy attains 6.4% lower contact forces than its single-modality counterparts, and that our framework achieves average success rates of 87.5% on seen objects and 77.1% on unseen objects, outperforming direct RL methods that train PiH policies from scratch by 18.1% in success rate. Demos, code, and datasets are available at https://sites.google.com/view/pooh2pih.

</details>


### [24] [ALAS: Adaptive Long-Horizon Action Synthesis via Async-pathway Stream Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20721)
*Yutong Shen,Hangxu Liu,Lei Zhang,Penghui Liu,Yinqi Liu,Liuxiang Yang,Tongtong Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALAS：一种通过生物启发的双流解耦实现长时程任务的跨域学习框架，在人类-场景交互任务中显著提升子任务成功率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖技能链式拼接，环境观测与自身状态紧密耦合，缺乏对新环境-技能组合的泛化能力，无法完成跨域的各种长时程任务。

Method: ALAS采用生物启发的"where-what"双通路机制，包含两个核心模块：1) 环境学习模块用于空间理解，捕获物体功能、空间关系和场景语义；2) 技能学习模块用于任务执行，处理自身状态信息包括关节自由度和运动模式。

Result: 在HSI场景的各种长时程任务实验中，ALAS相比现有方法平均子任务成功率提升23%，平均执行效率提升29%。

Conclusion: ALAS通过环境-自我完全解耦实现跨域迁移，通过独立运动模式编码实现跨技能迁移，为解决长时程任务的跨域学习问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Long-Horizon (LH) tasks in Human-Scene Interaction (HSI) are complex multi-step tasks that require continuous planning, sequential decision-making, and extended execution across domains to achieve the final goal. However, existing methods heavily rely on skill chaining by concatenating pre-trained subtasks, with environment observations and self-state tightly coupled, lacking the ability to generalize to new combinations of environments and skills, failing to complete various LH tasks across domains. To solve this problem, this paper presents ALAS, a cross-domain learning framework for LH tasks via biologically inspired dual-stream disentanglement. Inspired by the brain's "where-what" dual pathway mechanism, ALAS comprises two core modules: i) an environment learning module for spatial understanding, which captures object functions, spatial relationships, and scene semantics, achieving cross-domain transfer through complete environment-self disentanglement; ii) a skill learning module for task execution, which processes self-state information including joint degrees of freedom and motor patterns, enabling cross-skill transfer through independent motor pattern encoding. We conducted extensive experiments on various LH tasks in HSI scenes. Compared with existing methods, ALAS can achieve an average subtasks success rate improvement of 23\% and average execution efficiency improvement of 29\%.

</details>


### [25] [A Hough transform approach to safety-aware scalar field mapping using Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20799)
*Muzaffar Qureshi,Trivikram Satharasi,Tochukwu E. Ogri,Kyle Volle,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个在危险环境中使用机器人安全高效测绘未知标量场的框架，通过高斯过程建模和霍夫变换实时估计危险区域，结合安全采样策略和运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危险环境中（场值超过安全阈值的区域）进行标量场测绘时，需要确保传感器机器人的安全，同时高效完成测绘任务。现有方法需要同时处理场估计和危险区域避让的挑战。

Method: 1. 使用高斯过程(GP)对标量场进行建模，支持贝叶斯推断和闭式预测；2. 利用霍夫变换(HT)实时估计高强度危险区域的空间结构；3. 基于GP后验的概率安全保证设计安全采样策略；4. 结合危险区域估计设计安全的机器人运动规划。

Result: 通过两个数值仿真研究和一个室内光强场测绘实验验证了方法的有效性，使用轮式移动机器人成功实现了安全高效的场测绘。

Conclusion: 该框架成功整合了高斯过程建模、霍夫变换危险区域估计和安全采样策略，能够在危险环境中实现安全高效的标量场测绘，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a framework for mapping unknown scalar fields using a sensor-equipped autonomous robot operating in unsafe environments. The unsafe regions are defined as regions of high-intensity, where the field value exceeds a predefined safety threshold. For safe and efficient mapping of the scalar field, the sensor-equipped robot must avoid high-intensity regions during the measurement process. In this paper, the scalar field is modeled as a sample from a Gaussian process (GP), which enables Bayesian inference and provides closed-form expressions for both the predictive mean and the uncertainty. Concurrently, the spatial structure of the high-intensity regions is estimated in real-time using the Hough transform (HT), leveraging the evolving GP posterior. A safe sampling strategy is then employed to guide the robot towards safe measurement locations, using probabilistic safety guarantees on the evolving GP posterior. The estimated high-intensity regions also facilitate the design of safe motion plans for the robot. The effectiveness of the approach is verified through two numerical simulation studies and an indoor experiment for mapping a light-intensity field using a wheeled mobile robot.

</details>


### [26] [PokeVLA: Empowering Pocket-Sized Vision-Language-Action Model with Comprehensive World Knowledge Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20834)
*Yupeng Zheng,Xiang Li,Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Shuai Tian,Weize Li,Linbo Wang,Senyu Fei,Pengfei Li,Yinfeng Gao,Zebin Xing,Yilun Chen,Qichao Zhang,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PokeVLA是一个轻量级但强大的机器人操作基础模型，通过两阶段训练范式将视觉语言理解有效注入动作学习，在LIBERO-Plus基准测试和实际部署中实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中存在效率有限、缺乏高级知识和空间意识的问题，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1) 在包含240万样本的多模态数据集上预训练紧凑视觉语言模型PokeVLM；2) 通过多视角目标感知语义学习、几何对齐和新颖动作专家将操作相关表示注入动作空间。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准测试和真实世界部署中展示了最先进的性能，在成功率和多样化扰动下的鲁棒性方面优于可比基线。

Conclusion: PokeVLA是一个高效且强大的具身操作基础模型，通过有效融合视觉语言理解和动作学习解决了现有方法的局限性，并承诺开源促进社区发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have opened new avenues for robot manipulation, yet existing methods exhibit limited efficiency and a lack of high-level knowledge and spatial awareness. To address these challenges, we propose PokeVLA, a lightweight yet powerful foundation model for embodied manipulation that effectively infuses vision-language understanding into action learning. Our framework introduces a two-stage training paradigm: first, we pre-train a compact vision-language model (PokeVLM) on a curated multimodal dataset of 2.4M samples encompassing spatial grounding, affordance, and embodied reasoning tasks; second, we inject manipulation-relevant representations into the action space through multi-view goal-aware semantics learning, geometry alignment, and a novel action expert. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the LIBERO-Plus benchmark and in real-world deployment, outperforming comparable baselines in success rate and robustness under diverse perturbations. To foster reproducibility and community progress, we will open-source our code, model weights, and the scripts for the curated pre-training dataset. Project page: https://getterupper.github.io/PokeVLA

</details>

### [27] [Going MLIR-native: Demonstrating a Future for DSL compilers on a NumPy-like Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19906)
*Karl F. A. Friebel,Jascha A. Ohlmann,Jeronimo Castrillon*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全基于MLIR的NumPy风格DSL，用于卸载数值张量内核，实现了首个开源项目中在MLIR内部完成所有前端操作和语义分析，并展示了在天气建模和计算流体动力学领域的实际应用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言编译器在特定应用领域相比领域特定语言(DSL)编译器处于劣势，但DSL编译器领域缺乏统一的编译器框架和软件生态系统，导致大量工作重复、维护困难且难以达到"生产就绪"状态。MLIR的出现为DSL编译器带来了类似LLVM对通用工具链的影响。

Method: 开发了一个完全基于MLIR的NumPy风格DSL，首次在开源项目中实现了所有前端操作和语义分析直接在MLIR内部完成。创建了新的方言无关的MLIR类型检查器，采用简单有效的并行优先降低方案，将语言连接到另一个MLIR数据流方言以实现无缝卸载。

Result: 该方法在天气建模和计算流体动力学(CFD)领域的Fortran实际用例中表现良好，证明了MLIR-native DSL的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: MLIR-native DSL方法为解决DSL编译器领域的碎片化问题提供了有效途径，新的方言无关类型检查器为MLIR中DSL的未来发展奠定了基础，并行优先降低方案实现了与现有数据流方言的无缝集成。

Abstract: Compilers for general-purpose languages have been shown to be at a disadvantage when it comes to specialized application domains as opposed to their Domain-Specific Language (DSL) counterparts. However, the field of DSL compilers features little consolidation in terms of compiler frameworks and adjacent software ecosystems. As a result, considerable work is duplicated, lost to maintenance issues, or remains undiscovered, and most DSLs are never considered "production-ready". One notable development is the introduction of the Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR), which promises a similar impact on DSL compilers as LLVM had on general-purpose tooling.
  In this work, we present a NumPy-like DSL made for offloading numeric tensor kernels that is entirely MLIR-native. In a first for open-source, it implements all frontend actions and semantic analyses directly within MLIR. Most notably, this is made possible by our new dialect-agnostic MLIR type checker, created for the future of DSLs in MLIR. We implement a simple, yet effective, parallel-first lowering scheme that connects our language to another MLIR dataflow dialect for seamless offloading. We show that our approach performs well in real-world use cases from the domain of weather modeling and Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) in Fortran.

</details>


### [28] [Automatic Code and Test Generation of Smart Contracts from Coordination Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20507)
*Elvis Konjoh Selabi,Maurizio Murgia,António Ravara,Emilio Tuosto*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种用于分布式系统中去中心化协调的形式化方法，特别关注智能合约，包含动态角色、数据驱动转换和外部协调接口，支持形式化模型验证、Solidity代码生成和自动化测试合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为分布式系统中的去中心化协调提供形式化规范，特别是针对智能合约，以支持高层级的去中心化工作流推理，解决动态角色、数据驱动转换和外部协调接口的建模问题。

Method: 提出一个形式化模型，捕捉动态角色、数据驱动转换和外部协调接口；实现工具链支持形式化模型验证、Solidity代码生成（可扩展到其他智能合约语言）和自动化测试合成；方法平台无关，可推广到其他面向服务和分布式架构。

Result: 通过建模和实现智能合约中的协调模式，展示了该方法的表达能力和实用性；工具链支持形式化验证和代码生成，方法具有平台无关性，可应用于区块链平台及其他分布式架构。

Conclusion: 提出的形式化方法能够有效规范和实现分布式系统中的去中心化协调，特别适用于智能合约，具有表达能力强、实用性高和平台无关的特点，可推广到更广泛的分布式系统领域。

Abstract: We propose a formal approach for specifying and implementing decentralised coordination in distributed systems, with a focus on smart contracts. Our model captures dynamic roles, data-driven transitions, and external coordination interfaces, enabling high-level reasoning about decentralised workflows. We implement a toolchain that supports formal model validation, code generation for Solidity (our framework is extendable to other smart contract languages), and automated test synthesis. Although our implementation targets blockchain platforms, the methodology is platform-agnostic and may generalise to other service-oriented and distributed architectures. We demonstrate the expressiveness and practicality of the approach by modelling and realising some coordination patterns in smart contracts.

</details>

### [29] [Hint-Writing with Deferred AI Assistance: Fostering Critical Engagement in Data Science Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19931)
*Anjali Singh,Christopher Brooks,Warren Li,Juho Kim,Xu Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了三种个性化、可扩展的提示编写活动设计，发现延迟AI辅助能产生最高质量的提示，帮助学生识别更多错误，并培养调试和批判性评估AI输出的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为错误代码生成提示是一项促进学习和元认知发展的认知要求高的任务。研究旨在探索如何在数据科学课程中设计个性化、可扩展且具有反思性的提示编写活动，并研究AI支持如何在不减少学生生产性认知努力的情况下支持学习过程。

Method: 采用随机对照实验，研究生参与者(N=97)被分配到三种提示编写活动设计：(i)独立编写提示，(ii)按需AI辅助编写提示，(iii)延迟AI辅助(先独立编写，然后借助AI生成的提示进行修订)。研究比较了不同设计下的提示质量、错误识别能力以及学生对活动的评价。

Result: 延迟AI辅助设计产生了最高质量的提示。该设计帮助学生识别了在没有AI辅助时难以发现的各种错误。学生重视这些活动，认为它们是练习调试和批判性参与AI输出的机会。研究还发现了设计学生-AI协作学习体验的关键考虑因素。

Conclusion: 延迟AI辅助是设计提示编写活动的有效策略，既能提高提示质量，又能培养学生的调试和AI批判性评估能力。研究结果为设计学生-AI协作学习体验提供了重要见解，强调了维持学生参与度、管理认知负荷和减轻AI负面影响的重要性。

Abstract: Generating hints for incorrect code is a cognitively demanding task that fosters learning and metacognitive development. This study investigates three designs for personalized, scalable, and reflective hint-writing activities within a data science course: (i) writing a hint independently, (ii) writing a hint with on-demand AI assistance, and (iii) deferred AI assistance, in which students first write a hint independently and then revise it with the help of an AI-generated one. We examine how AI support can scaffold the learning process without diminishing students' productive cognitive effort. Through a randomized controlled experiment with graduate-level students (N=97), we found that deferring AI assistance leads to the highest-quality hints. Further, this design helps students identify a wide range of mistakes they otherwise struggle to identify without any AI assistance. Students valued these activities as opportunities to practice debugging and critically engage with AI outputs--skills that are now critical for learners to acquire as programming becomes increasingly automated and the use of AI for learning grows. Our findings also highlight key considerations for designing student-AI collaborative learning experiences to sustain student engagement, maintain appropriate cognitive load, and mitigate negative effects of AI, such as introducing redundancies and extraneous information into student work.

</details>


### [30] [LatentGandr: Visual Exploration of Generative AI Latent Space via Local Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19953)
*Mingwei Li,Suyang Li,Daisuke Sakurai,Bei Wang,Remco Chang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LatentGandr是一种可视化分析技术，通过提取高维潜在空间中嵌入的局部线性维度来促进潜在空间探索，解决了现有方法在控制维度增加时的可扩展性和可用性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在创意设计中展现出巨大潜力，但尽管深度AI模型在潜在空间中实现了有效的特征化，导航该空间仍然具有挑战性。现有技术如GANSlider和SliderSpace使用多个滑块生成高维向量，但即使应用全局PCA减少滑块数量，随着控制维度增加，这些方法仍面临可扩展性和可用性问题。

Method: LatentGandr通过分析嵌入的拓扑结构和局部曲率，自动识别局部邻域，并使用局部化PCA计算其主成分。这些局部主成分被可视化为交互式图像网格，允许用户高效探索和控制生成过程，为生成新颖内容和概念提供直观的细化手段。

Result: 通过与当前最先进的生成式AI模型可视化界面GANSlider进行比较研究，评估了LatentGandr的有效性。结果表明，局部化探索技术能够增强用户与这些模型的交互体验。

Conclusion: LatentGandr提供了一种有效的潜在空间探索方法，通过局部线性维度提取和可视化，解决了现有方法在可扩展性和可用性方面的限制，为生成式AI模型的用户交互提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Generative AI has demonstrated significant potential in creative design, enabling the rapid generation of visual content and imaginative concepts. Although deep AI models achieve effective featurization in the latent space, navigating the space remains a challenge. Current techniques, such as GANSlider and SliderSpace, use multiple sliders to generate high-dimensional vectors in generative AI's latent space. Despite applying (global) PCA to reduce the number of sliders, these approaches struggle with scalability and usability as the number of control dimensions increases. In this paper, we introduce LatentGandr, a visual analytics technique that facilitates latent space exploration by extracting locally linear dimensions from embeddings in high-dimensional latent spaces. By analyzing the topology and local curvature of the embeddings, LatentGandr automatically identifies local neighborhoods and computes their principal components using localized PCA. These local principal components are visualized as interactive image grids, allowing users to efficiently explore and control the generative process, providing an intuitive means to refine the generation of novel content and concepts. To evaluate the effectiveness of LatentGandr, we conducted a study comparing it to GANSlider, the current state-of-the-art visualization interface for generative AI models. The results offer insights into how localized exploration techniques can enhance user interaction with these models.

</details>


### [31] [Enhancing immersion in Virtual Reality sports through Physical Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20071)
*Arka Majhi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究VR体育控制器设计，通过物理原型增强滑冰VR游戏的沉浸感，对比市场现有控制器评估沉浸效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VR控制器在模拟体育场景中难以将真实世界体验与虚拟世界动作连接，导致沉浸感不足，需要设计具有真实触觉映射的物理控制器来解决这一问题

Method: 识别现有VR控制器的HCI问题，设计具有真实触觉映射的物理控制器原型，在设计的滑冰VR游戏中进行评估，通过Likert量表测量感知交互性、现实感、空间存在感和愉悦度等参数

Result: 研究将提供控制器评估结果，比较设计原型与市场现有控制器的表现，揭示影响沉浸感的关键参数

Conclusion: 研究结果将帮助理解设计针对特定体育项目的未来控制器时应考虑的参数，为定制化VR控制器设计提供指导

Abstract: Recent discoveries in VR have opened up scope for designing physical tools and controllers to enhance immersion, through perceived reality. In a virtually simulated sports scenario it is challenging to immerse user because most of the available controllers are unable to bridge the user experience in the real world to the actions in the virtual world. My research is to identify HCI problems in existing VR controllers, design a physical controller prototype with realistic tangible mapping, trying to solve the existing problems and evaluate it in a designed VR game for skating. Its immersiveness would be graded on Likert scale on parameters like perceived interactivity and reality, spatial presence and enjoyment. The evaluation will be done after trial runs and feedback sessions by playing the game with the designed controller and comparing it with ones available in the market. The findings will help people understand what all parameters we should consider while designing futuristic controllers, customized for a particular sport.

</details>


### [32] [Heterogeneous Layered Structures Can Modulate Human Softness Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20092)
*Yuno Higuchi,Yosuke Iwashita,Yuji Ohgi,Masashi Nakatani*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索了分层异质性如何调节触觉柔软度感知，发现最外层对感知影响最大，软质亚表层数量增加会提升感知柔软度，但贡献随深度递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现实世界中许多物体具有非均匀刚度的异质分层结构，但触觉柔软度感知研究主要使用机械均匀物体。本研究旨在探究分层异质性如何调节触觉柔软度感知。

Method: 通过3D打印制造16种晶格结构刺激物，系统改变上四层刚度，固定底部两层。22名参与者完成心理物理任务评估柔软度，并进行压缩测试量化机械特性。

Result: 感知柔软度显著由载荷下位移预测，但感知排名与物理排名不完全一致。最外层对感知柔软度影响最大；软质亚表层数量增加会提升感知柔软度，但贡献随深度递减，第2、3层有显著效应，第4层无显著效应。

Conclusion: 触觉柔软度感知不仅取决于整体刚度，还依赖于分层结构内柔顺性的深度依赖性分布，最外层对感知影响最大，亚表层贡献随深度递减。

Abstract: Human softness perception in haptics has mainly been studied using mechanically homogeneous objects, despite the fact that many real-world objects exhibit heterogeneous layered structures with nonuniform stiffness. This study examined how layered heterogeneity modulates haptic softness perception. Sixteen lattice-structured stimuli were fabricated by 3D printing, with the stiffness of the upper four layers systematically varied while the bottom two layers remained fixed. Twenty-two participants evaluated the softness of the stimuli in a psychophysical task, and compression tests were conducted to quantify their mechanical properties. Perceived softness was significantly predicted by displacement under load, however, perceptual ranking did not fully coincide with the physical ranking. Linear mixed-effects analyses showed that the softness of the outermost layer had the greatest impact on the perceived softness. Perceived softness also increased as the number of soft subsurface layers increased, although this contribution decreased with depth. Layers 2 and 3 showed significant effects, whereas Layer 4 did not. These findings suggest that haptic softness perception depends not only on the overall stiffness but also on the depth-dependent distribution of compliance within layered structures.

</details>


### [33] [Vibrotactile Preference Learning: Uncertainty-Aware Preference Learning for Personalized Vibration Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20210)
*Rongtao Zhang,Xin Zhu,Masoume Pourebadi Khotbehsara,Warren Dao,Erdem Bıyık,Heather Culbertson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VPL系统通过高斯过程不确定性感知偏好学习，在40轮成对比较中高效学习用户对振动触觉参数的个性化偏好


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振动触觉反馈在交互系统中日益普及，个体感知差异凸显个性化的重要性，需要开发能够高效学习用户特定偏好的系统

Method: 提出VPL系统，基于高斯过程的不确定性感知偏好学习，采用期望信息增益获取策略指导40轮成对比较查询选择，结合用户报告的不确定性，高效探索参数空间

Result: 在13名用户研究中，使用微软Xbox控制器振动反馈验证了VPL能高效学习个体化偏好，同时保持舒适、低工作负荷的用户交互

Conclusion: VPL展示了振动触觉体验可扩展个性化的潜力，为交互系统中的个性化触觉反馈提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Individual differences in vibrotactile perception underscore the growing importance of personalization as haptic feedback becomes more prevalent in interactive systems. We propose Vibrotactile Preference Learning (VPL), a system that captures user-specific preference spaces over vibrotactile parameters via Gaussian-process-based uncertainty-aware preference learning. VPL uses an expected information gain-based acquisition strategy to guide query selection over 40 rounds of pairwise comparisons of overall user preference, augmented with user-reported uncertainty, enabling efficient exploration of the parameter space. We evaluate VPL in a user study (N = 13) using the vibrotactile feedback from a Microsoft Xbox controller, showing that it efficiently learns individualized preferences while maintaining comfortable, low-workload user interactions. These results highlight the potential of VPL for scalable personalization of vibrotactile experiences.

</details>


### [34] [Odor Maps from the LLM-derived similarity scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20310)
*Yuki Harada,Manuel Aleixandre,Manabu Okumura,Takamichi Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了大型语言模型在气味空间分析中的应用，通过统计比较LLM生成的气味相似性与原始数据距离，发现LLM能够一定程度推断气味相似性，并基于此生成了精油气味地图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在气味空间分析中的应用日益受到关注，本研究旨在探索LLM生成的气味相似性评估与Dravnieks数据集中气味特征谱的对应关系，验证LLM在气味相似性推断方面的潜力

Method: 使用三种距离度量方法计算气味描述符的成对距离，统计比较LLM导出的相似性与原始数据距离；将此方法扩展到气味名称（成分）分析；基于相似性数据生成气味地图，并以精油为例进行应用验证

Result: 统计比较显示LLM能够在一定程度上推断气味相似性；生成的精油气味地图表明同一组内的精油在气味地图中位置相近，说明气味地图中的接近程度与人类评估结果相符

Conclusion: LLM在气味相似性推断方面具有潜力，基于LLM生成的气味相似性数据可以构建有效的嗅觉地图，为气味空间分析和分类提供了新的计算方法

Abstract: The application of large language models (LLMs) to OdorSpace analysis attracts growing interest. Recent studies have explored the comparison of sensory evaluation spaces derived from LLMs with odor character profiles in the Dravnieks' dataset. In this study, we calculated pairwise distances of odor descriptors using three distance measures and statistically compared these LLM-derived similarities with distances derived from the original data. Next, we extended this approach to odor names (ingredients). Statistical comparison revealed that LLMs can infer odor similarity to some degree, suggesting the potential of odor maps generated from these similarity data. Applying this approach, we generated an odor map of essential oils. It demonstrates that essential oils within the same group are closely located in the odor map, suggesting that the proximity in the odor map corresponds to human evaluation.

</details>


### [35] [The Effect of Idea Elaboration on the Automatic Assessment of Idea Originality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20569)
*Umberto Domanti,Moritz Mock,Sergio Agnoli,Antonella De Angeli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型在评估发散思维任务中的原创性时存在自我偏好偏差，倾向于给AI生成的回答更高评分，但当控制想法阐述程度后这种偏差消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动评估系统在创造性任务中应用日益广泛，虽然能解决人工评估的成本、疲劳和主观性等限制，但存在自我偏好偏差的初步证据。本研究旨在探究大型语言模型在发散思维任务中评估原创性时与人类评估者的一致性程度。

Method: 研究分析了4,813个替代用途任务的回答，包括高创造性和低创造性人类以及ChatGPT-4o生成的回答。人类评估者为两名经过密集培训的大学生，机器评估者包括两个在AUT回答和相应人类评分上微调的专业系统（OCSAI和CLAUS），以及使用与人类相同指令提示的ChatGPT-4o。

Result: 结果证实了LLMs中存在自我偏好偏差，自动系统倾向于给人工智能生成的回答更高评分。然而，当分析控制想法阐述程度时，这种自我偏好偏差消失。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLMs在创造性评估中的自我偏好偏差问题，但表明这种偏差可能与回答的阐述程度有关。研究强调了未来创造性评估研究需要考虑控制变量，并指出了自动评估系统在创造性任务中应用的理论和方法论意义。

Abstract: Automatic systems are increasingly used to assess the originality of responses in creative tasks. They offer a potential solution to key limitations of human assessment (cost, fatigue, and subjectivity), but there is preliminary evidence of a self-preference bias. Accordingly, automatic systems tend to prefer outcomes that are more closely related to their style, rather than to the human one. In this paper, we investigated how Large Language Models (LLMs) align with human raters in assessing the originality of responses in a divergent thinking task. We analysed 4,813 responses to the Alternate Uses Task produced by higher and lower creative humans and ChatGPT-4o. Human raters were two university students who underwent intensive training. Machine raters were two specialised systems fine-tuned on AUT responses and corresponding human ratings (OCSAI and CLAUS) and ChatGPT-4o, which was prompted with the same instructions as human raters. Results confirmed the presence of a self-preference bias in LLMs. Automatic systems tended to privilege artificial responses. However, this self-preference bias disappeared when the analyses controlled for the idea elaboration. We discuss theoretical and methodological implications of these findings by highlighting future directions for research on creativity assessment.

</details>


### [36] [Short-time, Wavelet-inspired Mouse Submovement Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20673)
*Auejin Ham,Ben Boudaoud*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于小波启发的技术，用于从一维速度时间序列中准确定位和参数化子运动，通过自加权损失细化步骤解决拟合质量差的区域问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子运动是人类运动中的弹道组成部分，源于感知、运动规划和运动执行的循环重叠认知过程。由于运动往往重叠或在前一个结束前开始，提取子运动具有挑战性。

Method: 采用小波启发技术，结合自加权损失细化步骤来识别和改进拟合质量差的区域。该方法处理一维速度时间序列，在合成数据上验证准确性。

Result: 在约6,400个1-2秒的合成第一人称射击游戏瞄准数据试验中验证了方法的准确性，与双阈值和持久性1D分割技术相比表现出优势。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效提取和参数化重叠的子运动，为未来改进提供了挑战和机遇，特别是在处理复杂运动模式方面。

Abstract: Submovements are ballistic components of human motion constituting a large part of motor interaction and arising from the cyclical and overlapping cognitive processes of perception, motor planning, and motor execution. Extracting submovements is challenging as the motions tend to overlap, or start before the previous ends. We propose and evaluate use of a wavelet-inspired technique to accurately locate and parameterize submovements from one-dimensional speed time series. Our method employs a self-weighted loss refinement step to identify and improve regions of poor quality of fit, a challenge for simpler wavelet transforms. We demonstrate the accuracy of our method by presenting analysis of ~6,400 1-2s trials of synthetic egocentric camera (first-person shooter) aim data for which we know ground truth, modeled from a similarly sized real data set of 13 users. We compare our method to dual-threshold and the persistence 1D segmentation techniques and note challenges and opportunities for future improvements.

</details>


### [37] [Autark: A Serverless Toolkit for Prototyping Urban Visual Analytics Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20759)
*Lucas Alexandre,João Rulff,Talisson Souza,Gustavo Moreira,Daniel de Oliveira,Claudio Silva,Fabio Miranda,Marcos Lage*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Autark是一个用于城市视觉分析系统快速原型设计的无服务器工具包，通过领域感知抽象和自包含架构，将设计意图转化为可部署、可共享的系统，同时支持AI辅助编码工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市视觉分析系统开发传统上劳动密集，需要平衡设计方法和复杂软件工程实践。领域特定挑战包括异构数据流和依赖复杂多服务架构，阻碍快速开发、部署和可重复性。尽管城市视觉分析文献丰富，但缺乏将空间数据管理、分析处理和可视化等核心组件整合为统一轻量级框架的工具包。

Method: 提出Autark工具包，采用无服务器架构，提供领域感知抽象和自包含架构。通过结构化、紧密限定范围的接口，支持AI辅助编码工作流，使LLM能够基于明确定义的抽象生成更可靠的代码，而不是从头生成复杂解决方案。

Result: 贡献包括：(1) Autark工具包，用于城市视觉分析快速原型设计的无服务器架构；(2) 比较研究，评估使用和不使用Autark时LLM编码效果；(3) 一系列使用场景，展示其简化创建稳健、可共享城市视觉分析原型的能力。工具包可在https://autarkjs.org/获取。

Conclusion: Autark通过提供统一轻量级框架，显著简化城市视觉分析系统开发流程，使研究人员能够在数小时内从设计意图过渡到可部署、可共享的系统，同时通过结构化接口增强AI辅助编码的可靠性。

Abstract: The development of visual analytics (VA) systems has traditionally been a labor-intensive process, balancing design methodologies with complex software engineering practices. In domain-specific fields like urban VA, this challenge is amplified by heterogeneous data streams and a reliance on complex, multi-service architectures that hinder fast development, deployment, and reproducibility. Despite the richness of the urban VA literature, the field lacks a consolidated toolkit that encapsulates the core components of these systems, such as spatial data management, analytical processing, and visualization, into a unified, lightweight framework. In this paper, we introduce Autark, a serverless toolkit designed for the rapid prototyping of urban VA systems. Autark provides domain-aware abstractions through a self-contained architecture, enabling researchers to transition from design intention to deployed, shareable systems within hours. Furthermore, Autark's structured, tightly scoped interfaces make it well-suited for AI-assisted coding workflows, where LLMs produce more reliable code when composing from well-defined abstractions rather than generating complex solutions from scratch. Our contributions are: (1) the Autark toolkit, a serverless architecture for rapid prototyping of urban VA; (2) a comparative study of LLM coding effectiveness with and without Autark; and (3) a series of usage scenarios demonstrating its capability to streamline the creation of robust, shareable urban VA prototypes. Autark is available at https://autarkjs.org/.

</details>


### [38] [Designing a Visualization Atlas: Lessons & Reflections from The UK Co-Benefits Atlas for Climate Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20781)
*Jinrui Wang,Alexis Pister,Sian Phillips,Sarah Bissett,Ruaidhri Higgins-Lavery,Clare Wharmby,Andrew Sudmant,Uta Hinrichs,Benjamin Bach*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了英国协同效益地图集的设计过程，这是一个通过交互式可视化传播气候减缓数据的平台，探讨了地图集设计中的挑战和五个关键驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化地图集作为一种新兴平台，旨在通过交互式可视化和解释性内容使复杂主题的数据易于理解。然而，地图集设计面临超越传统可视化项目的挑战：需要应对多样且不确定的受众和使用场景，支持解释性和引导性探索，并适应复杂、不断演化的数据。

Method: 研究团队在10个月内进行了8次设计研讨会、迭代原型开发、15次利益相关者入门会议和持续反思。这些交织的过程包括可视化专家和领域专家的合作，开发了包含400多页可视化和解释内容的地图集。

Result: 开发了英国协同效益地图集，并深入理解了利益相关者在实践中如何批判性地参与地图集使用，包括他们的兴趣点、在浏览海量数据时可能遇到的摩擦，以及预期的使用场景。研究识别了地图集设计的五个驱动力：数据、人员、故事、上下文和地图集本身。

Conclusion: 基于案例研究，作者提出将这五个驱动力作为概念起点，用于构建和反思未来的地图集设计过程。这些驱动力在不同阶段以不同方式影响可视化地图集设计，为复杂数据平台的设计提供了框架性指导。

Abstract: This paper reports on the process of designing the UK Co-Benefits Atlas, which communicates and publicizes data for climate mitigation. Visualization atlases -- an emerging type of platform to make data about complex topics comprehensive through interactive visualizations and explanatory content -- pose challenges beyond traditional visualization projects. Atlases must address diverse and often uncertain audiences and use cases, support both explanatory and guided exploration, and accommodate complex, evolving data. Over 10 months, our team of visualization and domain experts conducted 8 design workshops, iterative prototyping, 15 stakeholder onboarding sessions, and continuous reflection. These intertwined processes informed the development of the Atlas, comprising over 400 pages of visualizations and explanations. They also enabled a deeper understanding of how stakeholders may critically engage with the atlas in practice, in terms of interests, potential frictions when navigating huge amounts of data, and envisioned usage scenarios. Reflecting on our design process, we identify five driving forces in atlas design -- data, people, stories, context, and the atlas itself -- whose shifting dynamics influence different stages of visualization atlas design in different ways. Grounded in our case study, we discuss using these forces as a conceptual starting point for structuring and reflecting on future atlas design processes.

</details>


### [39] [From Meme to Method: Rethinking Animal Adoption Platforms through the Cat Distribution System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20823)
*Carl Angelo Angcana,Jamlech Iram Gojo Cruz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于猫分配系统（CDS）互联网迷因开发的原型应用，通过算法匹配、社区报告和邻近发现等功能，使宠物领养过程更具偶然性而非交易性，在菲律宾流浪动物过多背景下重新构想领养平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菲律宾流浪猫狗数量庞大（约1311万只），全球过度繁殖排名第六，需要创新方式改进领养系统。CDS互联网迷因作为一种文化隐喻，反映了人们感知和参与领养的方式，为重新设计领养平台提供了新视角。

Method: 开发基于CDS原则的原型应用，包含算法匹配、社区报告和邻近发现等核心功能。对35名潜在用户进行初步评估，收集用户反馈。

Result: 用户对系统的易用性和符合直觉期望给予积极评价，但指出了匹配算法透明度和领养者-原主人沟通方面的改进空间。研究发现CDS等文化隐喻能够塑造用户心智模型，使领养过程感觉更偶然、更少交易性。

Conclusion: 文化嵌入的隐喻如CDS能够有效重塑用户对领养系统的认知，使过程更自然、更少交易性。这种基于文化共鸣的设计方法可为宠物领养平台提供有价值的改进方向。

Abstract: The internet folklore of the Cat Distribution System (CDS) humorously suggests that cats are "assigned" to people rather than intentionally sought. Beyond its playful origins, CDS reflects a culturally resonant way people perceive and engage in adoption, and this user context can guide the redesign and improvement of adoption systems. In the Philippines, where an estimated 13.11 million stray cats and dogs place the country sixth worldwide in overpopulation, this framing offers a novel way to rethink adoption platforms. We developed a prototype application inspired by CDS principles, focusing on features such as algorithmic matchmaking, community reporting, and proximity-based discovery. An initial evaluation with potential users (n=35) indicated that the system was positively received for its ease of use and its alignment with users' intuitive expectations, though participants highlighted areas for improvement in transparency of matchmaking and owner-adopter communication. The findings suggest that culturally embedded metaphors like CDS can shape mental models, making adoption processes feel more serendipitous and less transactional.

</details>
