### [40] [Learning Hippo: Multi-attractor Dynamics and Stability Effects in a Biologically Detailed CA3 Extension of Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20679)
*Daniele Corradetti,Renato Corradetti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 扩展的CA3海马模型在模式完成任务中表现出三个关键特征：多吸引子交叉种子行为、目标选择性联想回忆和降低的交叉种子方差，这些特征在最小Hopfield基线中不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个生物学上更详细的CA3海马自联想记忆模型，超越经典的Hopfield/Marr模型，以捕捉更复杂的神经计算特性。

Method: 开发了一个包含10个种群（2种非对称锥体细胞亚型，8种GABA能中间神经元类）、47个区室、多种可塑性规则（循环Hebb、BCM抗饱和、苔状纤维短期、内源性大麻素iLTD、爆发门控Hebb）以及双峰胆碱能编码/巩固循环的扩展模型。

Result: 在N=256规模下，完整架构表现出三个在最小Hopfield基线中不存在的定性特征：1）在K=5时具有生物学现实抑制比例的多吸引子交叉种子行为；2）在K≥5时对配对(A,B)记忆的目标选择性联想回忆；3）在干净上游条件下交叉种子方差低于最小基线。

Conclusion: 扩展的生物学详细CA3模型能够产生经典Hopfield模型无法捕捉的复杂计算特征，这些架构特异性特征在独立机制中一致出现，表明生物细节对神经计算功能的重要性。

Abstract: We present a biologically detailed extension of the classical Hopfield/Marr auto-associative memory model for CA3, implementing ten populations (two asymmetric pyramidal subtypes, eight GABAergic interneuron classes), forty-seven compartments, multi-rule plasticity (recurrent Hebb, BCM anti-saturation, mossy-fiber short-term, endocannabinoid iLTD, burst-gated Hebb), and a bimodal cholinergic encoding/consolidation cycle. Evaluated on pattern completion across auto-associative, associative, and temporal regimes, and on a controlled inhibitory-proportion manipulation at $N{=}256$, the full architecture exhibits \emph{three qualitative signatures absent from a minimal Hopfield baseline}: (i)~multi-attractor cross-seed behaviour at $K{=}5$ with biologically realistic inhibitory proportions, where two of five seeds converge to positive attractors with margin ${+}0.10{-}0.22$ (Cohen's $d{=}0.71$, one-sided $p{=}0.08$); (ii)~target-selective associative recall in paired $(A, B)$ memory at $K{\geq}5$, where the full model retrieves $B$ from a partial cue of $A$ while the minimal model echoes $A$ (Pearson margin $Δ{=}{+}0.163$ at $K{=}5$); (iii)~reduced cross-seed variance of the full model below the minimal baseline under clean upstream, with ratios $1.0{-}3.0$. These three signatures are architecture-specific: they appear consistently across independent regimes and are absent from the minimal control.

</details>

### [41] [Trust, Lies, and Long Memories: Emergent Social Dynamics and Reputation in Multi-Round Avalon with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20582)
*Suveen Ellawela*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM智能体在重复玩《抵抗组织：阿瓦隆》隐藏角色欺骗游戏时，通过保留跨游戏记忆，自发形成了可测量的声誉机制和策略性欺骗动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM智能体在重复社交互动中的涌现行为，特别是当智能体保留先前游戏记忆时，如何形成社会动态，这与以往只关注单次游戏表现的研究不同。

Method: 让LLM智能体重复玩《抵抗组织：阿瓦隆》隐藏角色欺骗游戏，智能体保留跨游戏记忆（包括角色分配和行为历史），共进行了188场游戏，并比较不同推理努力水平下的行为差异。

Result: 发现两个关键现象：1) 跨游戏记忆自发产生声誉动态，智能体会引用过去行为（如"我警惕重复上次过度信任早期成功的错误"），声誉具有角色条件性（同一智能体在扮演好人时被描述为"直率"，扮演坏人时被描述为"微妙"），高声誉玩家获得团队包含率增加46%；2) 高推理努力支持更策略性欺骗：邪恶玩家更常在早期任务中通过以建立信任，然后在后期任务中破坏，高努力游戏中这一比例为75%，低努力游戏中为36%。

Conclusion: 重复互动与记忆机制使LLM智能体产生了可测量的声誉和欺骗动态，表明LLM能够发展出复杂的社会行为模式，这对于理解多智能体系统中的社会动态具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study emergent social dynamics in LLM agents playing The Resistance: Avalon, a hidden-role deception game. Unlike prior work on single-game performance, our agents play repeated games while retaining memory of previous interactions, including who played which roles and how they behaved, enabling us to study how social dynamics evolve. Across 188 games, two key phenomena emerge. First, reputation dynamics emerge organically when agents retain cross-game memory: agents reference past behavior in statements like "I am wary of repeating last game's mistake of over-trusting early success." These reputations are role-conditional: the same agent is described as "straightforward" when playing good but "subtle" when playing evil, and high-reputation players receive 46% more team inclusions. Second, higher reasoning effort supports more strategic deception: evil players more often pass early missions to build trust before sabotaging later ones, 75% in high-effort games vs 36% in low-effort games. Together, these findings show that repeated interaction with memory gives rise to measurable reputation and deception dynamics among LLM agents.

</details>


### [42] [Anchor-and-Resume Concession Under Dynamic Pricing for LLM-Augmented Freight Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20732)
*Hoang Nguyen,Lu Wang,Marta Gaia Bras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出两阶段锚定-恢复框架，通过基于价差的自适应β参数实现动态定价调整，同时保证报价单调性，将LLM仅用作自然语言翻译层而非定价决策器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 货运经纪商在动态定价环境下需要处理大量承运商费率谈判，传统固定β参数的时间依赖让步框架无法适应模型实时更新目标价格的问题。基于价差推导β参数虽能适应变化，但可能导致报价回撤违反单调性。LLM驱动的经纪商虽有灵活性但成本高、非确定性且易受提示注入攻击。

Method: 提出两阶段锚定-恢复框架：1) 基于价差推导自适应β参数，将每个货物的利润结构映射到正确的让步姿态；2) 锚定-恢复机制保证在任意价格变动下报价单调非递减。所有定价决策保持确定性公式，LLM仅作为自然语言翻译层。

Result: 在115,125次谈判的实证评估中：窄价差时快速让步优先成交和货物覆盖；中宽价差时匹配或超越最佳固定β基线的经纪商节省。相比200亿参数无约束LLM经纪商，达到相似协议率和节省。对抗LLM驱动的承运商（更现实的随机对手），保持可比节省和更高协议率。通过解耦LLM与定价逻辑，框架可水平扩展到数千并发谈判，推理成本可忽略且决策透明。

Conclusion: 锚定-恢复框架解决了动态定价环境下的自适应性和单调性矛盾，通过确定性公式实现高效定价决策，将LLM限制为翻译层而非决策器，实现了可扩展性、成本效益和决策透明性。

Abstract: Freight brokerages negotiate thousands of carrier rates daily under dynamic pricing conditions where models frequently revise targets mid-conversation. Classical time-dependent concession frameworks use a fixed shape parameter $β$ that cannot adapt to these updates. Deriving $β$ from the live spread enables adaptation but introduces a new problem: a pricing shift can cause the formula to retract a previous offer, violating monotonicity. LLM-powered brokers offer flexibility but require expensive reasoning models, produce non-deterministic pricing, and remain vulnerable to prompt injection.
  We propose a two-index anchor-and-resume framework that addresses both limitations. A spread-derived $β$ maps each load's margin structure to the correct concession posture, while the anchor-and-resume mechanism guarantees monotonically non-decreasing offers under arbitrary pricing shifts. All pricing decisions remain in a deterministic formula; the LLM, when used, serves only as a natural-language translation layer. Empirical evaluation across 115,125 negotiations shows that the adaptive $β$ tailors behavior by regime: in narrow spreads, it concedes quickly to prioritize deal closure and load coverage; in medium and wide spreads, it matches or exceeds the best fixed-$β$ baselines in broker savings. Against an unconstrained 20-billion-parameter LLM broker, it achieves similar agreement rates and savings. Against LLM-powered carriers as more realistic stochastic counterparties, it maintains comparable savings and higher agreement rates than against rule-based opponents. By decoupling the LLM from pricing logic, the framework scales horizontally to thousands of concurrent negotiations with negligible inference cost and transparent decision-making.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [43] [Coding with Eyes: Visual Feedback Unlocks Reliable GUI Code Generating and Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19750)
*Zhilin Liu,Ye Huang,Ting Xie,Ruizhi Zhang,Wen Li,Lixin Duan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VF-Coder：基于视觉反馈的多智能体系统，通过感知视觉信息并直接与程序界面交互，解决GUI代码调试中视觉信息缺失的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的代码生成智能体主要依赖文本输出反馈进行多轮调试，难以处理涉及视觉信息的图形用户界面。存在两个主要限制：1）GUI程序是事件驱动的，现有方法无法模拟用户交互来触发GUI元素逻辑；2）GUI程序具有视觉属性，基于文本的方法难以评估渲染界面是否满足用户需求

Method: 提出VF-Coder，一个基于视觉反馈的多智能体系统，通过感知视觉信息并直接与程序界面交互来调试GUI代码。同时引入InteractGUI Bench基准，包含984个常用真实桌面GUI应用任务，用于细粒度评估交互逻辑和视觉结构

Result: 在InteractGUI Bench上，VF-Coder将Gemini-3-Flash的成功率从21.68%提升到28.29%，视觉得分从0.4284提升到0.5584，证明了视觉反馈在GUI调试中的有效性

Conclusion: 视觉反馈对于GUI代码调试至关重要，VF-Coder通过模拟人类交互方式识别逻辑和布局问题，显著提升了GUI代码生成和调试的性能

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents have shown remarkable progress in code generation. However, current agent methods mainly rely on text-output-based feedback (e.g. command-line outputs) for multi-round debugging and struggle in graphical user interface (GUI) that involve visual information. This is mainly due to two limitations: 1) GUI programs are event-driven, yet existing methods cannot simulate user interactions to trigger GUI element logic 2) GUI programs possess visual attributes, making it difficult for text-based approaches to assess whether the rendered interface meets user needs. To systematically address these challenges, we first introduce InteractGUI Bench, a novel benchmark comprising 984 commonly used real-world desktop GUI application tasks designed for fine-grained evaluation of both interaction logic and visual structure. Furthermore, we propose VF-Coder, a vision-feedback-based multi-agent system for debugging GUI code. By perceiving visual information and directly interacting with program interfaces, VF-Coder can identify potential logic and layout issues in a human-like manner. On InteractGUI Bench, our VF-Coder approach increases the success rate of Gemini-3-Flash from 21.68% to 28.29% and raises the visual score from 0.4284 to 0.5584, indicating the effectiveness of visual feedback in GUI debugging.

</details>


### [44] [KnowPilot: Your Knowledge-Driven Copilot for Domain Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19820)
*Zekun Xi,Yichen Nie,Ziyan Jiang,Yujie Bao,Zhenqian Xu,Zhisong Qiu,Ziwen Xu,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KnowPilot是一个领域特定知识增强的生成式智能体系统，通过整合任务先验、显性知识和经验知识来提升专业应用中的智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式智能体在现实工业场景中部署时常因缺乏领域特定知识而面临挑战，需要填补这一空白。

Method: KnowPilot是一个开源框架，整合任务特定先验、显性知识和经验知识，结合结构化知识库检索和通过人机交互捕获专家经验的记忆系统，以领域特定文本生成为代表案例。

Result: 实验结果表明KnowPilot在领域导向文本生成中取得优越性能，适用于医疗、金融和工业等多个领域。

Conclusion: KnowPilot通过知识增强方法有效解决了生成式智能体在专业领域应用中的知识不足问题，支持私有部署和专家知识积累。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of generative agents, their deployment in real-world industry scenarios often encounters significant challenges due to a lack of domain-specific knowledge. To address this gap, we present KnowPilot: a Domain-Specific Knowledge Augmented Generative Agent System. KnowPilot is an open-source framework that integrates task-specific priors, explicit knowledge, and experiential knowledge to enhance agent performance in specialized applications. It combines knowledge retrieval from structured repositories with a memory system capable of capturing expert experience through human AI interaction. Taking domain-specific writing generation as a representative case, KnowPilot enables private deployment, supports injection of task requirements, loads private knowledge bases, and stores tacit expert knowledge as persistent memory. Experimental results demonstrate that KnowPilot achieves superior performance in domain-oriented text generation and is applicable across fields such as medicine, finance and industry.

</details>


### [45] [Statistical Software Engineering with Tuned Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19822)
*Nimrod Busany*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AI系统维护的核心不是代码+配置，而是版本化治理的程序空间，包含领域、结构约束、资格、评估资产和统计发布门控，强调在动态环境中将模型选择、检索策略等视为可调变量进行治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在动态环境中运行：提供商模型和API变化、输入分布漂移、评估集老化、质量/成本/延迟/安全等目标随时间重新协商。实践中团队常通过临时调整模型选择、检索策略、提示结构等应对，但固定分配推理不足，因为选择的有效性依赖于环境、评估集和政策状态。

Method: 将AI系统中的选择（模型选择、检索策略、提示结构、操作阈值）视为可调变量，在环境和评估集演化过程中进行治理维护。基于SE4AI工作和先前治理调优研究，将治理空间定位为软件工程对象，采用统计方法（采样评估集、估计证据、效应大小边界、置信/风险阈值）进行发布决策。

Result: 提出了将AI系统维护从传统代码+配置转向版本化治理程序空间的新范式，强调在动态环境中通过统计门控机制管理可调变量，为AI系统的工程化维护提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: AI系统维护的核心应该是版本化治理的程序空间而非静态代码，在动态环境中将关键选择作为可调变量进行治理，采用统计方法进行发布决策，这代表了AI系统软件工程的新方向。

Abstract: The maintained artifact in an AI-enabled system is not code plus settings, but a versioned governed program space: domains, structural constraints, eligibility, evaluation assets, and a statistical release gate. AI-enabled systems operate under changing world conditions: provider models and APIs change, input distributions drift, evaluation sets age, and objectives such as quality, cost, latency, and safety are renegotiated over time. In practice, teams often respond through ad hoc changes to model choice, retrieval policy, prompt structure, and operational thresholds. Fixed-assignment reasoning is therefore insufficient: a chosen assignment is valid only relative to an environment, evaluation set, and policy state. We argue that such choices should be treated as tuned variables: program variables maintained under governance as environments and evaluation sets evolve. Building on SE4AI work and our prior work on governed tuning, this paper positions the governed space as the software-engineering object. Here, statistical means that promotion relies on sampled evaluation sets, estimated evidence, effect-size margins, and confidence/risk thresholds.

</details>


### [46] [Stateful Embedded Fuzzing with Peripheral-Accurate SystemC Virtual Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19824)
*Chiara Ghinami,Igor Pontes Tresolavy,Luis Seibt,Nils Bosbach,Rainer Leupers*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个集成AFL++与状态感知SystemC-TLM虚拟原型的新框架，用于嵌入式软件的真实模糊测试，消除误报并保持代码覆盖率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式软件复杂度增加使得手动测试不切实际，现有模糊测试方法在嵌入式环境中存在局限性：快速用户模式模拟器牺牲外设真实性，全系统模拟器需要手动插装且难以扩展到大规模软件

Method: 开发一个集成AFL++与状态感知SystemC-TLM虚拟原型的新框架，模糊测试生成的输入直接注入外设模型，允许外设触发自然副作用（如中断和FIFO更新），实现全系统模拟与模糊测试的结合

Result: 在嵌入式工作负载上的结果显示，该方法消除了误报，同时保持了与最先进工具相当的代码覆盖率和执行性能

Conclusion: 通过将模糊测试与全系统模拟集成，该框架提升了嵌入式系统硅前测试的有效性，为嵌入式软件测试提供了更真实的测试环境

Abstract: The increasing complexity of embedded software has made comprehensive manual testing impractical, motivating the use of automated techniques such as fuzzing. Coverage-guided fuzzers like AFL++ have shown strong results for conventional software but remain challenging to apply effectively in embedded contexts, where peripheral behaviors play critical roles. Existing approaches either use fast user-mode simulators, sacrificing peripheral realism, or rely on full-system simulators with manual instrumentation, limiting applicability to large-scale software. In this work, we present a novel framework that integrates AFL++ with a stateful SystemC-TLM virtual prototype to enable realistic fuzzing of embedded software. Fuzzer-generated inputs are injected directly into peripheral models, allowing peripherals to trigger natural side effects such as interrupts and FIFO updates. By integrating fuzzing with full-system simulation, our framework advances the effectiveness of pre-silicon testing for embedded systems. Results on embedded workloads show that our approach eliminates false positives while maintaining comparable code coverage and execution performance as state-of-the-art tools.

</details>


### [47] [SolidCoder: Bridging the Mental-Reality Gap in LLM Code Generation through Concrete Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19825)
*Woojin Lee,Jin-Xia Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SolidCoder提出S.O.L.I.D.架构解决代码生成中的"心理-现实鸿沟"，通过强制边缘情况意识和沙箱执行验证，在多个基准测试中实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成框架依赖心理模拟（LLMs内部追踪执行来验证正确性），但存在"心理-现实鸿沟"的根本限制：模型会幻觉执行轨迹并自信地验证有缺陷的代码。这一鸿沟体现在两个正交维度：规范鸿沟（规划时忽略边缘情况）和验证鸿沟（对有缺陷的代码幻觉出正确行为）。

Method: 提出SolidCoder，基于"不要想象——执行"的简单原则。S.O.L.I.D.架构通过两个关键组件解决两个鸿沟维度：1）在算法设计前强制边缘情况意识；2）用基于属性的沙箱执行替代想象的执行轨迹。具体使用属性预言机进行沙箱执行验证。

Result: 使用GPT-4o时，SolidCoder在多个基准测试中达到最先进的pass@1性能：HumanEval上95.7%（+0.6%p），CodeContests上77.0%（+4.3%p），APPS上26.7%（+3.4%p）。消融实验显示边缘情况意识提供最大的个体增益，而执行基础能捕获规范改进无法解决的不同类别错误。这些增益可推广到RL后训练模型。

Conclusion: 填补心理-现实鸿沟的两个维度对于稳健的代码合成至关重要。SolidCoder通过强制边缘情况意识和沙箱执行验证，显著提高了代码生成的质量和可靠性。研究释放了代码和框架以促进未来研究。

Abstract: State-of-the-art code generation frameworks rely on mental simulation, where LLMs internally trace execution to verify correctness. We expose a fundamental limitation: the Mental-Reality Gap -- where models hallucinate execution traces and confidently validate buggy code. This gap manifests along two orthogonal dimensions: the Specification Gap (overlooking edge cases during planning) and the Verification Gap (hallucinating correct behavior for flawed code). We propose SolidCoder with a simple principle: don't imagine -- execute. The S.O.L.I.D. architecture addresses both dimensions by forcing edge-case awareness before algorithm design and replacing imagined traces with sandboxed execution using property-based oracles. With GPT-4o, SolidCoder achieves state-of-the-art pass@1 performance: 95.7% on HumanEval (+0.6%p), 77.0% on CodeContests (+4.3%p), and 26.7% on APPS (+3.4%p). Ablation reveals that edge-case awareness provides the largest individual gain, while execution grounding catches categorically different errors that specification improvements cannot address. These gains generalize to RL post-trained models, validating that bridging both gap dimensions is essential for robust code synthesis. We release our code and framework to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [48] [Co-Located Tests, Better AI Code: How Test Syntax Structure Affects Foundation Model Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19826)
*Éric Jacopin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究AI代码生成中测试代码结构（内联测试vs分离测试）对生成质量的影响，发现内联测试能显著提升代码正确性和一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码助手在生成代码时同时生成测试代码，开发者面临测试代码结构的选择问题。传统上这是测试哲学问题，但研究需要探究这种选择是否影响AI代码生成质量

Method: 使用SEGA三维评估框架（确定性、保持性、正确性），对12个模型、3个提供商进行大规模实证研究（830+生成文件），比较Python doctests（内联测试）和Rust #[test] blocks（分离测试）在d-ary堆实现上的表现。进行机制分析，包括对7个开源架构的注意力分析和因果验证实验

Result: 1) 内联测试在所有模型中实现近乎完美的保持性（100%）和正确性（92-100%）；2) 分离测试暴露模型层级差距（0-100%正确性），且保持性与正确性独立；3) 模型行为随代际演变，一个模型打破了其三个前代的测试抑制模式；4) 机制分析显示内联测试标记在5/7模型中接收2.8-4.4倍更强的注意力，因果验证证实共定位机制扩展到非Transformer架构

Conclusion: 在基础模型时代，测试语法结构成为软件设计关注点：将测试与实现代码共定位能产生可测量更好的AI生成代码。该效应受模型能力和编程语言限制

Abstract: AI coding assistants increasingly generate code alongside tests. How developers structure test code, whether inline with the implementation or in separate blocks, has traditionally been a matter of testing philosophy. We investigate whether this choice affects AI code generation quality.
  We conduct a large-scale empirical study (830+ generated files, 12 models, 3 providers) using SEGA, a three-dimensional evaluation framework measuring Determinism, Preservation, and Correctness. Comparing inline test syntax (Python doctests) against separated test syntax (Rust #[test] blocks) on a d-ary heap implementation, we find that: (1) inline tests yield near-perfect preservation (100%) and correctness (92-100%) across all models; (2) separated tests expose stark model-tier gaps (0-100% correctness) and independence between preservation and correctness; (3) model behavior evolves across generations, and notably one model breaks the test suppression pattern of its three predecessors; (4) mechanistic analysis on 7 open-source architectures (6 transformers and a gated-linear Recurrent Neural Network (RNN)) reveals inline test markers receive 2.8-4.4$\times$ stronger attention in 5/7 models, with causal validation via knockout and steering experiments on the 4 code-specialized transformers and RWKV-6; the co-location mechanism extends to a non-transformer architecture, suggesting the design recommendation is robust to future architectural shifts. In the Foundation Model era, test syntax structure is a software design concern: co-locating tests with implementation code produces measurably better AI-generated code. This arxiv long version includes appendices that further qualify the effect as bounded by both model capability and programming language.

</details>


### [49] [More Is Different: Toward a Theory of Emergence in AI-Native Software Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19827)
*Daniel Russo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出将AI原生软件生态系统视为复杂适应系统（CAS），而非传统软件架构，以解释多智能体AI系统中出现的不可预测故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件工程理论无法解释多智能体AI系统的故障模式：虽然单个智能体运行正确，但它们的交互会导致整个生态系统退化，这表明我们对软件演化的理解存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 将Holland的六个CAS特性映射到可观察的生态系统动态中；定义微观状态变量、粗粒度函数和可处理的测量框架来量化因果涌现；提出七个可证伪命题，将CAS理论与软件演化联系起来。

Result: 提出了一个理论框架，将AI原生软件生态系统重新概念化为复杂适应系统，识别了架构熵、级联故障和理解债务等涌现特性，并挑战了Lehman定律中基于智能体假设的局限性。

Conclusion: 如果该理论被证实，将需要根本性转变：将生态系统级监控作为AI原生系统的主要治理机制；如果被证伪，现有理论可能只需增量更新。无论如何，这项工作迫使软件工程重新审视其核心假设在自主智能体时代的适用性。

Abstract: Software engineering faces a fundamental challenge: multi-agent AI systems fail in ways that defy explanation by traditional theories. While individual agents perform correctly, their interactions degrade entire ecosystems, revealing a gap in our understanding of software evolution. This paper argues that AI-native software ecosystems must be studied as complex adaptive systems (CAS), where emergent properties like architectural entropy, cascade failures, and comprehension debt arise not from individual components, but from their interactions. We map Holland's six CAS properties onto observable ecosystem dynamics, distinguishing these systems from microservices or open-source networks. To measure causal emergence, we define micro-level state variables, coarse-graining functions, and a tractable measurement framework. Seven falsifiable propositions link CAS theory to software evolution, challenging or extending Lehman's laws where agent-level assumptions fail. If confirmed, these findings would demand a radical shift: ecosystem-level monitoring as the primary governance mechanism for AI-native systems. If refuted, existing theories may only need incremental updates. Either way, this work forces us to ask: Can software engineering's core assumptions survive the age of autonomous agents?

</details>


### [50] [ViBR: Automated Bug Replay from Video-based Reports using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19905)
*Sidong Feng,Dingbang Wang,Nikola Tomic,Tingting Yu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ViBR：一种轻量级全自动方法，直接从GUI录屏视频中复现bug，结合CLIP嵌入相似性进行动作边界分割，以及视觉语言模型进行区域感知GUI状态比较和引导式bug重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI录屏视频作为bug报告工具越来越流行，但现有方法依赖脆弱的图像处理启发式、显式触摸指示器或预构建的UI转换图，需要复杂的插桩和特定应用设置，自动复现bug仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: ViBR结合CLIP嵌入相似性进行动作边界分割，使用视觉语言模型进行区域感知GUI状态比较和引导式bug重放。该方法轻量级、全自动化，无需复杂插桩或应用特定设置。

Result: 实验结果显示，ViBR成功复现了72%的bug录屏，显著优于最先进的基线方法和消融变体。

Conclusion: ViBR提供了一种有效且实用的方法，能够直接从GUI录屏视频中自动复现bug，解决了现有方法的局限性，在bug复现任务上表现出色。

Abstract: Bug reports play a critical role in software maintenance by helping users convey encountered issues to developers. Recently, GUI screen capture videos have gained popularity as a bug reporting artifact due to their ease of use and ability to retain rich contextual information. However, automatically reproducing bugs from such recordings remains a significant challenge. Existing methods often rely on fragile image-processing heuristics, explicit touch indicators, or pre-constructed UI transition graphs, which require non-trivial instrumentation and app-specific setup. This paper presents ViBR, a lightweight and fully automated approach that reproduces bugs directly from GUI recordings. Specifically, ViBR combines CLIP-based embedding similarity for action boundary segmentation with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for region-aware GUI state comparison and guided bug replay. Experimental results show that ViBR successfully reproduces 72% of bug recordings, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and ablation variants.

</details>


### [51] [Automated Quantum Software and AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19970)
*Nazanin Siavash,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对量子软件工程和量子人工智能中（半）自动化方法的系统性文献综述，重点关注量子与混合量子-经典应用中的自动化趋势与未来方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算领域专家稀缺，自动化方法对提升软件工程和人工智能在量子应用中的效率至关重要，特别是在混合量子-经典应用中，自动化能帮助决策部署策略

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，分析量子软件工程和量子人工智能中的（半）自动化方法，识别最新趋势和未来研究方向

Result: 识别了量子与混合量子-经典应用中自动化方法的关键趋势，为更广泛的量子AI软件系统开发部署提供了方法和技术洞察

Conclusion: 自动化在量子软件工程和量子人工智能中具有关键作用，特别是在专家资源有限的量子计算领域，系统性综述为未来研究和实践提供了方向性指导

Abstract: In this paper, we conduct a systematic literature review of (semi-) automated approaches to Quantum Software Engineering (QSE) and Quantum Artificial Intelligence (QAI). Prior work in the literature indicated that both Software Engineering (SE) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) practices may become more efficient by using (semi-) automated approaches. This also holds in the Quantum Computing (QC), Quantum Information Science (QIS), and Quantum Engineering (QE) world, as well as in hybrid quantum-classical applications. In fact, automation is even more crucial in such cases since there is a limited number of developers and AI experts (e.g., data scientists) who possess the required knowledge and skills in QC. Moreover, in hybrid setups, automation may help decide what part of the application should be deployed on quantum hardware and on which of the available quantum platforms, if applicable. This can be a significant help to achieve productivity leap and efficiency even for subject matter experts. Unlike prior literature reviews and surveys, this work focuses on automation in SE and AI for quantum and hybrid quantum-classical applications and identifies the recent trends and future directions through a systematic literature review. We are interested in methods and techniques that can enable a broader development and deployment of quantum and hybrid AI-enabled software systems.

</details>


### [52] [FIKA: Expanding Dependency Reachability with Executability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20015)
*Yogya Gamage,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Martin Monperrus,Benoit Baudry*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FIKA是一个用于提供第三方库调用点可执行性保证的管道，通过生成可执行代码并分析执行轨迹来验证调用点的实际可达性，相比现有测试套件能提高20%的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖可达性分析工具大多是静态的，缺乏执行验证，导致结果精度有限。需要一种能够提供第三方库调用点可执行性保证的方法，以提高依赖管理的精确性。

Method: 提出FIKA管道，通过生成可执行代码并分析执行轨迹来验证第三方库调用点的实际可达性。该方法基于代码执行而非静态分析，为调用点提供可执行性证据。

Result: 在8个Java项目数据集上评估，现有测试套件平均覆盖54%的调用点，FIKA进一步提高20%的覆盖率，能够证明2363个依赖方法的可执行性。在6/8的项目中，FIKA为超过75%的调用点提供了强可执行性保证。FIKA还能改进Semgrep等静态漏洞可达性分析工具的结果，帮助优先处理具有更强可执行性保证的漏洞更新。

Conclusion: FIKA通过执行验证为第三方库调用点提供可执行性保证，显著提高了依赖可达性分析的精确性，能够有效补充现有测试套件和静态分析工具的不足，为依赖管理提供更可靠的决策依据。

Abstract: Automated third-party library analysis tools help developers by addressing key dependency management challenges, such as automating version updates, detecting vulnerabilities, and detecting breaking updates. Dependency reachability analysis aims at improving the precision of dependency management, by reducing the space of dependency issues to the ones that actually matter. Most tools for dependency reachability analysis are static and fundamentally limited by the absence of execution. In this paper, we propose FIKA, a pipeline for providing guarantees of executability for third-party library call sites. FIKA generates code that is executed, and whose execution trace provides guarantees that a third-party library call site is actually reachable. We apply our approach to a dataset of eight Java projects to empirically evaluate the effectiveness of FIKA. On average, 54% of these call sites are covered by the existing test suites, and therefore, have evidence for their executability. FIKA further improves this coverage by 20% and is able to demonstrate executability for 2363 dependency methods. In six out of eight projects, FIKA provides strong guarantees that more than 75% of call sites are executable. We further demonstrate that FIKA is capable of improving the results provided by Semgrep, a state-of-the-art static vulnerability reachability analysis tool. We show that FIKA can help prioritize the vulnerability updates with stronger guarantees of executability in cases where Semgrep yields inconclusive reachability results.

</details>


### [53] [Hallucination Inspector: A Fact-Checking Judge for API Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20202)
*Marcos Tileria,Santanu Kumar Dash,Profir-Petru Pârţachi,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Hallucination Inspector工具，用于检测LLM在API迁移任务中产生的Scaffolding Hallucination问题，即模型生成不存在的API符号（Phantom Symbols）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动化软件工程任务（如API迁移）中经常产生Scaffolding Hallucination问题，即生成不存在的API符号（如虚构的导入、构造函数、常量），而标准指标无法可靠检测这种幻觉

Method: 提出Hallucination Inspector静态分析工具，通过轻量级评估框架验证从抽象语法树提取的符号是否与直接从软件文档构建的知识库匹配

Result: 在Android API迁移的初步评估中，该方法成功识别幻觉，并显著减少了与标准指标和概率判断器相比的误报

Conclusion: 需要专门工具检测LLM在代码生成中的Scaffolding Hallucination问题，Hallucination Inspector提供了一种有效的静态分析方法，优于传统指标

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in automated software engineering for tasks such as API migration. While LLMs are able to identify migration patterns, they often make mistakes and fail to produce correct glue code to invoke the new API in place of the old one. We call this issue Scaffolding Hallucination, a failure mode where models generate incorrect calling contexts by inventing Phantom Symbols -- such as imaginary imports, constructors, and constants -- that do not exist in the API specification. In this paper, we show that standard metrics cannot be relied upon to detect these instances of hallucination. We propose Hallucination Inspector, a static analysis tool to detect Scaffolding Hallucination in LLM-generated code. Our approach includes a lightweight evaluation framework that verifies symbols extracted from the abstract syntax tree against a knowledge base derived directly from software documentation for the API. A preliminary evaluation on Android API migrations demonstrates that our approach successfully identifies hallucinations and significantly reduces false positives compared to standard metrics and probabilistic judges

</details>


### [54] [Towards Secure Logging: Characterizing and Benchmarking Logging Code Security Issues with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20211)
*He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Kundi Yao,An Ran Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了日志代码的安全问题，构建了包含4类10种模式的分类法，创建了包含101个真实问题的基准数据集，并评估了LLM在检测和修复日志安全问题上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志代码在软件系统中至关重要，但不安全的日志实践可能暴露敏感信息或引发攻击。现有研究对日志代码安全问题的系统性分析有限，特别是在利用LLM进行检测和修复方面。

Method: 1) 推导了包含4个常见类别和10种对应模式的日志代码安全问题分类法；2) 构建了包含101个真实世界日志安全问题报告的手动审查和标注基准数据集；3) 提出了一个结合多种上下文知识的自动化框架来评估LLM在检测和修复日志安全问题上的能力。

Result: 实验结果显示性能存在显著差异：LLM在检测安全问题方面表现中等（平均准确率从12.9%到52.5%），但在可靠生成正确代码修复方面面临明显挑战。还发现仅问题描述比安全模式解释或两者结合更能提高LLM的检测准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果为从业者提供了可操作的见解，并突出了当前LLM在安全日志记录方面的潜力和局限性。LLM在检测方面有一定效果，但在修复方面仍面临挑战，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Logging code plays an important role in software systems by recording key events and behaviors, which are essential for debugging and monitoring. However, insecure logging practices can inadvertently expose sensitive information or enable attacks such as log injection, posing serious threats to system security and privacy. Prior research has examined general defects in logging code, but systematic analysis of logging code security issues remains limited, particularly in leveraging LLMs for detection and repair. In this paper, we derive a comprehensive taxonomy of logging code security issues, encompassing four common issue categories and 10 corresponding patterns. We further construct a benchmark dataset with 101 real-world logging security issue reports that have been manually reviewed and annotated. We then propose an automated framework that incorporates various contextual knowledge to evaluate LLMs' capabilities in detecting and repairing logging security issues. Our experimental results reveal a notable disparity in performance: while LLMs are moderately effective at detecting security issues (e.g., the accuracy ranges from 12.9% to 52.5% on average), they face noticeable challenges in reliably generating correct code repairs. We also find that the issue description alone improves the LLMs' detection accuracy more than the security pattern explanation or a combination of both. Overall, our findings provide actionable insights for practitioners and highlight the potential and limitations of current LLMs for secure logging.

</details>


### [55] [Shift-Up: A Framework for Software Engineering Guardrails in AI-native Software Development -- Initial Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20436)
*Petrus Lipsanen,Liisa Rannikko,François Christophe,Konsta Kalliokoski,Vlad Stirbu,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Shift-Up框架将传统软件工程实践（BDD、C4、ADRs）重新解释为生成式AI原生开发的结构性护栏，以解决vibe coding中的架构漂移、可追溯性差和可维护性低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在重塑软件工程，使开发从手动编码转向智能体驱动实现。然而，vibe coding虽然能快速原型开发，但常常存在架构漂移、有限的可追溯性和降低的可维护性问题。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，提出Shift-Up框架，将可执行需求（BDD）、架构建模（C4）和架构决策记录（ADRs）等成熟的软件工程实践重新解释为生成式AI原生开发的结构性护栏。

Result: 初步探索性评估比较了无结构的vibe coding、结构化提示工程和Shift-Up方法在Web应用开发中的表现。结果表明，嵌入机器可读需求和架构工件能稳定智能体行为、减少实现漂移，并将人类努力转向更高层次的设计和验证活动。

Conclusion: 传统软件工程工件可以作为AI辅助开发的有效控制机制，Shift-Up框架为解决生成式AI驱动开发中的架构和质量问题提供了结构化解决方案。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is reshaping software engineering by shifting development from manual coding toward agent-driven implementation. While vibe coding promises rapid prototyping, it often suffers from architectural drift, limited traceability, and reduced maintainability. Applying the design science research (DSR) methodology, this paper proposes Shift-Up, a framework that reinterprets established software engineering practices, like executable requirements (BDD), architectural modeling (C4), and architecture decision records (ADRs), as structural guardrails for GenAI-native development. Preliminary findings from our exploratory evaluation compare unstructured vibe coding, structured prompt engineering, and the Shift-Up approach in the development of a web application. These findings indicate that embedding machine-readable requirements and architectural artifacts stabilizes agent behavior, reduces implementation drift, and shifts human effort toward higher-level design and validation activities. The results suggest that traditional software engineering artifacts can serve as effective control mechanisms in AI-assisted development.

</details>


### [56] [On the Informativeness of Security Commit Messages: A Large-scale Replication Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20461)
*Syful Islam,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究独立复制并扩展了Reis等人(2023)关于安全相关提交信息信息量的研究，确认了原始发现：安全相关提交信息普遍信息量不足，且随时间推移情况恶化，不同软件生态系统间存在显著差异，符合Conventional Commits规范的提交信息反而信息量更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Reis等人(2023)关于安全相关提交信息信息量不足的研究结果的稳健性，并扩展分析以了解不同软件生态系统间的差异、时间趋势以及新兴最佳实践（如Conventional Commits规范）对提交信息质量的影响。

Method: 独立复制原始研究，不使用原始数据或分析管道，重新收集50,673个安全相关提交，使用独立重新实现的技术分析信息量。扩展研究包括：延长分析时间范围（至2025年10月），按软件生态系统（Linux内核、Ubuntu、Go、PyPI等）分解结果，以及评估Conventional Commits规范等新兴最佳实践的影响。

Result: 复制研究在统计上显著地确认了原始发现：安全相关提交信息普遍信息量不足。扩展分析显示：随时间推移提交信息信息量在恶化；不同软件生态系统间存在显著的信息量差异；令人意外的是，符合Conventional Commits规范的提交信息比不符合的信息量更低。

Conclusion: 安全相关提交信息的信息量问题普遍存在且日益严重，需要跨生态系统分析以理解平台和社区特定的提交信息实践，并指导制定和采用适用于所有软件生态系统的信息量安全相关提交信息编写指南。

Abstract: The informativeness of security-related commit messages is crucial for patch triage: when high, it enables the rapid distribution and deployment of security fixes. Prior research (Reis et al., 2023) reported, however, that commit messages are often too uninformative to support these activities. To assess the robustness of this negative result, we independently replicate the original study using only the information provided in the paper, without reusing any of the original artifacts (data, analysis pipeline, etc.). We retrieve \num{50673} security-related commits and analyze their informativeness using an independent re-implementation of the techniques introduced by Reis et al. For the same source (i.e., GitHub) and time period (from June 1999 to August 2022) as the original study, our replication confirms the original findings in a statistically significant way: security-related commit messages are, in general, not informative enough for security-focused purposes. We then extend the original study in several ways. Over a longer time period (from June 1999 to October 2025), we find that commit-message informativeness is worsening. Breaking results down by software ecosystem (Linux kernel, Ubuntu, Go, PyPI, etc.), we observe significant differences in informativeness. Finally, we examine emerging best practices for writing commit messages, such as the Conventional Commits Specification (CCS), and again find significant differences in an unexpected direction: CCS-compliant commits are less informative than non-compliant ones. Our findings highlight the need for cross-ecosystem analyses to understand platform- and community-specific commit-message practices, and to inform the development and adoption of universally applicable guidelines for writing informative security-related commit messages.

</details>


### [57] [Finding Duplicates in 1.1M BDD Steps: cukereuse, a Paraphrase-Robust Static Detector for Cucumber and Gherkin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20462)
*Ali Hassaan Mughal,Noor Fatima,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发了cukereuse工具，用于静态检测BDD测试套件中的步骤文本重复，结合哈希、编辑距离和语义嵌入技术，在公开GitHub仓库数据集上验证了高重复率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BDD重复检测方法需要运行测试或局限于单个组织，缺乏静态、抗释义、步骤级别且适用于任何仓库的检测工具。

Method: 开发cukereuse开源Python CLI工具，采用分层管道结合精确哈希、Levenshtein比率和句子转换器嵌入；构建包含347个GitHub仓库、23,667个.feature文件、1,113,616个Gherkin步骤的语料库；通过人工标注1,020对样本进行验证。

Result: 步骤加权精确重复率为80.2%；仓库中位数重复率为58.6%；最大混合聚类包含20.7k次出现、2.2k个文件；在无分数标注下F1=0.822，主标注方案下语义F1=0.906；基线方法F1分别为0.761和0.799。

Conclusion: 填补了静态、抗释义BDD步骤重复检测工具的空白；揭示了Gherkin语言在认知维度框架下8/14个维度存在问题；工具、语料库和标注数据已开源发布。

Abstract: Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) suites accumulate step-text duplication whose
  maintenance cost is established in prior work. Existing detection techniques require
  running the tests (Binamungu et al., 2018-2023) or are confined to a single
  organisation (Irshad et al., 2020-2022), leaving a gap: a purely static,
  paraphrase-robust, step-level detector usable on any repository. We fill the gap
  with cukereuse, an open-source Python CLI combining exact hashing, Levenshtein
  ratio, and sentence-transformer embeddings in a layered pipeline, released alongside
  an empirical corpus of 347 public GitHub repositories, 23,667 parsed .feature
  files, and 1,113,616 Gherkin steps. The step-weighted exact-duplicate rate is 80.2
  %; the median-repository rate is 58.6 % (Spearman rho = 0.51 with size). The top
  hybrid cluster groups 20.7k occurrences across 2.2k files. Against 1,020 pairs
  manually labelled by the three authors under a released rubric (inter-annotator
  Fleiss' kappa = 0.84 on a 60-pair overlap), we report precision, recall, and F1 with
  bootstrap 95 % CIs under two protocols: the primary rubric and a score-free
  second-pass relabelling. The strongest honest pair-level number is near-exact at F1
  = 0.822 on score-free labels; the primary-rubric semantic F1 = 0.906 is inflated by
  a stratification artefact that pins recall at 1.000. Lexical baselines
  (SourcererCC-style, NiCad-style) reach primary F1 = 0.761 and 0.799. The paper also
  presents a CDN-structured critique of Gherkin (Cognitive Dimensions of Notations);
  eight of fourteen dimensions are rated problematic or unsupported. The tool, corpus,
  labelled pairs, rubric, and pipeline are released under permissive licences.

</details>


### [58] [Early-Stage Product Line Validation Using LLMs: A Study on Semi-Formal Blueprint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20523)
*Viet-Man Le,Thi Ngoc Trang Tran,Sebastian Lubos,Alexander Felfernig,Damian Garber*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMs可直接在半正式文本蓝图上执行特征模型分析操作，实现软件产品线早期验证，推理优化模型准确率达88-89%接近求解器水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型是否能在半正式文本蓝图上直接执行特征模型分析操作，实现软件产品线范围界定的早期验证，避免传统形式化方法的前期建模成本

Method: 使用12个先进LLM和16个标准分析操作，在文本蓝图上进行测试，与基于求解器的FLAMA基准系统进行对比，评估模型在结构解析和约束推理方面的表现

Result: 推理优化模型（如Grok 4 Fast Reasoning、Gemini 2.5 Pro）在所有评估蓝图和操作上的平均准确率达到88-89%，接近求解器正确性；识别出结构解析和约束推理中的系统性错误，并揭示了准确性与成本的权衡关系

Conclusion: LLMs可作为轻量级辅助工具用于早期可变性验证，在软件产品线范围界定中提供有效的早期分析能力，但需注意模型选择和成本效益平衡

Abstract: We study whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can perform feature model analysis operations (AOs) directly on semi-formal textual blueprints, i.e., concise constrained-language descriptions of feature hierarchies and constraints, enabling early validation in Software Product Line scoping. Using 12 state-of-the-art LLMs and 16 standard AOs, we compare their outputs against the solver-based oracle FLAMA. Results show that reasoning-optimized models (e.g., Grok 4 Fast Reasoning, Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieve 88-89% average accuracy across all evaluated blueprints and operations, approaching solver correctness. We identify systematic errors in structural parsing and constraint reasoning, and highlight accuracy-cost trade-offs that inform model selection. These findings position LLMs as lightweight assistants for early variability validation.

</details>


### [59] [DeepParse: Hybrid Log Parsing with LLM-Synthesized Regex Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20553)
*Amir Shetaia,Sean Kauffman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeepParse：一种混合日志解析框架，利用LLM从小样本中挖掘可重用变量模式，然后通过Drain算法确定性应用，实现高精度、可扩展且成本高效的日志结构化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式系统产生海量异构日志，但日志解析面临格式演进和标注数据有限的挑战。基于机器学习的解析器（如Drain）速度快但复杂变量精度不足，而LLM泛化能力强但推理成本过高。需要一种既能保持高精度又能控制成本的解决方案。

Method: 提出DeepParse混合框架：1）使用LLM从小规模日志样本中自动挖掘可重用的变量模式；2）将这些模式通过Drain算法确定性应用。通过将推理阶段与执行阶段分离，避免依赖脆弱的手工规则或逐行神经推理。

Result: 在16个基准数据集上，DeepParse在变量提取方面达到97.6%的平均解析精度，优于启发式和纯LLM基线。集成到异常检测管道中，相比启发式基线减少30%以上的误报，推理延迟降低36%。

Conclusion: DeepParse通过LLM模式挖掘与确定性算法执行的结合，实现了准确、可扩展且成本高效的日志结构化，为大规模日志分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Modern distributed systems produce massive, heterogeneous logs essential for reliability, security, and anomaly detection. Converting these free-form messages into structured templates (log parsing) is challenging due to evolving formats and limited labeled data. Machine-learning-based parsers like Drain are fast but accuracy often degrades on complex variables, while Large Language Models (LLMs) offer better generalization but incur prohibitive inference costs. This paper presents DeepParse, a hybrid framework that automatically mines reusable variable patterns from small log samples using an LLM, then applies them deterministically through the Drain algorithm. By separating the reasoning phase from execution, DeepParse enables accurate, scalable, and cost-efficient log structuring without relying on brittle handcrafted rules or per-line neural inference. Across 16 benchmark datasets, DeepParse achieves higher accuracy in variable extraction (97.6% average Parsing Accuracy) and better consistency than both heuristic and LLM-only baselines. Integrating DeepParse into an anomaly detection pipeline reduced false alarms by over 30% and reduced inference latency by 36% compared to heuristic baselines.

</details>


### [60] [Evaluating Software Defect Prediction Models via the Area Under the ROC Curve Can Be Misleading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20742)
*Luigi Lavazza,Gabriele Rotoloni,Sandro Morasca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了传统ROC曲线和AUC在软件缺陷预测模型评估中的可靠性，指出高AUC值并不保证模型在所有阈值下都比随机模型表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证传统信念是否正确：ROC曲线完全位于ROC空间对角线之上是否意味着模型优于随机模型，以及高AUC值是否确实代表良好性能。作者质疑基于ROC曲线和AUC的软件缺陷预测模型评估是否可靠。

Method: 方法包括：1）对ROC曲线进行装饰，突出显示对应阈值值的点；2）将真阳性率和假阳性率表示为阈值的函数；3）通过这些可视化方法评估模型是否在所有阈值下都能比随机模型更好地分类缺陷模块和非缺陷模块。

Result: 结果表明，常用的评估标准可能导致错误结论。高AUC值并不能保证模型在所有可能阈值下的真阳性率和假阳性率都优于随机模型。装饰后的ROC曲线或替代表示方法才能全面评估SDP模型的相关方面。

Conclusion: 结论是：仅依赖传统ROC曲线和AUC进行软件缺陷预测模型评估存在局限性。需要采用装饰ROC曲线或替代表示方法来全面理解模型性能，避免基于不完整信息做出错误判断。

Abstract: Background: Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curves are widely used to evaluate the performance of Software Defect Prediction (SDP) models that estimate module fault-proneness, i.e., the probability that a module is faulty. A ROC curve maps a model's performance in terms of True Positive Rate and False Positive Rate for any possible threshold set on fault-proneness. The Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) summarizes the performance of a model across all possible thresholds. Traditionally, ROC curves completely above the bisector of the ROC space are considered better than random, and high AUC values are associated with good performance. Aim: We investigate whether these beliefs are correct, hence if SDP model evaluation based on ROC curves and AUC is reliable. Method: We decorate ROC curves by highlighting the points corresponding to threshold values. We also represent True Positive Rate and False Positive Rate as functions of the threshold. Thus, we can evaluate whether a model classifies both faulty and non-faulty modules better than the random model. Results: We show that commonly used evaluation criteria may lead to wrong conclusions. Conclusions: A high value of AUC does not guarantee that both the True Positive Rate and the False Positive Rate of a model are better than the random model's for all possible thresholds. Either decorated ROC curves or alternative representations are needed to appreciate all the relevant aspects of SDP models.

</details>


### [61] [Autonomous LLM-generated Feedback for Student Exercises in Introductory Software Engineering Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20803)
*Andreas Metzger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NAILA是一个利用生成式AI为软件工程课程提供24/7自主反馈的工具，通过LLM评估学生作业，研究涉及900+学生，探讨采用动机、用户接受度、使用模式和学术表现影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程入门课程面临学生人数快速增长、背景多样化以及生成式AI解决方案的影响。高师生比使得提供及时、高质量和个性化反馈成为教育者的重大挑战。

Method: 开发NAILA工具，利用现代LLM处理学生提交的开放文档格式解决方案，通过专门设计的提示模板与教师定义的模型解决方案进行对比评估。对杜伊斯堡-埃森大学900多名活跃学生进行实证研究，调查四个主要研究问题。

Result: 研究调查了：(1)学生采用或拒绝NAILA的动机因素；(2)通过感知有用性、易用性和主观学习进展衡量的用户接受度；(3)学生使用NAILA的频率和一致性；(4)使用NAILA接收AI反馈与接收人工反馈对学业表现的影响。

Conclusion: NAILA通过生成式AI技术为大规模软件工程课程提供了可行的自主反馈解决方案，实证研究评估了其在真实教育环境中的采用动机、用户接受度、使用模式和学术影响。

Abstract: Introductory Software Engineering (SE) courses face rapidly increasing student enrollment numbers, participants with diverse backgrounds and the influence of Generative AI (GenAI) solutions. High teacher-to-student ratios often challenge providing timely, high-quality, and personalized feedback a significant challenge for educators. To address these challenges, we introduce NAILA, a tool that provides 24/7 autonomous feedback for student exercises. Utilizing GenAI in the form of modern LLMs, NAILA processes student solutions provided in open document formats, evaluating them against teacher-defined model solutions through specialized prompt templates. We conducted an empirical study involving 900+ active students at the University of Duisburg-Essen to assess four main research questions investigating (1) the underlying motivations that drive students to either adopt or reject NAILA, (2) user acceptance by measuring perceived usefulness and ease of use alongside subjective learning progress, (3) how often and how consistently students engage with NAILA, and (4) how using NAILA to receive AI feedback impacts on academic performance compared to human feedback.

</details>

### [62] [Efficient Arithmetic-and-Comparison Homomorphic Encryption with Space Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19890)
*Erwin Eko Wahyudi,Yan Solihin,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出空间切换方法，在FV类全同态加密方案中统一支持算术和比较运算，相比方案切换和多项式近似方法显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全同态加密方案无法高效统一支持算术和比较运算。方案切换方法在大输入时开销过大，多项式近似方法在临界点附近存在误差，限制了在精度敏感任务中的应用。

Method: 提出空间切换方法，识别算术和比较运算需要不同的明文空间，引入两个过程：从数空间$\mathbb{Z}_{p^r}$到数字空间$\mathbb{Z}_{p}$的约简步骤，以及将结果映射回$\mathbb{Z}_{p^r}$的模提升步骤，实现同一方案内连续评估算术和比较运算。

Result: 实验显示该方法比方案切换快17倍，比直接比较快15倍（数据库工作负载），证明了其在现实世界隐私保护计算中的实用性。

Conclusion: 空间切换方法成功解决了全同态加密中算术和比较运算的统一支持问题，提供了高效实用的解决方案，代码和工具已开源。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables computation on encrypted data without decryption, making it central to privacy-preserving applications. However, no existing scheme efficiently supports both arithmetic and comparison operations in a unified framework. Prior approaches such as scheme switching and polynomial approximation face serious limitations: switching incurs prohibitive overhead for large inputs, while approximation methods introduce errors near critical points, restricting use in accuracy-sensitive tasks. We propose space switching method to integrate arithmetic and comparison computation seamlessly within FV-style schemes. Our approach identifies that the two types of operations require different plaintext spaces and introduces two procedures: a reduction step to transition from the number space $\mathbb{Z}_{p^r}$ to the digit space $\mathbb{Z}_{p}$, and a modulus-raising step to map results back to $\mathbb{Z}_{p^r}$. This design enables continuous evaluation of arithmetic and comparison within the same scheme. Experiments show that our method achieves up to $17\times$ faster performance than scheme switching and $15\times$ faster than direct comparison on database workloads, demonstrating its practicality for real-world privacy-preserving computation. Code and artifacts are available at https://github.com/UCF-Lou-Lab-PET/Universal-BGV.

</details>


### [63] [A Data-Free Membership Inference Attack on Federated Learning in Hardware Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19891)
*Gijung Lee,Wavid Bowman,Olivia P. Dizon-Paradis,Reiner N. Dizon-Paradis,Ronald Wilson,Damon L. Woodard,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对硬件保障中联邦学习图像分割模型的新型无数据成员推理攻击，利用标准单元库布局作为先验知识，通过梯度反演重构图像，从而推断敏感硬件特性，挑战了联邦学习的隐私保证假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然通过不共享原始数据来增强隐私，但在硬件保障领域仍然容易受到成员推理攻击，可能泄露敏感知识产权。传统MIA需要匹配私有数据统计特性的辅助数据集，这在硬件领域往往不切实际，因此需要开发无数据的攻击方法。

Method: 提出了一种针对图像分割模型的无数据MIA，利用标准单元库布局作为先验知识指导梯度反演攻击。通过拦截客户端模型更新，在不访问私有数据的情况下重构图像，并引入新颖的损失函数项来克服结构复杂数据的评估瓶颈，增强攻击效果。

Result: 攻击能够成功重构图像并推断敏感硬件特性，包括区分电路层（如金属层与扩散层）和技术节点（如32nm与90nm）。实验证明，通过分析重构保真度，攻击者能够有效识别私有数据中的敏感信息。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了联邦学习在硬件保障领域存在显著的知识产权风险，挑战了FL提供固有隐私保证的假设，证明即使在没有领域特定数据集的情况下，严重的信息泄露仍然可能发生。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is an emerging solution to the data scarcity problem for training deep learning models in hardware assurance. While FL is designed to enhance privacy by not sharing raw data, it remains vulnerable to Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) that can leak sensitive intellectual property (IP). Traditional MIAs are often impractical in this domain because they require access to auxiliary datasets that can match the unique statistical properties of private data. This paper introduces a novel, data-free MIA targeting image segmentation models in FL for hardware assurance. Our methodology leverages Standard Cell Library Layouts (SCLLs) as priors to guide a gradient inversion attack, allowing an adversary to reconstruct images from a client's intercepted model update without needing any private data. We demonstrate that, by analyzing the reconstruction fidelity, an adversary can infer sensitive hardware characteristics, successfully distinguishing between circuit layers (e.g., metal vs. diffusion) and technology nodes (e.g., 32nm vs. 90nm). Our findings reveal that a novel loss term can conditionally amplify the attack's effectiveness by overcoming evaluation bottlenecks for structurally complex data. This work underscores a significant IP risk, challenging the assumption that FL provides inherent privacy guarantees and proving that severe information leakage can occur even without access to domain-specific datasets.

</details>


### [64] [DECIFR: Domain-Aware Exfiltration of Circuit Information from Federated Gradient Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19915)
*Gijung Lee,Wavid Bowman,Olivia P. Dizon-Paradis,Reiner N. Dizon-Paradis,Ronald Wilson,Damon L. Woodard,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DECIFR是一种针对联邦学习的成员推理攻击，利用标准单元库布局重建客户端训练图像，无需辅助数据集即可判断数据成员身份


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在硬件保障中作为隐私保护技术具有前景，但其对具备领域特定知识的攻击者的安全性研究不足。本文旨在揭示利用标准单元库布局可破坏敏感集成电路训练数据隐私的关键漏洞。

Method: 提出DECIFR，一种新颖的两阶段成员推理攻击方法：第一阶段使用引导梯度反转攻击从截获的模型更新中重建客户端训练图像；第二阶段基于重建图像的质量与成员状态的相关性来区分成员与非成员。

Result: 攻击发现重建图像的保真度与成员状态直接相关，攻击者能够基于图像质量可靠地区分成员与非成员。该攻击克服了传统攻击的局限性，表明标准联邦学习协议在具有广泛知识的领域中不足以保证安全。

Conclusion: 标准联邦学习协议不足以保护具有广泛领域知识的应用场景，在硬件保障中安全应用联邦学习需要强大的防御机制。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a promising approach for multiparty collaboration as a privacy-preserving technique in hardware assurance, but its security against adversaries with domain-specific knowledge is underexplored. This paper demonstrates a critical vulnerability where available standard cell library layouts (SCLL) can be exploited to compromise the privacy of sensitive integrated circuit (IC) training data. We introduce DECIFR, a novel two-stage Membership Inference Attack (MIA) that requires no auxiliary dataset. The attack employs a guided Gradient Inversion Attack (GIA) to reconstruct a client's training images from intercepted model updates. Our findings reveal that the fidelity of these reconstructions directly correlates with membership status, allowing an adversary to reliably distinguish members from non-members based on image quality. This work exposes a practical threat that overcomes the limitations of conventional attacks and underscores that standard FL protocols are insufficient for securing domains with extensive knowledge. We conclude that robust defenses are essential for the secure application of FL in hardware assurance.

</details>


### [65] [Potentials and Pitfalls of Applying Federated Learning in Hardware Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20020)
*Gijung Lee,Wavid Bowman,Olivia Dizon-Paradis,Reiner Dizon-Paradis,Ronald Wilson,Damon Woodard,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究首次探索了联邦学习在硬件安全保障中的应用，发现FL在逆向工程分割任务中优于单客户端集中学习，但暴露了FL对梯度反转攻击的脆弱性，可能泄露敏感IP信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着微电子产业外包化发展，硬件木马、假冒电子产品和逆向工程攻击等安全威胁日益严重。传统检测方法在可靠性和可扩展性方面存在局限。深度学习可用于自动化逆向工程，但面临数据稀缺问题。联邦学习理论上能实现协作训练而不共享原始数据，但其在硬件保障领域的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 本研究首次将联邦学习应用于硬件安全保障，采用基于深度学习的联邦学习框架进行逆向工程分割任务。通过比较FL与单客户端集中学习的性能，并评估不同客户端数量对模型效果的影响。同时研究了FL对梯度反转攻击的脆弱性，展示了攻击者如何从梯度中恢复SEM图像。

Result: 1. 联邦学习在逆向工程分割任务中表现优于单客户端集中学习；2. 增加客户端数量能通过更多数据协作训练提升FL性能；3. 但FL存在重大缺陷——对梯度反转攻击脆弱，攻击者能够从梯度中恢复SEM图像，从而暴露本应保护的敏感知识产权信息。

Conclusion: 联邦学习为硬件安全保障提供了有前景的协作训练方案，但当前的FL实现存在严重隐私风险。研究揭示了FL在保护敏感IP方面的局限性，并强调了需要进一步研究改进FL的安全性和有效性，以应对硬件保障领域的独特挑战。

Abstract: As microelectronics flourish and outsourcing of the design and manufacturing stages of integrated circuits (ICs) and printed circuit boards (PCBs) becomes the norm, microelectronics stakeholders must also confront a new wave of security challenges, including the threats posed by hardware Trojans, counterfeit electronics, and reverse engineering attacks. Traditional detection and prevention methods like testing and side-channel analysis have limitations in reliability and scalability. Automated reverse engineering by deep learning (DL) models is a foolproof approach to hardware assurance, but faces challenges due to limited data. By pooling data from different stakeholders (competitors in industry, governments, etc.), DL models can be more effectively trained but privacy of intellectual property (IP) is a significant concern. Federated Learning (FL) has been proposed as a potential alternative allowing for the collaborative training of a DL model without sharing raw data. While FL has been widely used in healthcare, IoT, and finance, its application in hardware assurance remains underexplored. This study investigates, for the first time, FL-based DL for hardware assurance, demonstrating that FL outperforms single-client centralized learning in segmentation tasks for reverse engineering. Our results show that increasing the number of clients improves FL performance by collaboratively training the model with more data. However, and more importantly, a major pitfall of FL is also exposed -- it remains vulnerable to gradient inversion attacks. We show that SEM images used in FL can be recovered by attackers, which would therefore expose the sensitive and proprietary IPs that FL was supposed to protect. We highlight these privacy risks and also suggest future research directions to improve security and effectiveness in hardware assurance.

</details>


### [66] [AgentSOC: A Multi-Layer Agentic AI Framework for Security Operations Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20134)
*Joyjit Roy,Samaresh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentSOC是一个多层智能体AI框架，通过整合感知、预期推理和基于风险的行动规划来增强安全运营中心自动化，提供从告警归一化到策略合规响应的完整操作循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临异构告警关联困难、多阶段攻击进展解释复杂以及安全有效响应行动选择困难等挑战，需要更智能的自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出AgentSOC多层智能体AI框架，整合多个抽象层提供统一操作循环，包括告警归一化、上下文丰富、假设生成、结构可行性验证和执行策略合规响应。

Result: 在大企业环境中的概念评估显示，AgentSOC提高了分类一致性，能够预测攻击者意图，并提供既操作可行又在安全效能与运营影响之间平衡的遏制选项推荐。使用LANL认证数据的POC演示验证了架构可行性。

Conclusion: 混合智能体推理有潜力作为大型企业开发自适应、更安全SOC自动化的基础，AgentSOC框架为解决SOC自动化挑战提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) increasingly encounter difficulties in correlating heterogeneous alerts, interpreting multi-stage attack progressions, and selecting safe and effective response actions. This study introduces AgentSOC, a multi-layered agentic AI framework that enhances SOC automation by integrating perception, anticipatory reasoning, and risk-based action planning. The proposed architecture consolidates several layers of abstraction to provide a single operational loop to support normalizing alerts, enriching context, generating hypotheses, validating structural feasibility, and executing policy-compliant responses. Conceptually evaluated within a large enterprise environment, AgentSOC improves triage consistency, anticipates attackers' intentions, and provides recommended containment options that are both operationally feasible and well-balanced between security efficacy and operational impact. The results suggest that hybrid agentic reasoning has the potential to serve as a foundation for developing adaptive, safer SOC automation in large enterprises. Additionally, a minimal Proof-Of-Concept (POC) demonstration using LANL authentication data demonstrated the feasibility of the proposed architecture.

</details>


### [67] [TLSCheck 2.0: An Enhanced Memory Forensics Approach to Efficiently Detect TLS Callbacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20378)
*Kartik N. Iyer,Parag H. Rughani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 增强版TlsCheck插件用于Volatility 3，通过分析PE头部和内存结构精确检测TLS回调表，结合反汇编和模式匹配技术识别恶意TLS回调行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS回调在数字取证中具有挑战性，因为它们既是合法的Windows构造，又是恶意软件执行的潜在载体。现有工具在检测和分析TLS回调方面存在不足，需要更精确的检测方法来应对基于TLS的威胁。

Method: 开发增强版TlsCheck插件，实现：1) 通过PE头部和内存结构分析精确检测TLS回调表；2) 支持32位和64位架构；3) 结合反汇编分析回调例程；4) 使用自定义正则表达式和YARA规则进行模式匹配；5) 指令级分析识别反调试、代码注入等恶意行为。

Result: 该插件显著提升了防御者在内存取证中检测和调查基于TLS威胁的能力，为分析师提供回调位置、汇编行为和可疑活动迹象的深入洞察，支持更有效的恶意软件分析和事件响应操作。

Conclusion: 增强版TlsCheck插件通过精确的TLS回调检测和深入的行为分析，填补了内存取证中TLS分析的技术空白，为数字取证调查人员提供了强大的工具来应对日益复杂的基于TLS的恶意软件威胁。

Abstract: Memory analysis is a crucial technique in digital forensics that enables investigators to examine the runtime state of a system through physical memory dumps. While significant advances have been made in memory forensics, the detection and analysis of Thread Local Storage (TLS) callbacks remain challenging due to their dual nature as both legitimate Windows constructs and potential vectors for malware execution. An early version of the TlsCheck plugin received recognition in the Volatility Plugin Contest 2024. In this paper, we present an enhanced version of TlsCheck for Volatility 3, designed to detect and analyze TLS callbacks in process memory. It implements precise detection of TLS callback tables through analysis of PE headers and memory structures, combined with disassembly of identified callback routines. The plugin supports both 32-bit and 64-bit architectures, offering investigators insights into callback locations, assembly behavior, and potential signs of suspicious activity. To enhance detection, we incorporate pattern matching using custom regular expressions and YARA rules, helping analysts identify specific code patterns or suspicious constructs within TLS callbacks. The framework also includes instruction-level analysis to highlight behavior often linked to malware, such as anti-debugging, code injection, and process manipulation. This implementation significantly improves defenders' ability to detect and investigate TLS-based threats during memory forensics, supporting more effective malware analysis and incident response operations.

</details>


### [68] [Onyx: Cost-Efficient Disk-Oblivious ANN Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20401)
*Deevashwer Rathee,Jean-Luc Watson,Zirui Neil Zhao,G. Edward Suh,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Onyx是一个高效的无感知近似最近邻搜索系统，通过ANN层最小化带宽消耗和ORAM层最小化访问次数的协同设计，在SSD上实现成本效益高的安全搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在第三方基础设施上处理敏感数据的近似最近邻搜索需要保护，但现有基于可信执行环境和SSD的方案会泄露查询访问模式，而传统ORAM与ANN结合时资源利用率低、延迟高、成本效益差。

Method: 提出Onyx系统，包含两个协同设计组件：1) Onyx-ANNS使用紧凑中间表示主动剪枝带宽密集型访问；2) Onyx-ORAM采用局部感知浅层树设计减少访问次数，同时保持与带宽高效ORAM技术的兼容性。

Result: 相比最先进的无感知ANN搜索系统，Onyx实现了1.7-9.9倍的成本降低和2.3-12.3倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 通过反转传统设计思路，让ANN层专注于带宽效率、ORAM层专注于访问次数优化，Onyx在SSD上实现了高效且安全的无感知近似最近邻搜索，平衡了带宽和访问次数两个关键资源。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search in AI systems increasingly handles sensitive data on third-party infrastructure. Trusted execution environments (TEEs) offer protection, but cost-efficient deployments must rely on external SSDs, which leaks user queries through disk access patterns to the host. Oblivious RAM (ORAM) can hide these access patterns but at a high cost; when paired with existing disk-based ANN search techniques, it makes poor use of SSD resources, yielding high latency and poor cost-efficiency. The core challenge for efficient oblivious ANN search over SSDs is balancing both bandwidth and access count. The state-of-the-art ORAM-ANN design minimizes access count at the ANN level and bandwidth at the ORAM level, each trading-off the other, leaving the combined system with both resources overutilized. We propose inverting this design, minimizing bandwidth consumption in the ANN layer and access count in the ORAM layer, since each component is better suited for its new role: ANN's inherent approximation allows for more bandwidth efficiency, while ORAM has no fundamental lower bounds on access count (as opposed to bandwidth). To this end, we propose a cost-efficient approach, Onyx, with two new co-designed components: Onyx-ANNS introduces a compact intermediate representation that proactively prunes the majority of bandwidth-intensive accesses without hurting recall, and Onyx-ORAM proposes a locality-aware shallow tree design that reduces access count while remaining compatible with bandwidth-efficient ORAM techniques. Compared to the state-of-the-art oblivious ANN search system, Onyx achieves $1.7-9.9\times$ lower cost and $2.3-12.3\times$ lower latency.

</details>


### [69] [Towards Certified Malware Detection: Provable Guarantees Against Evasion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20495)
*Nandakrishna Giri,Asmitha K. A.,Serena Nicolazzo,Antonino Nocera,Vinod P*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于随机平滑的认证鲁棒恶意软件检测框架，通过特征消融和定向噪声注入对抗变形引擎攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的静态恶意软件检测器容易受到对抗性规避技术（如变形引擎突变）的攻击，需要提供认证鲁棒性的解决方案

Method: 采用随机平滑技术，通过特征消融和定向噪声注入生成多个消融变体，使用平滑分类器进行分类，基于多数投票确定最终标签，并通过Wilson得分区间分析提供形式化鲁棒性证书

Result: 提出的平滑分类器在干净可执行文件和PyMetaEngine生成的消融变体上均表现出色，成功提供对抗变形规避攻击的认证鲁棒性，无需修改底层机器学习架构

Conclusion: 基于随机平滑的框架能够有效提供对抗变形引擎攻击的认证鲁棒恶意软件检测，为静态恶意软件检测器提供了实用的鲁棒性增强方案

Abstract: Machine learning-based static malware detectors remain vulnerable to adversarial evasion techniques, such as metamorphic engine mutations. To address this vulnerability, we propose a certifiably robust malware detection framework based on randomized smoothing through feature ablation and targeted noise injection. During evaluation, our system analyzes an executable by generating multiple ablated variants, classifies them by using a smoothed classifier, and identifies the final label based on the majority vote. By analyzing the top-class voting distribution and the Wilson score interval, we derive a formal certificate that guarantees robustness within a specific radius against feature-space perturbations. We evaluate our approach by comparing the performance of the base classifier and the smoothed classifier on both clean executables and ablated variants generated using PyMetaEngine. Our results demonstrate that the proposed smoothed classifier successfully provides certifiable robustness against metamorphic evasion attacks without requiring modifications to the underlying machine learning architecture.

</details>


### [70] [PVAC: A RowHammer Mitigation Architecture Exploiting Per-victim-row Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20576)
*Jumin Kim,Seungmin Baek,Hwayong Nam,Minbok Wi,Nam Sung Kim,Jung Ho Ahn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PVAC是一种基于受害行的RowHammer计数机制，通过专用计数器子阵列实现高效计数，相比DDR5的PRAC机制避免了误报和性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDR5引入的PRAC（每行激活计数）机制存在根本性缺陷：它跟踪攻击者行但旨在保护受害者行，导致计数器在空闲时也会饱和，触发不必要的缓解措施并降低性能。

Method: 提出PVAC（每受害者行受击计数）机制，采用受害者行计数方法，通过专用计数器子阵列（CSA）实现与正常访问并发的计数器更新，无需额外时序开销。CSA布局经过优化以减少刷新引起的计数器访问。

Result: PVAC相比PRAC支持更高的锤击容忍度，同时保持相同的最坏情况安全保证。在良性工作负载和对抗性攻击模式下，PVAC避免了虚假警报，消除了PRAC的时序惩罚，实现了更高的性能和更低的能耗。

Conclusion: PVAC通过将计数器语义与RowHammer的物理干扰机制对齐，解决了PRAC的根本性不匹配问题，提供了更高效、更精确的RowHammer防御方案。

Abstract: As DRAM scaling exacerbates RowHammer, DDR5 introduces per-row activation counting (PRAC) to track aggressor activity. However, PRAC indiscriminately increments counters on every activation -- including benign refreshes -- while relying solely on explicit RFM operations for resets. Consequently, counters saturate even in an idle bank, triggering cascading mitigations and degrading performance. This vulnerability arises from a fundamental mismatch: PRAC tracks the aggressor but aims to protect the victim.
  We present Per-Victim-row hAmmered Counting (PVAC), a victim-based counting mechanism that aligns the counter semantics with the physical disturbance mechanism of RowHammer. PVAC increments the counters of victim rows, resets the activated row, and naturally bounds counter values under normal refresh. To enable efficient victim-based updates, PVAC employs a dedicated counter subarray (CSA) that performs all counter resets and increments concurrently with normal accesses, without timing overhead. We further devise an energy-efficient CSA layout that minimizes refresh-induced counter accesses. Through victim-based counting, PVAC supports higher hammering tolerance than PRAC while maintaining the same worst-case safety guarantee. Across benign workloads and adversarial attack patterns, PVAC avoids spurious Alerts, eliminates PRAC timing penalties, and achieves higher performance and lower energy consumption than prior PRAC-based defenses.

</details>


### [71] [SoK: The Next Frontier in AV Security: Systematizing Perception Attacks and the Emerging Threat of Multi-Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20621)
*Shahriar Rahman Khan,Tariqul Islam,Raiful Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了48项关于自动驾驶汽车感知层攻击的研究，建立了统一的攻击向量分类法，揭示了多传感器融合系统中的安全漏洞，并指出了当前研究的关键空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶汽车越来越多地依赖多传感器感知系统，这些系统结合摄像头、激光雷达、雷达等多种模态数据来理解环境。然而，这种多传感器融合在提高鲁棒性的同时，也引入了新的安全漏洞。论文旨在系统化分析感知层攻击研究，揭示从单传感器攻击到复杂跨模态威胁的演变，并识别当前研究中的关键空白。

Method: 论文系统综述了48项同行评审研究，开发了包含20个攻击向量的统一分类法，按传感器类型、攻击阶段、媒介和感知模块进行组织。通过分析揭示了融合逻辑和跨传感器依赖关系中的未充分探索的漏洞。为说明其中一个研究空白，作者通过结合红外和激光雷达欺骗的概念验证模拟来验证融合级漏洞。

Result: 研究发现自动驾驶安全领域发生了根本性转变：随着系统融合更多传感器以提高鲁棒性，攻击者开始利用本应确保安全性的冗余性。分析揭示了多传感器融合逻辑和跨传感器依赖关系中的关键漏洞，并识别了研究空白，包括有限的真实世界测试、短期评估偏见以及缺乏考虑传感器间一致性的防御措施。

Conclusion: 论文强调了自动驾驶安全面临的新挑战：多传感器融合系统在提高鲁棒性的同时，其冗余性反而成为攻击目标。作者提出了面向融合感知的防御设计方向，并为自主系统中可信感知制定了研究议程，呼吁关注融合逻辑安全性和跨传感器一致性的防御机制。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) increasingly rely on multi-sensor perception pipelines that combine data from cameras, lidar, radar, and other modalities to interpret the environment. This SoK systematizes 48 peer-reviewed studies on perception-layer attacks against AVs, tracking the field's evolution from single-sensor exploits to complex cross-modal threats that compromise multi-sensor fusion (MSF). We develop a unified taxonomy of 20 attack vectors organized by sensor type, attack stage, medium, and perception module, revealing patterns that expose underexplored vulnerabilities in fusion logic and cross-sensor dependencies. Our analysis identifies key research gaps, including limited real-world testing, short-term evaluation bias, and the absence of defenses that account for inter-sensor consistency. To illustrate one such gap, we validate a fusion-level vulnerability through a proof-of-concept simulation combining infrared and lidar spoofing. The findings highlight a fundamental shift in AV security: as systems fuse more sensors for robustness, attackers exploit the very redundancy meant to ensure safety. We conclude with directions for fusion-aware defense design and a research agenda for trustworthy perception in autonomous systems.

</details>


### [72] [Auto-ART: Structured Literature Synthesis and Automated Adversarial Robustness Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20704)
*Abhijit Talluri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了对抗鲁棒性评估领域的结构化元科学分析框架Auto-ART，包含50+攻击方法、28个防御模块、鲁棒性诊断指数和梯度掩蔽检测，支持多范数评估和合规性映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒性评估是可信机器学习部署的基础，但该领域存在协议碎片化和梯度掩蔽未被检测的问题。现有评估方法缺乏系统性分析框架，无法全面评估模型在不同攻击范数下的真实鲁棒性。

Method: 采用结构化综合方法：1) 分析九个同行评审语料源(2020-2026)，通过七种互补协议进行端到端结构化分析；2) 开发Auto-ART开源框架，包含50多种攻击方法、28个防御模块、鲁棒性诊断指数(RDI)和梯度掩蔽检测，支持l1/l2/linf/语义/空间多范数评估，并映射到NIST AI RMF、OWASP LLM Top 10和欧盟AI法案等合规标准。

Result: 在RobustBench上的实证验证表明：Auto-ART预筛选在92%的标记案例中成功检测到梯度掩蔽；RDI排名与完整AutoAttack高度相关；多范数评估揭示了最先进模型的平均鲁棒性与最坏情况鲁棒性之间存在23.5个百分点的差距。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将结构化元科学分析与可执行评估框架相结合，填补了文献空白与工程实践之间的鸿沟。Auto-ART框架为对抗鲁棒性评估提供了系统化、标准化的解决方案，有助于推动可信机器学习部署的实际应用。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness evaluation underpins every claim of trustworthy ML deployment, yet the field suffers from fragmented protocols and undetected gradient masking. We make two contributions. (1) Structured synthesis. We analyze nine peer-reviewed corpus sources (2020--2026) through seven complementary protocols, producing the first end-to-end structured analysis of the field's consensus and unresolved challenges. (2) Auto-ART framework. We introduce Auto-ART, an open-source framework that operationalizes identified gaps: 50+ attacks, 28 defense modules, the Robustness Diagnostic Index (RDI), and gradient-masking detection. It supports multi-norm evaluation (l1/l2/linf/semantic/spatial) and compliance mapping to NIST AI RMF, OWASP LLM Top 10, and the EU AI Act. Empirical validation on RobustBench demonstrates that Auto-ART's pre-screening identifies gradient masking in 92% of flagged cases, and RDI rankings correlate highly with full AutoAttack. Multi-norm evaluation exposes a 23.5 pp gap between average and worst-case robustness on state-of-the-art models. No prior work combines such structured meta-scientific analysis with an executable evaluation framework bridging literature gaps into engineering.

</details>


### [73] [Fresh Masking Makes NTT Pipelines Composable: Machine-Checked Proofs for Arithmetic Masking in PQC Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20793)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过Lean 4形式化验证了NTT流水线在算术掩码下的安全性，解决了之前布尔掩码框架无法处理ℤq上NTT的问题，并发现了Adams Bridge加速器的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码加速器中的NTT流水线安全性缺乏形式化验证，现有组合框架（ISW、t-SNI、PINI、DOM）仅针对GF(2)上的布尔掩码设计，无法处理ℤq上的算术掩码NTT蝴蝶操作。

Method: 使用Lean 4定理证明器和Mathlib库进行机器验证，提出三个核心结果：1) 值独立性在新鲜随机性下蕴含常数边际分布；2) 蝴蝶操作的每上下文均匀性；3) k级NTT流水线在ISW一阶探测模型下的安全性。

Result: 成功形式化验证了9个定理，包含1738个构建任务且零错误。发现了Adams Bridge加速器（CHIPS Alliance Caliptra）因仅在INTT第0轮使用掩码而违反新鲜掩码假设，解释了其结构不安全性的架构原因。

Conclusion: 首次为ℤq上的NTT流水线提供了机器验证的安全性证明，填补了算术掩码组合框架的空白，并为实际硬件实现提供了安全指导，同时指出了点状值独立性在蝴蝶输出中不成立的重要警告。

Abstract: Post-quantum cryptographic (PQC) accelerators for ML-KEM (FIPS 203) and ML-DSA (FIPS 204) rely on pipelined Number Theoretic Transform (NTT) stages over $\mathbb{Z}_q$. Our prior work established structural dependency analysis at scale [1] and quantified the security margin of partial NTT masking [2]. Whether per-stage arithmetic masking guarantees pipeline-level security had no prior machine-checked answer for the r-bearing case: composition frameworks (ISW, t-SNI, PINI, DOM) were formalized exclusively for Boolean masking over $\mathrm{GF}(2)$; no proof assistant artifact addresses the NTT butterfly over $\mathbb{Z}_q$. We present three machine-checked results in Lean 4 with Mathlib, all zero sorry. First, we close a stated limitation of prior work: value-independence implies constant marginal distribution under fresh randomness (via an algebraic MutualInfoZero proxy). Second, butterfly per-context uniformity: for any Cooley-Tukey butterfly with fresh output mask over $\mathbb{Z}/q\mathbb{Z}$ ($q > 0$), each output wire has exactly one mask value producing each output, a uniform marginal independent of secrets, universal over all moduli, twiddle factors, and inputs. Third, a k-stage NTT pipeline with fresh per-stage masking satisfies per-context uniformity at every stage under the ISW first-order probing model. We document a named warning: pointwise value-independence is false for butterfly outputs. The Adams Bridge accelerator (CHIPS Alliance Caliptra) fails the fresh masking hypothesis, masking active only in INTT round 0, architecturally explaining its structural insecurity. Artifact: nine theorems, 1,738 build jobs, zero sorry. Composition for nonlinear gadgets (Barrett) is addressed in forthcoming manuscripts proving Barrett's PF-PINI(2) satisfaction ('one-bit barrier') [3] and k-stage composition for PF-PINI gadgets under fresh-mask renewal [4].

</details>


### [74] [AVISE: Framework for Evaluating the Security of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20833)
*Mikko Lempinen,Joni Kemppainen,Niklas Raesalmi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AVISE是一个用于识别AI系统漏洞和评估安全性的模块化开源框架，通过增强型Red Queen攻击和自动化安全评估测试来发现语言模型的越狱漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在关键领域的部署增加，其安全漏洞带来高风险，但目前缺乏系统性的AI安全评估方法。

Method: 开发AVISE框架，扩展基于心理理论的多轮Red Queen攻击为对抗性语言模型增强攻击，创建包含25个测试用例和评估语言模型的安全评估测试。

Result: 安全评估测试达到92%准确率、0.91 F1分数和0.83马修斯相关系数；评估的9个语言模型都不同程度地易受增强Red Queen攻击。

Conclusion: AVISE为研究人员和从业者提供了可扩展的基础，用于开发和部署自动化安全评估测试，是迈向更严格、可复现AI安全评估的具体步骤。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed across critical domains, their security vulnerabilities pose growing risks of high-profile exploits and consequential system failures. Yet systematic approaches to evaluating AI security remain underdeveloped. In this paper, we introduce AVISE (AI Vulnerability Identification and Security Evaluation), a modular open-source framework for identifying vulnerabilities in and evaluating the security of AI systems and models. As a demonstration of the framework, we extend the theory-of-mind-based multi-turn Red Queen attack into an Adversarial Language Model (ALM) augmented attack and develop an automated Security Evaluation Test (SET) for discovering jailbreak vulnerabilities in language models. The SET comprises 25 test cases and an Evaluation Language Model (ELM) that determines whether each test case was able to jailbreak the target model, achieving 92% accuracy, an F1-score of 0.91, and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.83. We evaluate nine recently released language models of diverse sizes with the SET and find that all are vulnerable to the augmented Red Queen attack to varying degrees. AVISE provides researchers and industry practitioners with an extensible foundation for developing and deploying automated SETs, offering a concrete step toward more rigorous and reproducible AI security evaluation.

</details>
