### [75] [SAT + NAUTY: Orderly Generation of Small Kochen-Specker Sets Containing the Smallest State-independent Contextuality Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19947)
*Zhengyu Li,Curtis Bright,Stefan Trandafir,Adán Cabello,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 开发了基于SAT的有序生成框架，结合递归规范标记(RCL)和NAUTY图同构工具，首次穷举枚举了包含Yu-Oh集完全25射线SI-C集的所有最多33射线的KS集，验证了Schütte的33射线集是最小的三维KS集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找三维空间中小的Kochen-Specker(KS)集，特别是Yu-Oh 13射线集的扩展。Yu-Oh集已被证明是状态无关上下文性的最小见证，但需要寻找包含其完全25射线SI-C集的最小KS集。

Method: 提出新颖的SAT有序生成框架，整合递归规范标记(RCL)与NAUTY图同构工具。解决了传统SAT方法依赖字典序规范性的指数级扩展问题，在大型实例(25-33顶点)上保持毫秒级性能。

Result: 在1,641 CPU小时内完成了首次穷举枚举，发现并验证了Schütte的33射线集是包含完全25射线SI-C集的最小三维KS集。所有非存在结果都通过扩展的DRAT证明格式提供了独立可验证的证明证书。

Conclusion: RCL检查消除了SAT方法在规范图上的性能瓶颈，使得大规模KS集搜索变得可行。验证了Schütte的33射线集是最小KS集，为量子上下文性研究提供了重要工具和结果。

Abstract: We present a search for small Kochen-Specker (KS) sets in dimension 3, specifically targeting extensions of the 13-ray Yu-Oh set, which has been proven to be the minimal witness to state-independent contextuality. To enable this search, we introduce a novel SAT-based orderly generation framework integrating recursive canonical labeling (RCL) with the graph isomorphism tool NAUTY. We demonstrate that previous SAT approaches relying on lexicographical canonicity suffer from exponential scaling on canonical graphs. This limitation renders them intractable on the large instances (25 to 33 vertices) encountered in our search, whereas our RCL check maintains consistent millisecond-level performance, effectively eliminating the bottleneck. Overcoming this bottleneck allows us to perform the first exhaustive enumeration of all KS sets with up to 33 rays containing the complete 25-ray state-independent contextuality (SI-C) set obtained by rigid extensions of the Yu-Oh set in 1,641 CPU hours. We found and verified that the 33-ray set discovered by Schütte is the smallest three-dimensional KS set containing the complete 25-ray SI-C set. All non-existence results are backed by independently verifiable proof certificates via an extension of the DRAT proof format.

</details>


### [76] [Visualising CTL Witnesses and Counterexamples -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20253)
*Arend Rensink*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出CTL属性在显式状态模型上的证据概念，包括满足属性的见证和违反属性的反例，重点关注人类可理解的可视化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL在反例概念上比CTL更直观易懂（反例是违反属性的轨迹），而CTL缺乏类似的直观证据概念。本文旨在为CTL属性提出一种既可作为满足属性的见证又可作为违反属性的反例的证据概念，并实现可视化，以增强人类对CTL属性的理解。

Method: 1. 建立CTL证据的形式化模型；2. 为每个时序算子（temporal operator）表征最小证据；3. 提出具体的可视化实现方案。

Result: 1. 形式化的CTL证据模型；2. 各时序算子的最小证据特征描述；3. 具体的可视化实现方案（已实现）。

Conclusion: 为CTL属性提出了统一的证据概念，既可作为见证也可作为反例，并通过可视化增强了人类对CTL属性的理解能力。这是SPIN 2026会议论文的扩展版本，包含所有结果的证明。

Abstract: One of the advantages of LTL over CTL is that the notion of a counterexample is easy to grasp, visualise and process: it is a trace that violates the property at hand. In this paper we propose a notion of evidence for CTL properties on explicit-state models -- which equally serves as witness for satisfied properties and counterexample for violated ones -- and how to visualise it, with the main aim of (human) comprehension. The main contribution consists of a formal model of evidence, a characterisation of minimal evidence per temporal operator, and a concrete, implemented proposal for its visualisation.
  This is the extended version of a paper published in SPIN 2026, containing the proofs of all results.

</details>


### [77] [A Rocq Formalization of Simplicial Lagrange Finite Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20345)
*Sylvie Boldo,François Clément,Vincent Martin,Micaela Mayero,Houda Mouhcine*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在Rocq证明助手中形式化有限元定义，包括几何、多项式逼近空间和线性形式，并实例化任意维度和多项式次数的单纯形Lagrange有限元


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为科学计算程序的正确性证明提供基础，特别是针对有限元方法这一流行的偏微分方程数值解法。当前研究聚焦于有限元的形式化定义，这是证明有限元方法正确性的长期目标中的关键一步。

Method: 在Rocq证明助手中将有限元设计为记录类型，包含几何、多项式逼近空间和线性形式等值，以及有效性证明（如逼近空间维数）。关键的有效性证明是唯一可解性（unisolvence），确保多项式表征的唯一性。然后实例化最流行和实用的单纯形Lagrange有限元，支持任意维度和多项式次数。

Result: 成功在Rocq中形式化定义了有限元，并实现了任意维度和多项式次数的单纯形Lagrange有限元的实例化，包括困难的唯一可解性证明。这些证明需要关于有限族、仿射空间、多元多项式在有限或无限维空间中的许多结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为有限元方法的形式化验证奠定了基础，通过Rocq证明助手实现了有限元的精确数学定义和关键性质证明，特别是针对广泛应用的单纯形Lagrange有限元。这为后续证明有限元方法的正确性提供了重要工具和框架。

Abstract: Formalization of mathematics is a major topic, that includes in particular numerical analysis, towards proofs of scientific computing programs. The present study is about the finite element method, a popular method to numerically solve partial differential equations. In the long-term goal of proving its correctness, we focus here on the formal definition of what is a finite element. Mathematically, a finite element describes what happens in a cell of a mesh. It notably includes the geometry of the cell, the polynomial approximation space, and a finite set of linear forms that computationally characterizes the polynomials. Formally, we design a finite element as a record in the Rocq proof assistant with both values (such as the vertices of the cell) and proofs of validity (such as the dimension of the approximation space). The decisive validity proof is unisolvence, that makes the previous characterization unique. We then instantiate this record with the most popular and useful, the simplicial Lagrange finite elements for evenly distributed nodes, for any dimension and any polynomial degree, including the difficult unisolvence proof. These proofs require many results (definitions, lemmas, canonical structures) about finite families, affine spaces, multivariate polynomials, in the context of finite or infinite-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [78] [Termination of Innermost-Terminating Right-Linear Overlay Term Rewrite Systems (Full Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20754)
*Naoki Nishida*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 对于右线性覆盖项重写系统，终止性与最内终止性等价，通过依赖对链分析证明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究右线性覆盖项重写系统中终止性与最内终止性之间的关系，旨在建立两者之间的等价条件，简化终止性分析

Method: 利用最内归约模拟性质，分析最小依赖对链，证明无限最小依赖对链与无限最内最小依赖对链的存在性等价

Result: 证明了对于右线性覆盖TRS，不存在无限最小依赖对链当且仅当不存在无限最内最小依赖对链，从而得出终止性与最内终止性等价

Conclusion: 右线性覆盖项重写系统的终止性与最内终止性等价，这一结果为终止性分析提供了简化途径

Abstract: It has been shown that, regarding a terminating right-linear overlay term rewrite system (TRS), any rewrite sequence ending with a normal form can be simulated by the innermost reduction. In this paper, using this simulation property, we show that for a right-linear overlay TRS, there is no infinite minimal dependency-pair chain if and only if there is no infinite innermost minimal dependency-pair chain. This implies that a right-linear overlay TRS is terminating if and only if it is innermost terminating.

</details>


### [79] [Formal Primal-Dual Algorithm Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20807)
*Mohammad Abdulaziz,Thomas Ammer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在Isabelle/HOL中构建形式化原对偶论证框架和库，用于算法分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为算法分析中的原对偶论证提供形式化验证框架，确保数学证明的正确性和可靠性

Method: 在Isabelle/HOL定理证明器中构建形式化框架和库，通过匹配算法理论中的经典和现代算法案例进行验证

Result: 开发了形式化原对偶论证框架，实现了匈牙利算法（首个原对偶算法）和Adwords算法（搜索引擎广告分配模型）的形式化验证

Conclusion: 成功构建了Isabelle/HOL中的原对偶论证形式化框架，为算法分析提供了可靠的形式化验证工具

Abstract: We present an ongoing effort to build a framework and a library in Isabelle/HOL for formalising primal-dual arguments for the analysis of algorithms. We discuss a number of example formalisations from the theory of matching algorithms, covering classical algorithms like the Hungarian Method, widely considered the first primal-dual algorithm, and modern algorithms like the Adwords algorithm, which models the assignment of search queries to advertisers in the context of search engines.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [3DPipe: A Pipelined GPU Framework for Scalable Generalized Spatial Join over Polyhedral Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19982)
*Lyuheng Yuan,Da Yan,Akhlaque Ahmad,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 3DPipe是一个用于三维多边形对象空间连接的可扩展GPU流水线框架，相比现有GPU解决方案TDBase实现高达9.0倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着激光雷达物体检测和三维数字病理学等应用中三维数据的快速增长，对三维数据集空间连接的需求日益增加。现有技术主要针对二维数据设计，导致三维空间连接研究不足且计算成本高昂。

Method: 3DPipe是一个流水线GPU框架，在过滤和细化阶段都利用GPU并行性，采用多级剪枝策略进行高效候选缩减，使用分块流式处理处理超出GPU内存的数据集，并通过流水线执行重叠CPU数据准备、主机-设备数据传输和GPU计算以提高吞吐量。

Result: 实验表明，3DPipe相比最先进的GPU解决方案TDBase实现了高达9.0倍的加速，同时保持了优秀的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 3DPipe为三维多边形对象提供了高效可扩展的空间连接解决方案，通过GPU并行化、多级剪枝和流水线执行显著提升了性能，已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Spatial join is a fundamental operation in spatial databases. With the rapid growth of 3D data in applications such as LiDAR-based object detection and 3D digital pathology, there is an increasing need to support spatial join over 3D datasets. However, existing techniques are largely designed for 2D data, leaving 3D spatial join underexplored and computationally expensive. We present 3DPipe, a pipelined GPU framework for scalable spatial join over polyhedral objects. 3DPipe exploits GPU parallelism across both filtering and refinement stages, incorporates a multi-level pruning strategy for efficient candidate reduction, and employs chunked streaming to handle datasets exceeding GPU memory. Its pipelined execution overlaps CPU data preparation, host-device data transfer, and GPU computation to improve throughput. Experiments show that 3DPipe achieves up to 9.0$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art GPU solution, TDBase, while maintaining excellent scalability. 3DPipe is open-sourced at https://github.com/lyuheng/3dpipe.

</details>


### [81] [A GPU-Accelerated Framework for Multi-Attribute Range Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20121)
*Zhonggen Li,Haoran Yu,Yifan Zhu,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Garfield是一个GPU加速的多属性范围过滤近似最近邻搜索框架，通过轻量级索引结构和硬件感知执行流水线解决现有方法的索引膨胀和CPU性能瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范围过滤近似最近邻搜索方法存在严重的索引膨胀和构建开销，且仅依赖CPU进行索引构建和查询处理，未能充分利用GPU的强大计算能力。

Method: 提出GMG索引结构，将数据分区为单元并构建局部图索引，通过添加恒定数量的跨单元边保证线性存储开销；采用集群引导的排序策略重新排序查询相关单元，实现高效的GPU单元遍历；设计面向单元的外核流水线，动态调度单元以最小化每批次活跃查询数量，并重叠GPU计算与CPU到GPU的索引流传输。

Result: Garfield将索引大小减少4.4倍，同时提供比最先进RFANNS方法高119.8倍的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Garfield通过GPU加速和轻量级索引设计，有效解决了范围过滤近似最近邻搜索的索引膨胀和性能瓶颈问题，为现代向量数据库提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (RFANNS) is increasingly critical for modern vector databases. However, existing solutions suffer from severe index inflation and construction overhead. Furthermore, they rely exclusively on CPUs for the heavy indexing and query processing, failing to leverage the powerful computational capabilities of GPUs. In this paper, we present Garfield, a GPU-accelerated framework for multi-attribute range filtered ANNS that overcomes these bottlenecks through designing a lightweight index structure and hardware-aware execution pipeline. Garfield introduces the GMG index, which partitions data into cells and builds local graph indexes. By adding a constant number of cross-cell edges, it guarantees linear storage and indexing overhead. For queries, Garfield utilizes a cluster-guided ordering strategy that reorders query-relevant cells, enabling a highly efficient cell-by-cell traversal on the GPU that aggressively reuses candidates as entry points across cells. To handle datasets exceeding GPU memory, Garfield features a cell-oriented out-of-core pipeline. It dynamically schedules cells to minimize the number of active queries per batch and overlaps GPU computation with CPU-to-GPU index streaming. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Garfield reduces index size by 4.4x, while delivering 119.8x higher throughput than state-of-the-art RFANNS methods.

</details>


### [82] [Pre-Execution Query Slot-Time Prediction in Cloud Data Warehouses: A Feature-Scoped Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20145)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于机器学习的BigQuery时隙时间预测模型，使用SQL复杂度、数据量特征和查询文本特征，在预执行阶段预测查询成本，减少预算超支和调度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据仓库按时隙时间计费，在共享多租户环境中查询成本高度可变且难以在执行前估计，导致预算超支和调度性能下降。静态查询规划器启发式方法无法捕捉复杂的SQL结构、数据倾斜和工作负载争用。

Method: 采用特征范围的机器学习方法，仅使用预执行可观测信号：从SQL操作成本派生的结构化查询复杂度评分、规划器估计和元数据的数据量特征、查询文本的文本特征。使用HistGradientBoostingRegressor训练对数变换的时隙时间，TF-IDF + TruncatedSVD-512文本处理管道融合数值和分类特征。

Result: 在7个部署环境的749个查询上训练，在2个保留环境的746个查询上评估，模型达到MAE 1.17时隙分钟、RMSE 4.71、74%解释方差。对于成本显著的查询（时隙时间≥0.01分钟，N=282），MAE 3.10对比预测均值基线4.95和预测中位数4.54，减少30-37%。长尾查询（≥20分钟，N=22）未超越简单基线。

Conclusion: 模型在大多数查询上有效预测时隙时间，但长尾查询受未观测运行时因素主导。提出复杂度路由的双模型架构作为实用改进，识别缩小长尾差距的方向作为未来工作。

Abstract: Cloud data warehouses bill compute based on slot-time consumed. In shared multi-tenant environments, query cost is highly variable and hard to estimate before execution, causing budget overruns and degraded scheduling. Static query-planner heuristics fail to capture complex SQL structure, data skew, and workload contention. We present a feature-scoped machine learning approach that predicts BigQuery slot-time before execution using only pre-execution observable signals: a structured query complexity score derived from SQL operator costs, data volume features from planner estimates and workload metadata, and textual features from query text. We deliberately exclude runtime factors (slot-pool utilization, cache state, realized skew) unknowable at submission. The model uses a HistGradientBoostingRegressor trained on log-transformed slot-time, with a TF-IDF + TruncatedSVD-512 text pipeline fused with numeric and categorical features. Trained on 749 queries across seven deployment environments and evaluated out-of-distribution on 746 queries from two held-out environments, the model achieves MAE 1.17 slot-minutes, RMSE 4.71, and 74% explained variance on the full workload. On cost-significant queries (slot-time >= 0.01 min, N=282) the model achieves MAE 3.10 versus 4.95 for a predict-mean baseline and 4.54 for predict-median, a 30-37% reduction. On long-tail queries (>= 20 min, N=22) the model does not outperform trivial baselines, consistent with the hypothesis that long-tail queries are dominated by unobserved runtime factors outside the current feature scope. A complexity-routed dual-model architecture is described as a practical refinement, and directions for closing the long-tail gap are identified as future work.

</details>


### [83] [Estimating Power-Law Exponent with Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20274)
*Adam Tan,Mohamed Hefny,Keval Vora*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文研究在边差分隐私下估计幂律度分布的指数α，提出通过发布低维充分统计量而非整个度分布来减少噪声引入的失真，支持离散近似和基于似然的数值优化两种估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实世界图具有近似幂律的度分布，其标度参数α为图分析和系统优化提供了有用信息。当图包含敏感关系数据时，必须在保护个体边隐私的前提下估计α。传统方法通过发布带噪声的度分布再拟合幂律模型会引入高失真，因此需要更有效的隐私保护估计方法。

Method: 提出在边差分隐私下仅发布估计α所需的低维充分统计量，而非整个度分布。支持两种参数估计方法：离散近似和基于似然的数值优化。开发了集中式和本地差分隐私模型下的边差分隐私算法，在本地设置中比较了度发布和对数统计量发布两种策略。

Result: 在多种图数据集、不同隐私预算和尾部截断设置下评估了所提出的方法。结果表明，通过发布低维充分统计量而非整个度分布，能够显著减少噪声引入的失真，在保护隐私的同时获得更准确的幂律指数估计。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种在边差分隐私下估计幂律度分布指数α的有效方法，通过仅发布低维充分统计量避免了传统方法的高失真问题。支持集中式和本地差分隐私模型，为保护敏感图数据隐私的同时进行结构分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs have degree distributions that are well approximated by a power-law, and the corresponding scaling parameter $α$ provides a compact summary of that structure which is useful for graph analysis and system optimization. When graphs contain sensitive relationship data, $α$ must be estimated without revealing information about individual edges. This paper studies power-law exponent estimation under edge differential privacy. Instead of first releasing a noisy degree distribution and then fitting a power-law model, we propose privatizing only the low-dimensional sufficient statistics needed to estimate $α$, thereby avoiding the high distortion introduced by traditional approaches. Using these released statistics, we support both discrete approximation and likelihood-based numerical optimization for efficient parameter estimation. We develop edge-DP algorithms for both centralized and local DP models, compare degree release and log-statistic release in the local setting, and evaluate the resulting methods on various graph datasets across multiple privacy budgets and tail-cutoff settings.

</details>

### [84] [A Hierarchical MARL-Based Approach for Coordinated Retail P2P Trading and Wholesale Market Participation of DERs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20586)
*Patrick Wilk,Ethan Cantor,Yikui Liu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于分层多智能体深度强化学习的市场参与框架，使产消者参与P2P零售拍卖并聚合参与批发市场，通过Stackelberg博弈协调提升市场性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力能源部门向去中心化转型，分布式能源资源（DERs）普及，需要DERs积极参与电力市场以支持电网运营。双向能源和通信流成为标准，智能、易部署、资源节约的需求侧参与对保障电网运行灵活性和市场效率至关重要。

Method: 提出市场参与框架，采用分层多智能体深度强化学习（MARL）方法：1）个体产消者参与点对点零售拍卖；2）聚合智能产消者促进DERs有效参与批发市场；3）使用Stackelberg博弈协调分层MARL框架。

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体实验结果，但框架旨在实现：1）个体产消者参与P2P零售市场；2）DERs有效参与批发市场；3）通过Stackelberg博弈协调提升整体市场性能。

Conclusion: 提出的分层MARL框架结合Stackelberg博弈能够协调DERs市场参与，支持电网运营灵活性，提升市场效率，为去中心化电力能源部门的智能需求侧参与提供解决方案。

Abstract: The ongoing shift towards decentralization of the electric energy sector, driven by the growing electrification across end-use sectors, and widespread adoption of distributed energy resources (DERs), necessitates their active participation in the electricity markets to support grid operations. Furthermore, with bi-directional energy and communication flows becoming standard, intelligent, easy-to-deploy, resource-conservative demand-side participation is expected to play a critical role in securing power grid operational flexibility and market efficiency. This work proposes a market engagement framework that leverages a hierarchical multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MARL) approach to enable individual prosumers to participate in peer-to-peer retail auctions and further aggregate these intelligent prosumers to facilitate effective DER participation in wholesale markets. Ultimately, a Stackelberg game is proposed to coordinate this hierarchical MARL-based DER market participation framework toward enhanced market performance.

</details>


### [85] [Generative Flow Networks for Model Adaptation in Digital Twins of Natural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20707)
*Pascal Archambault,Houari Sahraoui,Eugene Syriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于GFlowNet的数字孪生模型自适应方法，将模型校准转化为生成建模问题，以采样与观测数据一致的多个合理参数配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然系统的数字孪生需要与随时间演变的物理系统保持对齐，这些系统通常只能部分观测，且由参数无法直接测量的机理模拟器建模。稀疏和间接的观测往往无法确定唯一的最优校准，导致多个模拟器参数化方案与可用证据兼容。

Method: 采用GFlowNet方法，将模型自适应表述为完整模拟器配置的生成建模问题，使得合理的参数化方案能够以与模拟行为和观测行为之间一致性奖励成正比的概率进行采样。

Result: 基于机理番茄模型的受控环境农业案例研究表明，学习到的策略能够恢复自适应景观的主要区域，检索强校准假设，并在不确定性下保留多个合理的配置。

Conclusion: GFlowNet方法为数字孪生模型自适应提供了一种有效的生成建模框架，能够在观测不确定的情况下识别和保留多个合理的参数配置，提高数字孪生与物理系统对齐的能力。

Abstract: Digital twins of natural systems must remain aligned with physical systems that evolve over time, are only partially observed, and are typically modeled by mechanistic simulators whose parameters cannot be measured directly. In such settings, model adaptation is naturally posed as a simulation-based inference problem. However, sparse and indirect observations often fail to identify a unique and optimal calibration, leaving several simulator parameterizations compatible with the available evidence. This article presents a GFlowNet-based approach to model adaptation for digital twins of natural systems. We formulate adaptation as a generative modeling problem over complete simulator configurations, so that plausible parameterizations can be sampled with probability proportional to a reward derived from agreement between simulated and observed behavior. Using a controlled environment agriculture case study based on a mechanistic tomato model, we show that the learned policy recovers dominant regions of the adaptation landscape, retrieves strong calibration hypotheses, and preserves multiple plausible configurations under uncertainty.

</details>


### [86] [Fast Bayesian equipment condition monitoring via simulation based inference: applications to heat exchanger health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20735)
*Peter Collett,Alexander Johannes Stasik,Simone Casolo,Signe Riemer-Sørensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于仿真推理（SBI）和摊销神经后验估计的AI驱动框架，用于热交换器的实时故障诊断，相比传统MCMC方法加速82倍，同时保持相当的诊断精度和不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设备的准确状态监测需要从间接传感器测量中推断潜在退化参数，但传统的贝叶斯方法（如MCMC）计算成本过高，无法满足实时过程控制的需求。

Method: 提出基于仿真推理（SBI）的AI框架，利用摊销神经后验估计，通过在模拟数据集上训练神经密度估计器，学习从热流体观测到退化参数完整后验分布的似然无关映射。

Result: 在多种合成结垢和泄漏场景（包括低概率稀疏事件故障）中，SBI框架与MCMC基准相比，实现了相当的诊断精度和可靠的不确定性量化，同时将推理时间加速82倍。

Conclusion: SBI的摊销特性实现了近乎瞬时的推理，为复杂工程系统中的概率故障诊断和数字孪生实现提供了高度可扩展的实时替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate condition monitoring of industrial equipment requires inferring latent degradation parameters from indirect sensor measurements under uncertainty. While traditional Bayesian methods like Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) provide rigorous uncertainty quantification, their heavy computational bottlenecks render them impractical for real-time process control. To overcome this limitation, we propose an AI-driven framework utilizing Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) powered by amortized neural posterior estimation to diagnose complex failure modes in heat exchangers. By training neural density estimators on a simulated dataset, our approach learns a direct, likelihood-free mapping from thermal-fluid observations to the full posterior distribution of degradation parameters. We benchmark this framework against an MCMC baseline across various synthetic fouling and leakage scenarios, including challenging low-probability, sparse-event failures. The results show that SBI achieves comparable diagnostic accuracy and reliable uncertainty quantification, while accelerating inference time by a factor of82$\times$ compared to traditional sampling. The amortized nature of the neural network enables near-instantaneous inference, establishing SBI as a highly scalable, real-time alternative for probabilistic fault diagnosis and digital twin realization in complex engineering systems.

</details>


### [87] [Expert Upcycling: Shifting the Compute-Efficient Frontier of Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19835)
*Chaitanya Dwivedi,Binxuan Huang,Himanshu Gupta,Pratik Jayarao,Neeraj Varshney,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家升级方法，通过在持续预训练中增加专家数量来扩展MoE模型容量，保持推理成本不变，显著节省训练计算资源


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构已成为扩展大语言模型的主流方法，但训练大型MoE模型成本高昂，内存需求和设备间通信都随总参数量增长。需要一种更高效的方法来扩展MoE容量

Method: 专家升级方法：在持续预训练期间通过专家复制和路由器扩展来增加专家数量，保持top-K路由不变。引入基于效用的专家选择，使用基于梯度的重要性分数指导非均匀复制

Result: 在7B-13B总参数实验中，升级模型在验证损失上匹配固定大小基线，同时节省32%的GPU小时。基于效用的专家选择在有限训练预算下将差距缩小三倍以上

Conclusion: 专家升级是一种有原则、计算高效的替代方案，可用于部署大型MoE模型，避免从头开始训练的高成本

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the dominant architecture for scaling large language models: frontier models routinely decouple total parameters from per-token computation through sparse expert routing. Scaling laws show that under fixed active computation, model quality scales predictably with total parameters, and MoEs realize this by increasing expert count. However, training large MoEs is expensive, as memory requirements and inter-device communication both scale with total parameter count. We propose expert upcycling, a method for progressively expanding MoE capacity by increasing the number of experts during continued pre-training (CPT). Given a trained E-expert model, the upcycling operator constructs an mE-expert model through expert duplication and router extension while holding top-K routing fixed, preserving per-token inference cost. Duplication provides a warm initialization: the expanded model inherits the source checkpoint's learned representations, starting from a substantially lower loss than random initialization. Subsequent CPT then breaks the symmetry among duplicated experts to drive specialization. We formalize the upcycling operator and develop a theoretical framework decomposing the quality gap into a capacity term and an initialization term. We further introduce utility-based expert selection, which uses gradient-based importance scores to guide non-uniform duplication, more than tripling gap closure when CPT is limited. In our 7B-13B total parameter experiments, the upcycled model matches the fixed-size baseline on validation loss while saving 32% of GPU hours. Comprehensive ablations across model scales, activation ratios, MoE architectures, and training budgets yield a practical recipe for deploying expert upcycling, establishing it as a principled, compute-efficient alternative to training large MoE models from scratch.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking Reinforcement Fine-Tuning in LVLM: Convergence, Reward Decomposition, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19857)
*Carter Adams,Rafael Oliveira,Gabriel Almeida,Sofia Torres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了工具增强马尔可夫决策过程（TA-MDP）框架，从理论上分析了强化学习微调中可验证奖励的结构对GRPO收敛的影响、奖励分解的优化差距以及工具增强策略的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于可验证奖励的强化微调（RLVR）在视觉语言模型的工具使用和多步推理等智能体能力方面取得了显著经验成功，但其理论基础仍不完善。特别是两个关键问题缺乏严格答案：1）可验证奖励的复合结构如何影响GRPO的收敛；2）为什么在小规模工具增强任务上的训练能迁移到分布外领域。

Method: 提出了工具增强马尔可夫决策过程（TA-MDP）形式化框架，该框架建模了具有有界深度工具调用的多模态智能体决策过程。在此框架内建立了三个主要理论结果：GRPO在复合可验证奖励下的收敛性分析、奖励分解定理、以及工具增强策略的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: 1）证明了GRPO在复合可验证奖励下以O(1/√T)速率收敛到一阶稳定点，收敛率明确依赖于奖励组件数量和组大小；2）推导了奖励分解定理，界定了分解优化与联合优化之间的次优性差距；3）建立了工具增强策略的PAC-Bayes泛化界，解释了Visual-ARFT中观察到的强分布外迁移能力。

Conclusion: TA-MDP框架为基于可验证奖励的强化微调提供了严格的理论基础，解释了复合奖励结构对优化收敛的影响、奖励分解的有效性条件以及工具增强策略的泛化机制，填补了该领域理论理解的空白。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for equipping large vision-language models (LVLMs) with agentic capabilities such as tool use and multi-step reasoning. Despite striking empirical successes, most notably Visual Agentic Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (Visual-ARFT), the theoretical underpinnings of this paradigm remain poorly understood. In particular, two critical questions lack rigorous answers: (i)~how does the composite structure of verifiable rewards (format compliance, answer accuracy, tool executability) affect the convergence of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and (ii)~why does training on a small set of tool-augmented tasks transfer to out-of-distribution domains? We address these gaps by introducing the \emph{Tool-Augmented Markov Decision Process} (TA-MDP), a formal framework that models multimodal agentic decision-making with bounded-depth tool calls. Within this framework, we establish three main results. First, we prove that GRPO under composite verifiable rewards converges to a first-order stationary point at rate $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ with explicit dependence on the number of reward components and group size (\textbf{Theorem~1}). Second, we derive a \emph{Reward Decomposition Theorem} that bounds the sub-optimality gap between decomposed per-component optimization and joint optimization, providing a precise characterization of when reward decomposition is beneficial (\textbf{Theorem~2}). Third, we establish a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for tool-augmented policies that explains the strong out-of-distribution transfer observed in Visual-ARFT (\textbf{Theorem~3}).

</details>


### [89] [A Multi-Plant Machine Learning Framework for Emission Prediction, Forecasting, and Control in Cement Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19903)
*Sheikh Junaid Fayaz,Nestor D. Montiel-Bohorquez,Wilson Ricardo Leal da Silva,Shashank Bishnoi,Matteo Romano,Manuele Gatti,N. M. Anoop Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 开发数据驱动的排放控制框架，利用机器学习预测水泥生产中的NOx排放，通过优化源头控制减少下游SNCR的氨消耗，实现NOx减排34-64%和年节约5.8万美元


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水泥生产是工业空气污染的主要来源之一，年排放约300万吨NOx。行业标准的选择性非催化还原(SNCR)技术存在氨利用率低的问题，导致运行效率低下和试剂成本增加。需要开发更有效的排放控制方法。

Method: 开发数据驱动的排放控制框架，使用来自全球四个水泥厂的大规模运行数据。基准测试九种机器学习架构，分析预测误差变化。通过纳入短期过程历史数据提升NOx预测准确性，开发能够提前9分钟预测NOx超标的模型，为运行调整提供缓冲时间。

Result: 预测误差因数据丰富度差异在不同工厂间变化3-5倍。纳入短期过程历史数据使NOx预测准确性提高近三倍，揭示NOx形成具有显著的过程记忆效应，而CO和CO2则没有这种时间尺度依赖性。模型能够提前9分钟预测NOx超标，通过源头控制NOx形成减少下游SNCR的氨消耗。替代模型预测显示NOx减少34-64%同时保持熟料质量，相当于每年减少290吨NOx排放和节约5.8万美元氨成本。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个可推广的数据驱动排放控制框架，为水泥行业提供了一条无需结构改造或额外硬件即可实现低排放运行的途径。该框架具有潜在适用性，可扩展到其他难以减排的行业如钢铁、玻璃和石灰生产。

Abstract: Cement production is among the largest contributors to industrial air pollution, emitting ~3 Mt NOx/year. The industry-standard mitigation approach, selective non-catalytic reduction (SNCR), exhibits low NH3 utilization efficiency, resulting in operational inefficiencies and increased reagent costs. Here, we develop a data-driven framework for emission control using large-scale operational data from four cement plants worldwide. Benchmarking nine machine learning architectures, we observe that prediction error varies ~3-5x across plants due to variation in data richness. Incorporating short-term process history nearly triples NOx prediction accuracy, revealing that NOx formation carries substantial process memory, a timescale dependence that is absent in CO and CO2. Further, we develop models that forecast NOx overshoots as early as nine minutes, providing a buffer for operational adjustments. The developed framework controls NOx formation at the source, reducing NH3 consumption in downstream SNCR. Surrogate model projections estimate a ~34-64% reduction in NOx while preserving clinker quality, corresponding to a reduction of ~290 t NOx/year and ~58,000 USD/year in NH3 savings. This work establishes a generalizable framework for data-driven emission control, offering a pathway toward low-emission operation without structural modifications or additional hardware, with potential applicability to other hard-to-abate industries such as steel, glass, and lime.

</details>


### [90] [Generalization and Membership Inference Attack a Practical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19936)
*Fateme Rahmani,Mahdi Jafari Siavoshani,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 重新评估成员推理攻击（MIA）成功率和模型泛化能力之间的相关性，发现先进泛化技术可显著降低攻击性能达100倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MIA新评估指标和攻击方法的出现，需要重新评估先前关于MIA成功率和模型泛化能力相关性的假设

Method: 采用经验方法，使用数据增强和早停技术提升模型泛化能力，分析其对MIA成功率的影响；在受控环境中分析超过1000个模型

Result: 先进泛化技术可显著降低MIA攻击性能（最多达100倍）；组合使用这些方法不仅改善模型泛化，还通过引入训练随机性降低攻击有效性

Conclusion: 研究证实了泛化能力对MIA性能的直接影响，挑战了先前关于两者相关性的假设，表明泛化技术可作为有效的隐私保护手段

Abstract: With the emergence of new evaluation metrics and attack methodologies for Membership Inference Attacks (MIA), it becomes essential to reevaluate previously accepted assumptions. In this paper, we revisit the longstanding debate regarding the correlation between MIA success rates and model generalization using an empirical approach. We focused on employing augmentation techniques and early stopping to enhance model generalization and examined their impact on MIA success rates. We found that utilizing advanced generalization techniques can significantly decrease attack performance, potentially by up to 100 times. Moreover, combining these methods not only improves model generalization but also reduces attack effectiveness by introducing randomness during training. Additionally, our study confirmed the direct impact of generalization on MIA performance through an analysis of over 1K models in a controlled environment.

</details>


### [91] [Are LLM Uncertainty and Correctness Encoded by the Same Features? A Functional Dissociation via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19974)
*Het Patel,Tiejin Chen,Hua Wei,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过2x2框架将LLM预测按正确性和置信度划分，使用稀疏自编码器识别与这两个维度相关的特征，发现存在三种功能不同的特征群体：纯不确定性特征（功能必需）、纯错误特征（功能惰性）和混杂特征（有害）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能不确定但正确，或自信但错误，这引发了一个问题：模型的输出级不确定性和实际正确性是由相同的内部机制驱动，还是由不同的特征群体驱动。研究者希望理解不确定性和正确性在模型内部表征中的关系。

Method: 引入2x2框架，将模型预测沿正确性和置信度轴划分，使用稀疏自编码器独立识别与每个维度相关的特征。在Llama-3.1-8B和Gemma-2-9B模型上应用该方法，分析不同特征群体的功能角色。

Result: 识别出三种特征群体：1) 纯不确定性特征（功能必需，抑制会严重降低准确率）；2) 纯错误特征（功能惰性，尽管在正确和错误预测间有显著激活差异，但抑制对准确率影响近零）；3) 混杂特征（有害，针对性抑制可提高1.1%准确率和降低75%熵）。仅用单个中间层的3个混杂特征就能预测模型正确性（AUROC ~0.79），通过选择性弃权可将准确率从62%提升至81%（覆盖率为53%）。

Conclusion: 不确定性和正确性是模型内部不同的现象，这一发现对可解释性和针对性推理时干预具有重要意义。混杂特征的有害性表明可以通过抑制这些特征来改善模型性能，而特征类别的信息区分性为选择性弃权提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models can be uncertain yet correct, or confident yet wrong, raising the question of whether their output-level uncertainty and their actual correctness are driven by the same internal mechanisms or by distinct feature populations. We introduce a 2x2 framework that partitions model predictions along correctness and confidence axes, and uses sparse autoencoders to identify features associated with each dimension independently. Applying this to Llama-3.1-8B and Gemma-2-9B, we identify three feature populations that play fundamentally different functional roles. Pure uncertainty features are functionally essential: suppressing them severely degrades accuracy. Pure incorrectness features are functionally inert: despite showing statistically significant activation differences between correct and incorrect predictions, the majority produce near-zero change in accuracy when suppressed. Confounded features that encode both signals are detrimental to output quality, and targeted suppression of them yields a 1.1% accuracy improvement and a 75% entropy reduction, with effects transferring across the ARC-Challenge and RACE benchmarks. The feature categories are also informationally distinct: the activations of just 3 confounded features from a single mid-network layer predict model correctness (AUROC ~0.79), enabling selective abstention that raises accuracy from 62% to 81% at 53% coverage. The results demonstrate that uncertainty and correctness are distinct internal phenomena, with implications for interpretability and targeted inference-time intervention.

</details>


### [92] [Fast Amortized Fitting of Scientific Signals Across Time and Ensembles via Transferable Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19979)
*Sophia Zorek,Kushal Vyas,Yuhao Liu,David Lenz,Tom Peterka,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将隐式神经表示扩展到时空和多变量信号，通过特征迁移实现跨科学信号的高效可扩展表示，在多个科学领域显著提升重建质量和物理量计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场（隐式神经表示）在建模连续几何方面具有强大能力，但在高维科学场景中存在收敛慢和扩展性挑战的问题，需要更高效的表示方法。

Method: 扩展INR模型处理时空和多变量信号，开发可迁移特征机制，在控制变换机制（几何变换、局部扰动）和高保真科学领域（湍流、流体-材料冲击动力学、天体物理系统）进行验证。

Result: 可迁移特征将目标重建质量所需迭代次数减少一个数量级，早期重建质量提升多个dB（某些情况下超过10dB），梯度相关物理量计算精度持续改善。

Conclusion: 可迁移特征不仅提升了信号保真度，还提高了衍生几何和物理量（如密度梯度和涡度）的计算精度，为科学领域的高维连续表示提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural fields, also known as implicit neural representations (INRs), offer a powerful framework for modeling continuous geometry, but their effectiveness in high-dimensional scientific settings is limited by slow convergence and scaling challenges. In this study, we extend INR models to handle spatiotemporal and multivariate signals and show how INR features can be transferred across scientific signals to enable efficient and scalable representation across time and ensemble runs in an amortized fashion. Across controlled transformation regimes (e.g., geometric transformations and localized perturbations of synthetic fields) and high-fidelity scientific domains-including turbulent flows, fluid-material impact dynamics, and astrophysical systems-we show that transferable features improve not only signal fidelity but also the accuracy of derived geometric and physical quantities, including density gradients and vorticity. In particular, transferable features reduce iterations to reach target reconstruction quality by up to an order of magnitude, increase early-stage reconstruction quality by multiple dB (with gains exceeding 10 dB in some cases), and consistently improve gradient-based physical accuracy.

</details>


### [93] [Continuous Semantic Caching for Low-Cost LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20021)
*Baran Atalar,Xutong Liu,Jinhang Zuo,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于连续查询空间下语义LLM响应缓存的理论框架，通过动态ε-net离散化和核岭回归解决传统离散缓存方法在无限连续嵌入空间中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM用户和查询数量的增长，传统缓存方法假设有限离散查询空间的假设变得不可行，因为真实LLM查询存在于无限连续嵌入空间中。需要建立适用于连续查询空间的语义缓存理论框架。

Method: 采用动态ε-net离散化结合核岭回归，在连续表示空间和离散优化之间建立桥梁。该方法能够形式化地量化估计不确定性，并在连续语义查询邻域中泛化部分反馈。开发了离线学习和在线自适应算法，优化减少缓存响应变更带来的切换成本。

Result: 理论证明在线算法相对于最优连续oracle实现了次线性遗憾界，可简化为离散查询模型的现有界。大量实证评估表明，该框架能很好地近似连续最优缓存，同时相比现有方法减少了计算和切换开销。

Conclusion: 本文建立了首个连续查询空间下语义LLM响应缓存的理论框架，通过动态离散化和核岭回归有效解决了传统离散方法的局限性，在保持理论保证的同时实现了实际性能提升。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly popular, caching responses so that they can be reused by users with semantically similar queries has become a vital strategy for reducing inference costs and latency. Existing caching frameworks have proposed to decide which query responses to cache by assuming a finite, known universe of discrete queries and learning their serving costs and arrival probabilities. As LLMs' pool of users and queries expands, however, such an assumption becomes increasingly untenable: real-world LLM queries reside in an infinite, continuous embedding space. In this paper, we establish the first rigorous theoretical framework for semantic LLM response caching in continuous query space under uncertainty. To bridge the gap between discrete optimization and continuous representation spaces, we introduce dynamic $ε$-net discretization coupled with Kernel Ridge Regression. This design enables the system to formally quantify estimation uncertainty and generalize partial feedback on LLM query costs across continuous semantic query neighborhoods. We develop both offline learning and online adaptive algorithms optimized to reduce switching costs incurred by changing the cached responses. We prove that our online algorithm achieves a sublinear regret bound against an optimal continuous oracle, which reduces to existing bounds for discrete query models. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our framework approximates the continuous optimal cache well while also reducing computational and switching overhead compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [94] [Replicable Bandits with UCB based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20024)
*Rohan Deb,Udaya Ghai,Karan Singh,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机多臂老虎机和线性老虎机的可复制UCB算法，提出了RepUCB和RepLinUCB两种算法，显著降低了可复制性带来的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可复制老虎机算法主要基于消除策略，在线性老虎机中依赖离散化方法，导致维度d和可复制性参数ρ的依赖关系不优。需要开发更高效的乐观算法来降低可复制性的代价。

Method: 1. 针对随机多臂老虎机：提出RepUCB，一种可复制的批处理UCB算法。2. 针对随机线性老虎机：首先提出RepRidge，一种满足置信度保证和ρ-可复制性保证的可复制岭回归估计器；然后基于此设计RepLinUCB算法。

Result: 1. RepUCB的遗憾界为O(K²log²T/ρ² Σ(Δ_a + log(KTlogT)/Δ_a))。2. RepLinUCB的遗憾界为Õ((d + d³/ρ)√T)，相比之前最优结果改进了O(d/ρ)倍。

Conclusion: 提出的乐观可复制算法显著降低了可复制性的代价，RepRidge估计器在其他统计估计场景中也有独立价值。结果表明乐观算法能够有效减少可复制性带来的性能损失。

Abstract: We study replicable algorithms for stochastic multi-armed bandits (MAB) and linear bandits with UCB (Upper Confidence Bound) based exploration. A bandit algorithm is $ρ$-replicable if two executions using shared internal randomness but independent reward realizations, produce the same action sequence with probability at least $1-ρ$. Prior work is primarily elimination-based and, in linear bandits with infinitely many actions, relies on discretization, leading to suboptimal dependence on the dimension $d$ and $ρ$. We develop optimistic alternatives for both settings. For stochastic multi-armed bandits, we propose RepUCB, a replicable batched UCB algorithm and show that it attains a regret $O\!\left(\frac{K^2\log^2 T}{ρ^2}\sum_{a:Δ_a>0}\left(Δ_a+\frac{\log(KT\log T)}{Δ_a}\right)\right)$. For stochastic linear bandits, we first introduce RepRidge, a replicable ridge regression estimator that satisfies both a confidence guarantee and a $ρ$-replicability guarantee. Beyond its role in our bandit algorithm, this estimator and its guarantees may also be of independent interest in other statistical estimation settings. We then use RepRidge to design RepLinUCB, a replicable optimistic algorithm for stochastic linear bandits, and show that its regret is bounded by $\widetilde{O}\!\big(\big(d+\frac{d^3}ρ\big)\sqrt{T}\big)$. This improves the best prior regret guarantee by a factor of $O(d/ρ)$, showing that our optimistic algorithm can substantially reduce the price of replicability.

</details>


### [95] [Federated Learning over Blockchain-Enabled Cloud Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20062)
*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani,Kamal Kant Hiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了联邦学习与区块链技术在云边计算环境中的融合，提出了四维架构分类法，并对比分析了两个前沿框架，最后指出了该领域的主要挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式机器学习模型存在数据泄露、隐私侵犯和合规性风险，而物联网设备和云计算的普及加剧了这些挑战。联邦学习与区块链技术的结合为解决这些安全问题提供了潜在方案。

Method: 提出四维架构分类法（协调框架、共识算法、数据存储实践、信任模型），对现有集成系统进行系统评估。通过对比分析两个前沿框架（MORFLB和FBCI-SHS），并结合文献综述，识别该领域的独特贡献和研究空白。

Result: 建立了系统的分类和评估框架，揭示了联邦学习与区块链集成系统的关键设计维度。通过对比分析，明确了MORFLB在智能交通系统和FBCI-SHS在可持续医疗系统中的具体贡献和局限性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习与区块链的融合为解决数据隐私和安全挑战提供了有效方案，但仍面临适应性、弹性和标准化等方面的挑战。未来需要开发更灵活、鲁棒且标准化的BCFL系统，并拓展到更多应用领域。

Abstract: The rise of IoT devices and the uptake of cloud computing have informed a new era of data-driven intelligence. Traditional centralized machine learning models that require a large volume of data to be stored in a single location have therefore become more susceptible to data breaches, privacy violations, and regulatory non-compliance. This report presents a thorough examination of the merging of Federated Learning (FL) and blockchain technology in a cloud-edge setting, demonstrating it as an effective solution to the stated concerns. We are proposing a detailed four-dimensional architectural categorization that meticulously assesses coordination frameworks, consensus algorithms, data storage practices, and trust models that are significant to these integrated systems. The manuscript presents a comprehensive comparative examination of two cutting-edge frameworks: the Multi-Objectives Reinforcement Federated Learning Blockchain (MORFLB), which is designed for intelligent transportation systems, and the Federated Blockchain-IoT Framework for Sustainable Healthcare Systems (FBCI-SHS), elucidating their distinctive contributions and inherent limitations. Lastly, we engage in a thorough evaluation of the literature that integrates a comparative perspective on current frameworks to discern the singular nature of this research within existing knowledge systems. The manuscript culminates in delineating the principal challenges and offering a strategic framework for prospective research trajectories, emphasizing the advancement of adaptive, resilient, and standardized BCFL systems across diverse application domains.

</details>


### [96] [Maximum Entropy Semi-Supervised Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20074)
*Julien Audiffren,Michal Valko,Alessandro Lazaric,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MESSI算法将最大熵逆强化学习与半监督学习结合，利用专家轨迹和无监督轨迹提升学徒学习性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大熵逆强化学习仅使用专家轨迹，但实际场景中可能存在大量无监督轨迹。如何有效利用这些额外数据提升学徒学习性能是本研究的核心动机。

Method: 提出MESSI算法，将最大熵逆强化学习框架与半监督学习原则结合。通过轨迹间的成对惩罚项将无监督数据整合到MaxEnt-IRL框架中。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和网格世界问题上的实验结果表明，MESSI能够有效利用无监督轨迹，相比传统MaxEnt-IRL算法获得性能提升。

Conclusion: MESSI算法成功地将半监督学习思想融入最大熵逆强化学习框架，通过利用无监督轨迹数据显著提升了学徒学习的性能表现。

Abstract: A popular approach to apprenticeship learning (AL) is to formulate it as an inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) problem. The MaxEnt-IRL algorithm successfully integrates the maximum entropy principle into IRL and unlike its predecessors, it resolves the ambiguity arising from the fact that a possibly large number of policies could match the expert's behavior. In this paper, we study an AL setting in which in addition to the expert's trajectories, a number of unsupervised trajectories is available. We introduce MESSI, a novel algorithm that combines MaxEnt-IRL with principles coming from semi-supervised learning. In particular, MESSI integrates the unsupervised data into the MaxEnt-IRL framework using a pairwise penalty on trajectories. Empirical results in a highway driving and grid-world problems indicate that MESSI is able to take advantage of the unsupervised trajectories and improve the performance of MaxEnt-IRL.

</details>


### [97] [Analysis of Nystrom method with sequential ridge leverage scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20077)
*Daniele Calandriello,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出INK-ESTIMATE算法，通过增量计算岭杠杆分数估计，实现单次遍历核矩阵的大规模核岭回归近似求解，在固定空间预算下提供强近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模核岭回归需要存储大型核矩阵K_t，计算成本高。Nyström方法通过子采样核矩阵列来近似求解，但采样分布影响统计与计算权衡。岭杠杆分数提供强重建保证，但精确计算困难。需要开发能增量近似岭杠杆分数的算法。

Method: 提出INK-ESTIMATE算法：1) 增量计算岭杠杆分数估计；2) 维护核矩阵的小型草图，无需访问历史列，单次遍历即可；3) 空间预算固定，仅依赖于核矩阵的有效维度；4) 在每一步提供中间估计。

Result: 算法提供强近似保证：1) 真实核矩阵与其近似之间的距离；2) 任何时间点近似核岭回归解的统计风险；3) 所有保证在中间步骤都成立；4) 空间复杂度仅依赖于核矩阵有效维度。

Conclusion: INK-ESTIMATE算法通过增量估计岭杠杆分数，实现了大规模核岭回归的高效近似求解，具有单次遍历、固定空间预算和强理论保证的优势，解决了传统方法存储和计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Large-scale kernel ridge regression (KRR) is limited by the need to store a large kernel matrix K_t. To avoid storing the entire matrix K_t, Nystrom methods subsample a subset of columns of the kernel matrix, and efficiently find an approximate KRR solution on the reconstructed matrix. The chosen subsampling distribution in turn affects the statistical and computational tradeoffs. For KRR problems, recent works show that a sampling distribution proportional to the ridge leverage scores (RLSs) provides strong reconstruction guarantees for the approximation. While exact RLSs are as difficult to compute as a KRR solution, we may be able to approximate them well enough. In this paper, we study KRR problems in a sequential setting and introduce the INK-ESTIMATE algorithm, that incrementally computes the RLSs estimates. INK-ESTIMATE maintains a small sketch of K_t, that at each step is used to compute an intermediate estimate of the RLSs. First, our sketch update does not require access to previously seen columns, and therefore a single pass over the kernel matrix is sufficient. Second, the algorithm requires a fixed, small space budget to run dependent only on the effective dimension of the kernel matrix. Finally, our sketch provides strong approximation guarantees on the distance between the true kernel matrix and its approximation, and on the statistical risk of the approximate KRR solution at any time, because all our guarantees hold at any intermediate step.

</details>


### [98] [On the Quantization Robustness of Diffusion Language Models in Coding Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20079)
*Aarav Gupta,Gururaj Deshpande,Chandreyi Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型在低比特量化下比自回归模型更鲁棒，为高效部署提供优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型在代码任务上表现优秀但推理成本高，扩散语言模型提供有界推理成本，但其在训练后量化下的行为研究不足

Method: 研究GPTQ和改进的Hessian-Aware Quantization算法在扩散编码LLM（CoDA）上的应用，并与自回归模型Qwen3-1.7B在标准化评估流程下对比

Result: CoDA在低比特宽度（2-4位）下表现出更大鲁棒性，在HumanEval和MBPP基准上精度下降更小；HAWQ混合精度配置提供精度、延迟和内存的平滑权衡

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型由于更强的量化鲁棒性，可能为高效部署提供优势

Abstract: Auto-regressive Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on coding tasks, but incur high memory and inference costs. Diffusion-based language models (d-LLMs) offer bounded inference cost via iterative denoising, but their behavior under post-training quantization (PTQ) has been sparsely explored. We investigate the application and robustness of PTQ techniques, specifically GPTQ and a modified Hessian-Aware Quantization (HAWQ) algorithm, on a diffusion-based coding LLM (CoDA) and observe that these methods applied to CoDA exhibit greater robustness at low bitwidths compared to Qwen3-1.7B, its auto-regressive counterpart, under a standardized evaluation pipeline. We find that in our setup, CoDA exhibits greater robustness at low bitwidths (2-4 bits), with smaller accuracy degradation across HumanEval and MBPP benchmarks. Additionally, mixed-precision configurations derived from HAWQ provide smooth trade-offs across accuracy, latency, and memory. The results suggest that diffusion LLMs may offer advantages for efficient deployment due to more quantization-resilience.

</details>


### [99] [Differentially Private Clustered Federated Learning with Privacy-Preserving Initialization and Normality-Driven Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20596)
*Jie Xu,Haaris Mehmood,Rogier Van Dalen,Karthikeyan Saravanan,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINA：一个两阶段框架，在差分隐私保护下实现高效聚类联邦学习，通过LoRA适配器微调和压缩草图共享解决噪声干扰问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习在数据异构场景下收敛慢、泛化差，聚类联邦学习(CFL)通过用户分簇降低簇内异构性，但结合差分隐私(DP)时面临挑战：DP噪声使客户端更新过度嘈杂，服务器无法用低噪声聚合更新初始化簇中心

Method: 提出PINA两阶段框架：第一阶段让每个客户端微调轻量级LoRA适配器，并私有共享压缩更新草图；服务器利用这些草图构建鲁棒的簇中心。第二阶段引入正态性驱动的聚合机制，提升收敛性和鲁棒性

Result: 在隐私预算ε∈{2,8}下，PINA平均比最先进的DP-FL算法准确率提升2.9%，在保持聚类联邦学习优势的同时提供对不可信服务器的正式隐私保证

Conclusion: PINA成功解决了差分隐私与聚类联邦学习的兼容性问题，通过压缩草图共享和正态性驱动聚合，在保护隐私的同时有效处理数据异构性，实现了更好的模型性能

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables training of a global model while keeping raw data on end-devices. Despite this, FL has shown to leak private user information and thus in practice, it is often coupled with methods such as differential privacy (DP) and secure vector sum to provide formal privacy guarantees to its participants. In realistic cross-device deployments, the data are highly heterogeneous, so vanilla federated learning converges slowly and generalizes poorly. Clustered federated learning (CFL) mitigates this by segregating users into clusters, leading to lower intra-cluster data heterogeneity. Nevertheless, coupling CFL with DP remains challenging: the injected DP noise makes individual client updates excessively noisy, and the server is unable to initialize cluster centroids with the less noisy aggregated updates. To address this challenge, we propose PINA, a two-stage framework that first lets each client fine-tune a lightweight low-rank adaptation (LoRA) adapter and privately share a compressed sketch of the update. The server leverages these sketches to construct robust cluster centroids. In the second stage, PINA introduces a normality-driven aggregation mechanism that improves convergence and robustness. Our method retains the benefits of clustered FL while providing formal privacy guarantees against an untrusted server. Extensive evaluations show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art DP-FL algorithms by an average of 2.9% in accuracy for privacy budgets (epsilon in {2, 8}).

</details>


### [100] [Energy-Based Open-Set Active Learning for Object Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20083)
*Zongyao Lyu,William J. Beksi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于能量的双阶段框架用于开放集主动学习，通过两个专门的能量模型分别过滤未知类别样本和评估已知样本的信息量，在开放集环境下实现更高效的标注预算利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动学习在封闭集假设下工作，但现实世界往往是开放集环境，未标注数据包含已知和未知类别。标准AL技术会错误地查询未知类别样本，导致标注预算浪费。

Method: 提出双阶段能量框架：1) 基于能量的已知/未知分离器，过滤可能属于未知类别的样本；2) 基于能量的样本评分器，评估过滤后已知样本的信息量。利用能量景观区分已知和未知样本，已知样本分配较低能量，未知样本分配较高能量。

Result: 在2D（CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet）和3D（ModelNet40）物体分类基准测试中，该框架优于现有方法，在开放集环境下实现了更优的标注效率和分类性能。

Conclusion: 提出的双阶段能量框架有效解决了开放集主动学习中的挑战，通过专门的能量模型区分已知/未知类别并评估样本信息量，显著提高了标注预算的利用效率和模型性能。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) has emerged as a crucial methodology for minimizing labeling costs in deep learning by selecting the most valuable samples from a pool of unlabeled data for annotation. Traditional AL operates under a closed-set assumption, where all classes in the dataset are known and consistent. However, real-world scenarios often present open-set conditions in which unlabeled data contains both known and unknown classes. In such environments, standard AL techniques struggle. They can mistakenly query samples from unknown categories, leading to inefficient use of annotation budgets. In this paper, we propose a novel dual-stage energy-based framework for open-set AL. Our method employs two specialized energy-based models (EBMs). The first, an energy-based known/unknown separator, filters out samples likely to belong to unknown classes. The second, an energy-based sample scorer, assesses the informativeness of the filtered known samples. Using the energy landscape, our models distinguish between data points from known and unknown classes in the unlabeled pool by assigning lower energy to known samples and higher energy to unknown samples, ensuring that only samples from classes of interest are selected for labeling. By integrating these components, our approach ensures efficient and targeted sample selection, maximizing learning impact in each iteration. Experiments on 2D (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet) and 3D (ModelNet40) object classification benchmarks demonstrates that our framework outperforms existing approaches, achieving superior annotation efficiency and classification performance in open-set environments.

</details>


### [101] [Differentiable Conformal Training for LLM Reasoning Factuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20098)
*Nathan Hittesdorf,Marco Salzetta,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCF提出了一种可微的连贯事实性方法，通过可微松弛改进评分器，在保持可靠性保证的同时显著提高真实声明的保留率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Coherent Factuality方法不可微，需要手工设计评分器，在高可靠性水平下会移除近60%的真实声明，限制了其实际应用效果

Method: 提出Differentiable Coherent Factuality (DCF)，通过完全可微的松弛方法，使评分器能够学习改进，同时可证明地恢复原始算法的保证

Result: 在两个基准推理数据集上的实验表明，DCF在保持可靠性保证的同时，实现了高达141%的声明保留率提升

Conclusion: DCF代表了向可靠的一致性LLM系统迈出的重要一步，通过可微方法显著提高了真实声明的保留率

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently hallucinate, limiting their reliability in critical applications. Conformal Prediction (CP) addresses this by calibrating error rates on held-out data to provide statistically valid confidence guarantees. Recent work extends CP to LLM factuality to filter out risky claims, ensuring that hallucination rates remain below a user-specified level (e.g., 10%). While prior methods treat claims independently, Coherent Factuality extends to multi-step reasoning by representing outputs as dependency graphs and jointly validating claims with their logical ancestors. A key limitation is that Coherent Factuality is not differentiable, requiring hand-crafted scorers that at high reliability levels remove nearly 60% of true claims. We introduce Differentiable Coherent Factuality (DCF), a fully differentiable relaxation that enables learning improved scorers while provably recovering the original algorithm's guarantees. Experiments on two benchmark reasoning datasets demonstrate DCF achieves up to 141% improvement in claim retention while maintaining reliability guarantees, representing a significant step towards reliable conformal LLM systems.

</details>


### [102] [Meta Additive Model: Interpretable Sparse Learning With Auto Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20111)
*Xuelin Zhang,Xinyue Liu,Lingjuan Wu,Hong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于双层优化框架的元加性模型(MAM)，通过MLP参数化损失权重函数，实现数据驱动的样本重加权，提升稀疏加性模型在复杂噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏加性模型大多基于均方误差准则的单层学习，在非高斯扰动、离群值、噪声标签和类别不平衡等复杂噪声存在时性能显著下降。样本重加权策略需要预设权重函数和手动选择超参数。

Method: 提出元加性模型(MAM)，采用双层优化框架：内层学习稀疏加性模型，外层通过MLP参数化的权重函数学习数据驱动的损失权重。该框架能同时处理变量选择、鲁棒回归估计和不平衡分类等任务。

Result: 理论证明MAM在温和条件下具有计算收敛性、算法泛化性和变量选择一致性保证。实证表明MAM在各种数据污染下的合成和真实数据上均优于现有最先进的加性模型。

Conclusion: MAM通过双层优化框架实现了数据驱动的样本重加权，显著提升了稀疏加性模型在复杂噪声环境下的鲁棒性和性能，为高维数据分析提供了更灵活有效的工具。

Abstract: Sparse additive models have attracted much attention in high-dimensional data analysis due to their flexible representation and strong interpretability. However, most existing models are limited to single-level learning under the mean-squared error criterion, whose empirical performance can degrade significantly in the presence of complex noise, such as non-Gaussian perturbations, outliers, noisy labels, and imbalanced categories. The sample reweighting strategy is widely used to reduce the model's sensitivity to atypical data; however, it typically requires prespecifying the weighting functions and manually selecting additional hyperparameters. To address this issue, we propose a new meta additive model (MAM) based on the bilevel optimization framework, which learns data-driven weighting of individual losses by parameterizing the weighting function via an MLP trained on meta data. MAM is capable of a variety of learning tasks, including variable selection, robust regression estimation, and imbalanced classification. Theoretically, MAM provides guarantees on convergence in computation, algorithmic generalization, and variable selection consistency under mild conditions. Empirically, MAM outperforms several state-of-the-art additive models on both synthetic and real-world data under various data corruptions.

</details>


### [103] [On the Stability and Generalization of First-order Bilevel Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20115)
*Xuelin Zhang,Peipei Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对具有下层极小极大问题的梯度基双层极小极大求解器进行系统化泛化分析，揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置间的精确权衡关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献主要关注双层优化和双层极小极大优化的经验效率和收敛保证，但缺乏对这些算法泛化能力的理论理解。为填补这一关键理论空白，需要对基于梯度的双层极小极大求解器进行系统化泛化分析。

Method: 利用算法稳定性论证，为三种代表性算法推导细粒度的泛化边界：单时间尺度随机梯度下降-上升法，以及两种变体的双时间尺度随机梯度下降-上升法。

Result: 揭示了算法稳定性、泛化差距与实际设置之间的精确权衡关系。在具有双层极小极大结构的实际优化任务上进行了广泛的实证评估，验证了理论见解。

Conclusion: 首次为具有下层极小极大问题的梯度基双层极小极大求解器提供了系统化泛化分析，填补了现有文献的理论空白，为理解这些算法的泛化性能提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization and bilevel minimax optimization have recently emerged as unifying frameworks for a range of machine-learning tasks, including hyperparameter optimization and reinforcement learning. The existing literature focuses on empirical efficiency and convergence guarantees, leaving a critical theoretical gap in understanding how well these algorithms generalize. To bridge this gap, we provide the first systematic generalization analysis for first-order gradient-based bilevel minimax solvers with lower-level minimax problems. Specifically, by leveraging algorithmic stability arguments, we derive fine-grained generalization bounds for three representative algorithms, including single-timescale stochastic gradient descent-ascent, and two variants of two-timescale stochastic gradient descent-ascent. Our results reveal a precise trade-off among algorithmic stability, generalization gaps, and practical settings. Furthermore, extensive empirical evaluations corroborate our theoretical insights on realistic optimization tasks with bilevel minimax structures.

</details>


### [104] [Adaptive Conformal Anomaly Detection with Time Series Foundation Models for Signal Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20122)
*Natalia Martinez Gil,Fearghal O'Donncha,Wesley M. Gifford,Nianjun Zhou,Dhaval C. Patel,Roman Vaculin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种后验自适应保形异常检测方法，利用预训练基础模型预测监控时间序列，无需微调，提供可解释的异常分数（p值），实现稳定误报控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决工业场景中数据有限、缺乏训练专业知识、需要即时推理等挑战，同时利用日益普及的时间序列基础模型，提供透明可操作的决策支持。

Method: 采用加权分位数保形预测边界，自适应地从历史预测中学习最优权重参数，在分布漂移下实现校准并保持稳定误报控制，同时保留样本外保证。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在性能、简单性、可解释性、鲁棒性和自适应性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该方法为时间序列异常检测提供了一种模型无关、易于部署的解决方案，特别适合资源受限环境，能够有效利用基础模型优势解决实际工业问题。

Abstract: We propose a post-hoc adaptive conformal anomaly detection method for monitoring time series that leverages predictions from pre-trained foundation models without requiring additional fine-tuning. Our method yields an interpretable anomaly score directly interpretable as a false alarm rate (p-value), facilitating transparent and actionable decision-making. It employs weighted quantile conformal prediction bounds and adaptively learns optimal weighting parameters from past predictions, enabling calibration under distribution shifts and stable false alarm control, while preserving out-of-sample guarantees. As a model-agnostic solution, it integrates seamlessly with foundation models and supports rapid deployment in resource-constrained environments. This approach addresses key industrial challenges such as limited data availability, lack of training expertise, and the need for immediate inference, while taking advantage of the growing accessibility of time series foundation models. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that the proposed approach delivers strong performance, combining simplicity, interpretability, robustness, and adaptivity.

</details>


### [105] [Occupancy Reward Shaping: Improving Credit Assignment for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20627)
*Aravind Venugopal,Jiayu Chen,Xudong Wu,Chongyi Zheng,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Occupancy Reward Shaping方法，利用世界模型中的时间信息进行信用分配，在稀疏奖励的长时域任务中显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作与长期结果之间的时间延迟使得从数据中学习目标导向行为时的信用分配变得困难。生成世界模型捕获了智能体可能访问的未来状态分布，表明它们包含了时间信息，但如何提取这些时间信息进行信用分配是一个挑战。

Method: 通过最优传输理论形式化世界模型中存储的时间信息如何编码底层世界几何结构。从学习的占用测度模型中提取这种几何结构，转化为捕获目标到达信息的奖励函数，即Occupancy Reward Shaping方法。

Result: ORS在13个多样化的长时域运动和控制任务中平均提升性能2.2倍，在3个托卡马克核聚变控制任务中展示了现实世界有效性。理论上证明不改变最优策略。

Conclusion: Occupancy Reward Shaping方法有效缓解了稀疏奖励设置中的信用分配问题，通过提取世界模型中的时间几何信息来塑造奖励，在模拟和现实任务中均表现出显著性能提升。

Abstract: The temporal lag between actions and their long-term consequences makes credit assignment a challenge when learning goal-directed behaviors from data. Generative world models capture the distribution of future states an agent may visit, indicating that they have captured temporal information. How can that temporal information be extracted to perform credit assignment? In this paper, we formalize how the temporal information stored in world models encodes the underlying geometry of the world. Leveraging optimal transport, we extract this geometry from a learned model of the occupancy measure into a reward function that captures goal-reaching information. Our resulting method, Occupancy Reward Shaping, largely mitigates the problem of credit assignment in sparse reward settings. ORS provably does not alter the optimal policy, yet empirically improves performance by 2.2x across 13 diverse long-horizon locomotion and manipulation tasks. Moreover, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ORS in the real world for controlling nuclear fusion on 3 Tokamak control tasks.
  Code: https://github.com/aravindvenu7/occupancy_reward_shaping; Website: https://aravindvenu7.github.io/website/ors/

</details>


### [106] [Fourier Weak SINDy: Spectral Test Function Selection for Robust Model Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20141)
*Zhiheng Chen,Urban Fasel,Anastasia Bizyaeva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fourier Weak SINDy方法，结合弱形式稀疏方程学习和谱密度估计，用于无导数、抗噪声、可解释的方程学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方程学习方法对噪声敏感且需要导数估计，需要开发一种既能处理噪声数据又无需导数计算的鲁棒方法

Method: 结合弱形式稀疏回归与谱密度估计：使用正弦测试函数将弱形式回归转化为傅里叶系数回归，通过多锥谱估计选择主导频率

Result: 在多个混沌和超混沌ODE基准测试中验证了方法的有效性，展示了噪声鲁棒性和可解释性

Conclusion: Fourier Weak SINDy将弱形式学习与谱估计统一为紧凑灵活框架，为数据驱动方程发现提供了噪声鲁棒的无导数方法

Abstract: We introduce Fourier Weak SINDy, a minimal noise-robust and interpretable derivative-free equation learning method that combines weak-form sparse equation learning with spectral density estimation for data-driven test function selection. By using orthogonal sinusoidal test functions inspired by their prevalence in Modulating Function-based system identification, the weak-form sparse regression problem reduces to a regression over Fourier coefficients. Dominant frequencies are then selected via multitaper estimation of the frequency spectrum of the data. This formulation unifies weak-form learning and spectral estimation within a compact and flexible framework. We illustrate the effectiveness of this approach in numerical experiments across multiple chaotic and hyperchaotic ODE benchmarks.

</details>


### [107] [Temporally Extended Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20156)
*Zeyu Shen,Peter Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将强化学习中的选项框架应用于MoE模型，通过添加控制器学习何时切换专家集，将切换率从50%以上降至5%以下，同时保持90%的基础模型准确率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE模型在每个token处频繁切换专家，当模型超出GPU内存时，这种频繁切换使得卸载和预取等优化失效。需要一种方法来减少专家切换频率，提高内存效率

Method: 基于选项-批评框架和深思熟虑成本，为每个层添加控制器，学习何时切换专家集以及加载哪些专家。应用于GPT-OSS-20B模型，使用低秩适配器和自蒸馏奖励

Result: 在MATH、MMLU和MMMLU基准测试中，将切换率从50%以上降至5%以下，同时保持高达90%的基础模型准确率。深思熟虑成本允许在切换率和能力之间进行权衡

Conclusion: 基于选项框架的时延扩展MoE层为现有预训练模型提供了轻量级转换方法，为不断增长的MoE模型的内存高效服务和持续学习开辟了基于原则的路径

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts models, now popular for scaling capacity at fixed inference speed, switch experts at nearly every token. Once a model outgrows available GPU memory, this churn can render optimizations like offloading and pre-fetching ineffective. We make the case that the options framework in reinforcement learning is a perfect match to tackle this problem, and argue for temporally extended mixture-of-experts layers. Building on the option-critic framework with deliberation costs, we add a controller to each layer that learns when to switch expert sets and which to load. By applying this to gpt-oss-20b with low-rank adapters and a self-distillation reward, our method reduces switch rates from over 50% to below 5% while retaining up to 90% of base-model accuracy on MATH, MMLU, and MMMLU. This shows that even existing pre-trained models can be converted to temporally extended MoEs with lightweight training, with the deliberation cost allowing model trainers to trade off switching rates against capability. We hope this opens a principled path, grounded in the options framework, for memory-efficient serving and continual learning in ever-growing MoE models.

</details>


### [108] [Cover meets Robbins while Betting on Bounded Data: $\ln n$ Regret and Almost Sure $\ln\ln n$ Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20172)
*Shubhada Agrawal,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的混合投注策略，结合Robbins和Cover的思想，在几乎所有路径上实现O(ln ln n)的遗憾，同时在对抗性数据上保持O(ln n)的最坏情况遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cover的通用投资组合算法在最坏情况下提供O(ln n)的遗憾，但这是对抗性数据下的最优界限。然而，在随机数据（条件均值等于m0且内在方差趋于无穷）下，有可能实现更低的遗憾。本文旨在设计一种策略，既能适应随机数据实现几乎确定的O(ln ln n)遗憾，又能保护对抗性数据下的O(ln n)最坏情况遗憾。

Method: 提出一种新颖的混合投注策略，结合了Robbins和Cover的见解。该策略通过将两种截然不同的策略进行对冲，实现对随机数据的最佳适应性和对抗性数据的保护。具体来说，策略在几乎所有路径（测度为1的路径集）上表现出O(ln ln n)的遗憾，而在补集（测度为0的路径集）上保持O(ln n)的遗憾。

Result: 1. 在几乎所有路径上（当每个条件均值等于m0且内在方差趋于无穷时），策略产生O(ln ln n)的遗憾。2. 在补集（测度为0的路径集）上，策略保持O(ln n)的遗憾。3. 实现了对随机数据的最佳适应性和对抗性数据的保护之间的最佳权衡。4. 策略见证了一个尖锐的博弈论上界迭代对数定律，类似于Shafer(2005)的工作。

Conclusion: 本文首次指出通过对冲两种截然不同的策略，可以实现对随机数据的最佳适应性和对抗性数据的保护之间的最佳权衡。这种混合策略在几乎所有路径上实现O(ln ln n)的遗憾，同时保持对抗性数据下的O(ln n)最坏情况遗憾。与Agrawal等人(2025)在无界数据上的次高斯混合模型相比，本文的策略在保持最优投注增长率的同时，在随机数据上实现了几乎确定的ln ln n遗憾。该策略还见证了一个尖锐的博弈论上界迭代对数定律。

Abstract: Consider betting against a sequence of data in $[0,1]$, where one is allowed to make any bet that is fair if the data have a conditional mean $m_0 \in (0,1)$. Cover's universal portfolio algorithm delivers a worst-case regret of $O(\ln n)$ compared to the best constant bet in hindsight, and this bound is unimprovable against adversarially generated data. In this work, we present a novel mixture betting strategy that combines insights from Robbins and Cover, and exhibits a different behavior: it eventually produces a regret of $O(\ln \ln n)$ on \emph{almost} all paths (a measure-one set of paths if each conditional mean equals $m_0$ and intrinsic variance increases to $\infty$), but has an $O(\log n)$ regret on the complement (a measure zero set of paths). Our paper appears to be the first to point out the value in hedging two very different strategies to achieve a best-of-both-worlds adaptivity to stochastic data and protection against adversarial data. We contrast our results to those in~\cite{agrawal2025regret} for a sub-Gaussian mixture on unbounded data: their worst-case regret has to be unbounded, but a similar hedging delivers both an optimal betting growth-rate and an almost sure $\ln\ln n$ regret on stochastic data. Finally, our strategy witnesses a sharp game-theoretic upper law of the iterated logarithm, analogous to~\cite{shafer2005probability}.

</details>


### [109] [Lever: Inference-Time Policy Reuse under Support Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20174)
*Ihor Vitenki,Noha Ibrahim,Sihem Amer-Yahia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEVER框架通过行为嵌入检索预训练策略，离线Q值组合构建新策略，在确定性GridWorld环境中实现接近或优于从头训练的性能，但长时依赖场景存在局限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL策略针对固定目标训练，难以适应任务需求变化。研究如何在给定预训练策略库和新复合目标的情况下，完全离线构建高质量策略，无需额外环境交互

Method: 提出LEVER框架：1) 检索相关策略；2) 使用行为嵌入评估策略；3) 通过离线Q值组合构建新策略。专注于支持有限机制（无价值传播），提出控制候选策略探索的组合策略以平衡性能与计算成本

Result: 在确定性GridWorld环境中，推理时组合策略的性能可匹配甚至超过从头训练，同时提供显著加速。但在需要价值传播的长时依赖场景中性能下降，揭示了离线重用的基本限制

Conclusion: 推理时策略重用是可行的，其有效性关键取决于可用转移的覆盖范围。LEVER框架在支持有限机制中有效，但长时依赖需要价值传播的场景存在根本性限制，为离线策略重用提供了理论边界

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are typically trained for fixed objectives, making reuse difficult when task requirements change. We study inference-time policy reuse: given a library of pre-trained policies and a new composite objective, can a high-quality policy be constructed entirely offline, without additional environment interaction? We introduce lever (Leveraging Efficient Vector Embeddings for Reusable policies), an end-to-end framework that retrieves relevant policies, evaluates them using behavioral embeddings, and composes new policies via offline Q-value composition. We focus on the support-limited regime, where no value propagation is possible, and show that the effectiveness of reuse depends critically on the coverage of available transitions. To balance performance and computational cost, lever proposes composition strategies that control the exploration of candidate policies. Experiments in deterministic GridWorld environments show that inference-time composition can match, and in some cases exceed, training-from-scratch performance while providing substantial speedups. At the same time, performance degrades when long-horizon dependencies require value propagation, highlighting a fundamental limitation of offline reuse.

</details>


### [110] [Structure-Aware Variational Learning of a Class of Generalized Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20188)
*Yubin Lu,Xiaofan Li,Chun Liu,Qi Tang,Yiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于能量耗散原理的结构感知学习框架，用于从部分噪声观测中推断广义扩散过程的未知势函数，相比传统方法具有更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分噪声观测中学习随机梯度系统的底层势能是物理、化学和数据驱动建模中的基本问题。经典方法通常依赖于直接回归控制方程或速度场，这些方法对噪声和外部扰动敏感，且在观测不完整时可能失效。

Method: 提出基于能量变分方法的能量学习框架。从与Fokker-Planck方程相关的能量耗散定律出发，基于De Giorgi耗散泛函构建损失函数，将系统的自由能和耗散机制一致耦合。该公式避免显式强制执行控制偏微分方程，并保持动力学的底层变分结构。

Result: 通过一维、二维和三维数值实验证明，所提出的基于能量的损失函数在观测时间、噪声水平、可用训练数据的多样性和数量方面表现出增强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 能量耗散原理作为从数据中学习随机扩散动力学的可靠基础是有效的。该方法避免了传统回归方法的局限性，通过保持系统的变分结构提高了学习性能。

Abstract: Learning the underlying potential energy of stochastic gradient systems from partial and noisy observations is a fundamental problem arising in physics, chemistry, and data-driven modeling. Classical approaches often rely on direct regression of governing equations or velocity fields, which can be sensitive to noise and external perturbations and may fail when observations are incomplete. In this work, we propose a structure-aware, energy-based learning framework for inferring unknown potential functions in generalized diffusion processes, grounded in the energetic variational approach. Starting from the energy-dissipation law associated with the Fokker-Planck equation, we construct loss functions based on the De Giorgi dissipation functional, which consistently couple the free energy and the dissipation mechanism of the system. This formulation avoids explicit enforcement of the governing partial differential equation and preserves the underlying variational structure of the dynamics. Through numerical experiments in one, two, and three dimensions, we demonstrate that the proposed energy-based loss exhibits enhanced robustness with respect to observation time, noise level, and the diversity and amount of available training data. These results highlight the effectiveness of energy-dissipation principles as a reliable foundation for learning stochastic diffusion dynamics from data.

</details>


### [111] [ACT: Anti-Crosstalk Learning for Cross-Sectional Stock Ranking via Temporal Disentanglement and Structural Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20204)
*Juntao Li,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ACT框架通过时间解耦和结构净化解决股票横截面排名中的串扰问题，在CSI300和CSI500上实现最先进的排名准确性和投资组合表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在股票横截面排名中面临串扰问题：时间尺度串扰（趋势、波动、冲击在共享表示中纠缠）和结构串扰（异质关系被不加区分地融合）。这些串扰导致非可转移的局部模式污染跨股票学习，关系特定的预测信号被掩盖。

Method: 提出Anti-CrossTalk (ACT)框架：1) 将每个股票序列分解为趋势、波动和冲击分量；2) 通过专用分支提取分量特定信息，解耦非可转移的局部模式；3) 引入渐进式结构净化编码器，在缓解时间尺度串扰后对趋势分量进行结构净化；4) 自适应融合模块整合所有分支表示进行排名。

Result: 在CSI300和CSI500上的实验表明，ACT实现了最先进的排名准确性和卓越的投资组合表现，在CSI300数据集上改进高达74.25%。

Conclusion: ACT框架通过时间解耦和结构净化有效解决了股票横截面排名中的串扰问题，显著提升了模型性能和投资组合表现，为量化投资中的股票排名任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-sectional stock ranking is a fundamental task in quantitative investment, relying on both temporal modeling of individual stocks and the capture of inter-stock dependencies. While existing deep learning models leverage graph-based approaches to enhance ranking accuracy by propagating information over relational graphs, they suffer from a key challenge: crosstalk, namely unintended information interference across predictive factors. We identify two forms of crosstalk: temporal-scale crosstalk, where trends, fluctuations, and shocks are entangled in a shared representation and non-transferable local patterns contaminate cross-stock learning; and structural crosstalk, where heterogeneous relations are indiscriminately fused and relation-specific predictive signals are obscured. To address both issues, we propose the Anti-CrossTalk (ACT) framework for cross-sectional stock ranking via temporal disentanglement and structural purification. Specifically, ACT first decomposes each stock sequence into trend, fluctuation, and shock components, then extracts component-specific information through dedicated branches, which effectively decouples non-transferable local patterns. ACT further introduces a Progressive Structural Purification Encoder to sequentially purify structural crosstalk on the trend component after mitigating temporal-scale crosstalk. An adaptive fusion module finally integrates all branch representations for ranking. Experiments on CSI300 and CSI500 demonstrate that ACT achieves state-of-the-art ranking accuracy and superior portfolio performance, with improvements of up to 74.25% on the CSI300 dataset.

</details>


### [112] [Scaling Self-Play with Self-Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20209)
*Luke Bailey,Kaiyue Wen,Kefan Dong,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Tengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Self-Guided Self-Play (SGS)算法解决LLM自博弈中的奖励黑客问题，通过引入Guide角色评估合成问题的质量，在Lean4定理证明任务中显著提升学习效率和扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自博弈方法在大量计算资源下无法持续扩展，会达到学习平台期。这是因为Conjecturer模型会学习"奖励黑客"行为，生成人为复杂但对Solver改进无益的问题，导致训练退化。

Method: 提出Self-Guided Self-Play (SGS)算法，让语言模型承担三个角色：Solver（求解器）、Conjecturer（问题生成器）和Guide（指导器）。Guide评估合成问题对未解决目标问题的相关性、清晰度和自然性，为Conjecturer提供监督以防止退化。核心假设是语言模型能够评估子问题对实现目标的实用性。

Result: 在Lean4形式化定理证明任务中，SGS在少于80轮自博弈中超越了最强RL基线的渐近求解率。经过200轮自博弈后，7B参数模型解决的问题数量超过了671B参数模型的pass@4性能。通过拟合扩展定律到累积求解率曲线，验证了SGS的扩展特性。

Conclusion: SGS通过语言模型自身的指导机制有效防止了自博弈中的奖励黑客问题，实现了更好的学习扩展性，使较小模型能够超越极大模型的性能，为LLM自博弈训练提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM self-play algorithms are notable in that, in principle, nothing bounds their learning: a Conjecturer model creates problems for a Solver, and both improve together. However, in practice, existing LLM self-play methods do not scale well with large amounts of compute, instead hitting learning plateaus. We argue this is because over long training runs, the Conjecturer learns to hack its reward, collapsing to artificially complex problems that do not help the Solver improve. To overcome this, we introduce Self-Guided Self-Play (SGS), a self-play algorithm in which the language model itself guides the Conjecturer away from degeneracy. In SGS, the model takes on three roles: Solver, Conjecturer, and a Guide that scores synthetic problems by their relevance to unsolved target problems and how clean and natural they are, providing supervision against Conjecturer collapse. Our core hypothesis is that language models can assess whether a subproblem is useful for achieving a goal. We evaluate the scaling properties of SGS by running training for significantly longer than prior works and by fitting scaling laws to cumulative solve rate curves. Applying SGS to formal theorem proving in Lean4, we find that it surpasses the asymptotic solve rate of our strongest RL baseline in fewer than 80 rounds of self-play and enables a 7B parameter model, after 200 rounds of self-play, to solve more problems than a 671B parameter model pass@4.

</details>


### [113] [Machine Learning for Two-Stage Graph Sparsification for the Travelling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20236)
*Bo-Cheng Lin,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 两阶段图稀疏化方法：第一阶段结合α-Nearest和POPMUSIC最大化召回率，第二阶段训练模型降低密度，显著减少候选图密度同时保持高覆盖率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能TSP求解器在稀疏化候选图上搜索，但现有启发式方法（α-Nearest和POPMUSIC）无法在所有实例大小和分布上同时保持稀疏性和可靠性。机器学习方法有潜力学习更好的稀疏化模型，但现有方法在完整图上操作成本高且主要限于欧氏距离。

Method: 提出两阶段图稀疏化方法：第一阶段取α-Nearest和POPMUSIC的并集以最大化召回率；第二阶段训练单个模型来减少密度。该方法在四个TSPLIB距离类型、五种空间分布和50-500节点规模上进行实验。

Result: 两阶段方法显著减少了候选图密度同时保持高覆盖率，在不同距离类型和分布上具有良好泛化能力，优于仅限于欧氏距离的近期神经稀疏化方法，在更大规模问题上价值更明显（单阶段启发式方法性能下降时）。

Conclusion: 两阶段图稀疏化方法有效解决了TSP求解器中候选图稀疏化的平衡问题，通过结合传统启发式和机器学习模型，实现了稀疏性和可靠性的优化，特别适用于大规模问题。

Abstract: High-performance TSP solvers like LKH search within a sparsified candidate graph rather than over all possible edges. Graph sparsification is non-trivial: keep too many edges and the solver wastes time; cut too many and it loses edges that belong to the optimal tour. The two leading heuristic methods, $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC, produce high-quality candidate graphs, but no single heuristic is both sparse and reliable across all instance sizes and distributions. Machine learning methods can potentially learn better sparsification models. However, existing approaches operate on the complete graph, which is expensive and mostly restricted to Euclidean distances. To address this issue, we propose a two-stage graph sparsification approach: Stage~1 takes the union of $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC to maximise recall; Stage~2 trains a single model to reduce density. We conducted experiments across four TSPLIB distance types, five spatial distributions, and problem sizes from 50 to 500. The two-stage approach substantially reduces candidate-graph density while retaining high coverage, generalises across distance types and distributions, outperforms recent neural sparsification methods that are restricted to Euclidean distances, and becomes increasingly valuable at larger scales where single-stage heuristics degrade.

</details>


### [114] [Causal-Transformer with Adaptive Mutation-Locking for Early Prediction of Acute Kidney Injury](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20259)
*Weizhi Nie,Haolin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CT-Former：结合连续时间建模与因果Transformer的急性肾损伤早期预测模型，解决数据不规则性和模型黑箱问题，提供临床可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在处理不规则采样数据时存在困难，且序列架构的"黑箱"特性限制了临床信任度，需要开发既准确又具有临床可解释性的AKI早期预测模型

Method: 提出CT-Former框架，集成连续时间建模和因果Transformer：1）使用连续时间状态演化机制自然跟踪患者时间轨迹，避免有偏人工插值；2）因果注意力模块生成有向结构因果矩阵，识别和追踪严重生理冲击的历史起源；3）采用解耦的两阶段训练协议独立优化因果融合过程

Result: 在MIMIC-IV队列（N=18,419）上的广泛实验表明，CT-Former显著优于最先进的基线方法，证实了其明确透明的架构为临床决策提供了准确且可信的工具

Conclusion: CT-Former通过整合连续时间建模和因果Transformer，成功解决了AKI预测中的数据不规则性和模型黑箱问题，提供了原生临床可解释性，为临床决策支持提供了准确且可信的工具

Abstract: Accurate early prediction of Acute Kidney Injury (AKI) is critical for timely clinical intervention. However, existing deep learning models struggle with irregularly sampled data and suffer from the opaque "black-box" nature of sequential architectures, strictly limiting clinical trust. To address these challenges, we propose CT-Former, integrating continuous-time modeling with a Causal-Transformer. To handle data irregularity without biased artificial imputation, our framework utilizes a continuous-time state evolution mechanism to naturally track patient temporal trajectories.
